*Lefteris Kokoris-Kogias,Alberto Sonnino*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合故障模型下2消息延迟提交的容错界限，证明了紧界限n≥5f+3c+1，并提出了一种更具弹性的提交路径，在4消息延迟下可容忍额外的存活但损坏故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典部分同步状态机复制（如PBFT）需要3个通信步骤且n≥3f+1。现有协议如MinBFT虽能实现2消息延迟决策，但需要n≥5f+1的更强假设。Hydrangea和Kudzu等协议研究混合拜占庭和崩溃故障，但作者希望在混合模型下专门研究2消息延迟提交的容错能力，填补这一研究空白。

Method: 采用理论证明方法，首先证明了2消息延迟提交的紧界限n≥5f+3c+1（其中f为拜占庭故障数，c为崩溃故障数）。在此基础上，设计了一种更具弹性的提交路径，该路径在4消息延迟下可以容忍额外的f_abc < n-3f-2c个存活但损坏故障。协议包含核心活性保证和同步恢复机制，当拜占庭节点超过阈值时切换到弹性路径以保证安全性。

Result: 证明了2消息延迟提交在混合故障模型下的紧界限n≥5f+3c+1。提出的弹性提交路径在4消息延迟下可容忍额外的存活但损坏故障。具体实例：对于f=16、c=6、n=99的配置，协议可容忍22%副本故障（活性）、16%副本拜占庭（1-RTT安全性）和54%副本拜占庭（2-RTT安全性），显著提升了系统的容错能力。

Conclusion: 本研究为混合故障模型下的快速提交协议提供了理论界限和实践指导。通过区分不同故障类型和不同延迟需求，实现了更灵活的容错权衡，为设计高性能且安全的分布式系统提供了新思路。协议在乐观情况下保持快速提交，在极端情况下仍能保证安全性，具有良好的实用性。

Abstract: Classical partially synchronous state machine replication, as in PBFT, tolerates f Byzantine replicas among n at least 3f+1 using three communication steps per request. Recent protocols such as Minimmit achieve two-message-delay decisions under stronger size assumptions, notably n at least 5f+1 when any silent replica must be counted as a potential equivocator. Hydrangea and Kudzu treat mixed Byzantine and crash faults, focusing on providing a fast-path under optimistic conditions while maintaining a fall-back commitment path similar to PBFT. In this paper, we also consider a mixed model, but focus on studying the fault tolerance of the 2-message-delay commit. For this, we prove a tight bound of n at least 5f+3c+1. Extending this result, we also show that there exists a more resilient commit path that allows an extra f_abc < n-3f-2c alive-but-corrupt faults at 4-message-delays. Core liveness is claimed in executions with at most f equivocators; if this regime is violated (e.g., AbC-induced forks), the protocol enters synchronous recovery, where only the resilient-path safety guarantee is preserved. As a result, for f=16, c=6, and n=99, we obtain a commit path that tolerates 22% of replicas failing for liveness, 16% equivocating for 1-RTT safety, and 54% equivocating for 2-RTT safety.

</details>


### [33] [Performance evaluation of scheduling tasks in many-core systems utilizing processes and threads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04821)
*Mejgan Dedaj,Argyro Gailla,Theofanis Ioannou,Stamatia Kastrinaki,Hermione Kimpouropoulou,Dimitrios Kontodimos,Kleopatra Kontogianni,Sotirios Kontogiannis,Michail Panagiotidis Kannas,Anastasia Papouda,Anna Maria Sidiropoulou,George Tavridis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在24核平台上评估了众核共享内存系统中进程式和线程式调度器的可扩展性，发现线程调度器（特别是动态和引导式调度）性能最优，而基于管道的进程调度器在可扩展性方面表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着众核处理器的发展，需要评估不同调度策略在共享内存系统中的可扩展性表现，特别是在处理大规模三维张量的内存密集型行式快速排序工作负载时，为选择合适的调度方案提供依据。

Method: 对进程式调度器评估了有界繁殖式、有界集合式以及三种基于管道的生产者-消费者调度器（一对一、一对多、多对多）；对线程式调度器评估了静态、动态、引导式、分块式、分块窃取、自适应分块和AIMD自适应调度策略，其中AIMD调度器借鉴TCP拥塞控制思想，利用CPU利用率的EWMA调节竞争窗口。

Result: 线程调度器整体性能最优，动态和引导式调度实际效果最佳；进程调度器中基于管道的设计可扩展性最强，一对一管道适合较小工作负载，多对多管道适合较大工作负载。

Conclusion: 轻量级线程调度最适合共享内存行排序任务，而AIMD/自适应调度和基于管道的进程调度在感知竞争执行、显式进程间协调以及分布式风格异构工作负载管理方面仍有重要价值。

Abstract: This study assesses the scalability of process-based and thread-based schedulers for many-core shared-memory systems using a memory-intensive row-wise quick-sort workload on large three-dimensional tensors. The process-based evaluation considers bounded prolific, bounded collective, and three pipe-based producer-consumer schedulers: one-to-one, one-to-many, and many-to-many. These pipe schedulers dynamically stream task identifiers to worker processes, exchanging increased inter-process communication overhead for enhanced runtime load balancing and flexible chunk-based task dispatching. The thread-based evaluation examines static, dynamic, guided, chunk-based, chunk-stealing, adaptive chunk, and AIMD adaptive scheduling strategies. The AIMD scheduler employs an additive-increase multiplicative-decrease policy inspired by TCP congestion control, utilizing an exponentially weighted moving average (EWMA) of CPU utilization to regulate a contention window that limits the number of concurrently active chunks. The adaptive chunk scheduler further modifies chunk size based on observed per-thread execution speed. Experimental results on a 24-core x86-64 platform indicate that thread schedulers deliver the highest overall performance, with dynamic and guided scheduling yielding the most favorable practical outcomes. Among process schedulers, pipe-based designs demonstrate the strongest scalability, with one-to-one pipes excelling for smaller workloads and many-to-many pipes preferred for larger workloads. In summary, lightweight thread scheduling is optimal for shared-memory row sorting, while AIMD/adaptive scheduling and pipe-based process scheduling remain valuable for contention-aware execution, explicit inter-process coordination, and distributed-style heterogeneous workload management.

</details>


### [34] [No Distributed Quantum Advantage for 3-Coloring Rooted Trees and 2-Coloring Even Cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04852)
*Pierre Fraigniaud,Frédéric Magniez,Isabella Ziccardi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明了量子资源在分布式计算中无法加速有根树的3-着色问题（需要Ω(log* n)轮）和偶长度环的2-着色问题（需要n/2-1轮），表明量子计算在这两个经典问题上相比经典算法没有任何优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究（Coiteux-Roy等，STOC 2024）已证明量子资源对无根树的3-着色问题无帮助，但有根树的情况尚待解决。同时，偶长度环的2-着色问题的量子下界尚不紧确。本文旨在填补这些空白，探索量子资源在分布式图着色问题中的实际价值。

Method: 针对有根树3-着色问题，采用与Linial经典论证相似的"颜色提升"（color lifting）技术，证明在Δ-叉树上以至少1-1/Δ的成功概率进行3-着色需要Ω(log* Δ)轮。针对偶长度环的2-着色问题，证明即使允许预共享纠缠态，在量子-LOCAL模型中也需要n/2-1轮。

Result: 主要获得两个下界结果：(1) 在无预共享纠缠的量子-LOCAL模型中，以至少1-O(1/log n)的概率正确3-着色n节点有根树需要Ω(log* n)轮，与Cole-Vishkin经典算法匹配；(2) 即使允许预共享纠缠态，以1-O(1/n)概率2-着色偶长度n节点环需要n/2-1轮，将之前已知的最优下界提高了两倍。

Conclusion: 量子资源在这两个经典分布式图着色问题上相比经典确定性算法无法提供任何优势，既不能减少轮数复杂度，甚至连一轮都无法节省。这进一步限制了量子优势在分布式计算中的应用范围，强化了量子计算在符合因果律的分布式场景中的局限性认知。

Abstract: Significant effort has been devoted over the past decade to understanding whether quantum resources can provide advantages in distributed computing, and in particular whether they can help overcome locality constraints in networks, typically in Linial's LOCAL model. Recently, Coiteux-Roy~et~al.~(STOC 2024) showed that quantum resources do not help for 3-coloring \textit{unrooted} trees: in particular, their lower bound holds in the stronger \textit{non-signaling} model, which formalizes the principle of physical causality in distributed computing. The case of \textit{rooted} trees, however, was left open by their work.
  For rooted trees, the deterministic Cole-Vishkin algorithm 3-colors $n$-node trees in $O(\log^\star n)$ rounds, matching Linial's classical $Ω(\log^\star n)$ lower bound (FOCS 1987). In this paper, we show that any algorithm in quantum-LOCAL (without pre-shared entanglement) that properly 3-colors $n$-node rooted trees with probability at least ${1-O(1/\log n)}$ must perform $Ω(\log^\star n)$ rounds. That is, quantum resources provide no advantage for 3-coloring rooted trees. To get this result, we show a lower bound of $Ω(\log^\star Δ)$ for 3-coloring any $Δ$-ary tree with success probability at least $1-1/Δ$. The proof uses a \textit{color lifting} technique that bears similarity to Linial's original argument.
  We also show, as a separate result, that 2-coloring even-length $n$-node cycles with probability $1-O(1/n)$ requires $n/2-1$ rounds in the quantum-LOCAL model, even with pre-shared entangled states. This improves the previously known $\lceil (n-2)/4 \rceil$ lower bound of Gavoille, Kosowski, and Markiewicz (DISC 2009) by a factor of two, and shows that quantum algorithms cannot save even a single round over classical deterministic algorithms for 2-coloring even-length cycles.

</details>


### [35] [Symmetry all the way down](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04887)
*Ignacio Amores-Sesar,Christian Cachin,Simon Holmgaard Kamp,Juan Villacis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 非对称信任虽然能容忍更多故障场景，但对于需要深度≥2的任务（如可靠广播、共识），其可解性与传统对称系统相同；额外容忍的故障场景仅适用于深度≤1的简单任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非对称信任系统通过允许每个进程指定自己的故障假设，扩展了经典对称仲裁系统，能容忍更多故障场景。然而，在这些额外容忍的故障场景中，是否能够以及能够解决哪些分布式任务，此前尚不清楚。本文旨在明确非对称信任在任务可解性方面的精确能力边界。

Method: 利用非对称信任的深度层次理论（Amores-Sesar et al., OPODIS'25）进行分析，该理论刻画了进程解决任务时对他人的依赖程度。提出一个编译器，将非对称仲裁系统转换为对称仲裁系统，并证明对于深度≥2的任务，任何允许解决该任务的非对称系统的故障场景，都存在对应的对称系统也能容忍。

Result: 证明了对于需要深度≥2的任务（如可靠广播、共识），非对称信任不会增加任务的可解性。非对称信任额外容忍的故障场景仅对应于只能解决深度≤1的简单任务（如一致广播）的情况。结果在深度层次上是紧的：不存在能产生对深度≤1场景也有效的对称仲裁系统的编译器。

Conclusion: 非对称信任精确能力已明确：虽然严格扩大了可容忍的故障模式集合，但对于需要深度≥2的任务，它并不提供额外的解决能力。这为分布式系统设计提供了理论指导，表明非对称信任的优势主要体现在简单任务的容错能力上。

Abstract: Asymmetric trust generalizes classical symmetric quorum systems by allowing each process to specify its own failure assumptions. While this flexibility enables tolerance of strictly more failure scenarios, it is not known if, in these cases, it is actually possible to solve distributed tasks, and if so, which.
  We answer this question using the depth hierarchy for asymmetric trust (Amores-Sesar et al., OPODIS~'25), which characterizes how much a process must rely on others to solve a task. We prove that asymmetric trust does not increase the solvability of tasks requiring depth two or more, such as reliable broadcast or consensus. Specifically, for any Byzantine asymmetric quorum system, every failure scenario that permits solving a task requiring depth at least two can also be tolerated by a suitably constructed Byzantine symmetric quorum system. We show this via a compiler that transforms asymmetric quorum systems into symmetric ones.
  The additional failure patterns tolerated exclusively by asymmetric trust correspond to scenarios in which only simpler tasks requiring depth one or less (such as consistent broadcast) can be solved. We further prove that this result is tight in the depth hierarchy, meaning that there exist no compilers that produce symmetric quorum systems that are valid also in failure scenarios where correct processes have depths one or less.
  Our results clarify the precise power of asymmetric trust. While it strictly enlarges the set of tolerable failure patterns, it does not provide additional strength for solving tasks requiring depth two or higher.

</details>


### [36] [TARE: Tail Aware Evaluation of HPC Job Runtime Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04935)
*Haili Xiao,Can Wu,Shasha Lu,Xiaoning Wang,Yining Zhao,Rong He*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出面向尾部（tail）的HPC作业运行时间预测评估方法，发现用户提交的预估时间（UserReq）在高资源消耗作业上表现优异。基于此设计的混合调度策略可降低平均等待时间达8%，增加回填作业数量50%-115%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在HPC调度中，运行时间预估影响预约质量、回填机会和队列延迟。然而在重尾工作负载下，传统基于平均值的评估方法会误导调度影响分析，因为少量作业主导了资源使用。需要新的评估方法来准确刻画预测质量与调度性能的关系。

Method: 提出一种关注尾部的实证评估方法，结合按资源使用量加权的GeoAccuracy指标、分位数分析和滚动分割分析。使用NREL Eagle和ALCF Mira/Intrepid的生产轨迹数据，对比XGBoost、Last2和用户提交的墙钟时间预估（UserReq）三种方法。基于离线评估发现，设计混合调度策略：大部分作业使用XGBoost预测，顶部十分位作业通过proxy_cost路由至UserReq。

Result: 在三个数据集上，尾部关注的评估改变了离线结论：MeanAccuracy使各方法差异较小，而GeoAccuracy揭示更清晰的分离，展现UserReq在上尾部的优势。在顶部十分位，UserReq在所有数据集上实现最高GeoAccuracy和最低低估率，该模式在滚动分割中保持稳定。在线回放实验中，混合策略使平均等待时间降低最多8%，回填作业增加50%-115%。

Conclusion: 面向尾部的离线评估能更好地刻画与调度相关的预测质量，为调度策略设计提供有效指导。用户提交的预估在高资源消耗作业上具有优势，结合机器学习方法的混合策略可显著提升调度性能。

Abstract: Runtime estimates affect reservation quality, backfilling opportunities, and queue delay in HPC schedulers. Under heavy tailed workloads, however, averaging over jobs can misrepresent scheduling impact because a small fraction of jobs dominates resource usage. This paper presents an empirical evaluation methodology for HPC job runtime prediction that focuses on the tail, combining GeoAccuracy weighted by resource usage with decile and split analyses. Using production traces from NREL Eagle and ALCF Mira/Intrepid, we compare XGBoost and Last2 against the user provided walltime estimate at submission (UserReq). Across all three datasets, evaluation focused on the tail changes the offline conclusion: MeanAccuracy keeps the methods relatively close, whereas GeoAccuracy reveals clearer separation and makes UserReq's strength in the upper tail visible. In the top decile, UserReq achieves the highest GeoAccuracy and lowest underestimation rate on all three datasets, and this pattern remains stable across rolling splits. We then translate this signal into a simple hybrid scheduling policy that keeps XGBoost for most jobs and routes the top decile by proxy_cost at submission to UserReq. Online replay on four production queues reduces mean wait time by up to 8% and increases backfilled jobs by 50% to 115%. These results show that offline evaluation focused on the tail better characterizes prediction quality relevant to scheduling and informs scheduling policy design.

</details>


### [37] [When Words Predict Workload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04951)
*Anubhab Banerjee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对边缘GPU上处理法律文本时易发生的显存溢出问题，提出了一种CPU端语言资源预测网关，通过文本结构向量预测和动态路由，将误路由率降至0.09左右，峰值显存保持在4.82GB，有效防止了硬件崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分布式LLM调度器依赖静态token计数或滚动延迟平均值，在处理法定约束文本（如EPO权利要求）时容易失效。此类文本的语言刚性使人工与机器创作难以区分，导致运行时需要动态多模型集成扩展，引发不可预测的KV缓存和权重分配峰值，使消费级边缘GPU显存饱和并造成严重的OOM崩溃。

Method: 提出CPU端语言资源预测（LRF）网关：提取16维文本结构向量，使用XGBoost预测器预测陷阱带归属，得到升级概率（P_esc）；基于实时延迟遥测计算动态闭式路由阈值τ_route(t)；在分配边缘GPU内存之前，将请求安全路由至本地Qwen2.5-7B边缘工作器或NVIDIA H100上的远程对比集成模型（Qwen2.5 7B + 32B）。

Result: 在6000次请求的现场试验中，LRF网关将操作误路由率（R_mis）降至0.087-0.095，比token计数基线（0.849）低一个数量级；峰值边缘显存安全保持在4.82GB（低于8GB上限），在WAN延迟27倍变化下保持稳定；预测器现场试验AUROC达0.84，动态τ_route(t)控制器比等效静态阈值减少8.2%的相对误路由。

Conclusion: LRF网关通过CPU端预测和动态路由决策，有效解决了边缘GPU处理法律文本时的显存溢出问题，显著降低了误路由率并保持硬件稳定，为资源受限环境下的LLM部署提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Standard distributed \ac{llm} schedulers rely on static token counts or rolling latency averages, making them susceptible to failures on statutorily constrained text. On \ac{epo} claims governed by Article 84 \ac{epc}, linguistic rigidity makes human and machine authorship statistically indistinguishable. Resolving this ambiguity mid-flight forces dynamic multi-model ensemble expansion, triggering unpredictable KV-cache and weight-allocation spikes that saturate consumer-grade edge GPU VRAM and cause severe \ac{oom} crashes. To prevent hardware collapse, we propose a CPU-side Linguistic Resource Forecasting (LRF) gateway. The gateway extracts a 16-dimensional text-structure vector and applies an XGBoost predictor to forecast trap-band membership. The resulting escalation probability ($\Pesc$) is evaluated against a dynamic, closed-form routing threshold ($\Tauroute(t)$) computed via real-time latency telemetry. Requests are safely routed to either a local Qwen2.5-7B edge worker or a remote contrastive ensemble (Qwen2.5 7B + 32B) on an NVIDIA H100 \emph{before} any edge GPU memory is allocated. In a 6,000-request live trial, the LRF gateway reduced the operational misroute fraction ($R_{\mathrm{mis}}$) to $0.087$--$0.095$, an order of magnitude below the token-count baseline ($0.849$). Peak edge VRAM remained safely bounded at $\SI{4.82}{\gibi\byte}$ (under the $\SI{8}{\gibi\byte}$ ceiling) across a $27\times$ variation in \ac{wan} delay. The predictor achieved a live-trial AUROC of $0.84$, and the dynamic $\Tauroute(t)$ controller yielded an $8.2\%$ relative reduction in misroutes compared to an equivalent static threshold.

</details>


### [38] [Communication-Aware Placement and Pruning for Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05116)
*Xiao Shi,Yingying Sun,Jiangsu Du,Zhiguang Chen,Yutong Lu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CAP是一个针对大规模MoE模型的通信感知专家放置与剪枝框架，通过协同激活驱动的专家放置、通信计算权衡调整和通信感知专家剪枝三个组件，在单节点和多节点实验中实现了1.23x-1.86x的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着MoE模型扩展到数百个专家，专家放置和剪枝决策显著影响通信量，进而影响跨GPU和跨节点分布式推理的性能。现有方法未能同时考虑计算、通信和精度，导致性能次优。

Method: 提出CAP框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)协同激活驱动的专家放置，将频繁共同激活的专家分组以减少跨设备和跨节点通信；(2)通信-计算权衡调整，生成具有不同计算负载和通信量的放置方案；(3)通信感知专家剪枝，选择性移除路由目标以在有限的精度损失下降低通信。框架根据不同硬件配置选择高效的操作策略。

Result: 在单节点和多节点实验中，CAP相比DeepSeek EPLB和vLLM中的顺序放置实现了1.23x-1.86x的吞吐量提升，在有损加速场景下在相同目标加速比下保持了更好的模型精度。

Conclusion: CAP通过综合考虑计算、通信和精度，有效解决了大规模MoE模型的分布式推理效率问题，为不同硬件配置提供了高效的专家放置与剪枝策略。

Abstract: As MoE models scale to hundreds of experts, placement and pruning decisions increasingly dictate communication volume, affecting the performance of distributed inference across GPUs and nodes. We propose CAP (Communication-Aware Assignment and Pruning), a framework that considers computation, communication and accuracy together for efficient MoE inference through expert placement and pruning. It consists of three components: (1) Co-activation driven expert placement, which groups frequently co-activated experts to reduce inter-device and inter-node communication; (2) Communicationcomputation trade-off adjustment, which generates placements with different computational load and communication volume; and (3) Communication-aware expert pruning, which selectively removes routing destinations to reduce communication with limited accuracy degradation. By combining these components, CAP selects an efficient operating strategy for different hardware configurations. Across our single-node and multi-node experiments, it achieves 1.23x - 1.86 x throughput improvement over DeepSeek EPLB and sequential placement in vLLM, and preserves better model accuracy at the same target speedup under lossy acceleration.

</details>

*Andreas Penzkofer,Franck Cassez*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨链原子交易协议（CAT），通过引入共享协调层实现了不同区块链之间的可组合原子执行，在保证强安全性和活性且不破坏链自治的前提下，解决了异步通信和拜占庭行为下的原子性保证问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨链互操作性面临的核心挑战是如何在存在异步通信和拜占庭行为的情况下提供原子性保证。现有方案往往需要在链自治与原子性之间做取舍，本研究旨在设计一种既能保持各链独立执行模型又能实现跨链协调原子执行的协议。

Method: 提出包含排序器、交易处理器、协调器和确认层的共享协调层架构；将交易执行分为接受集和延迟集以避免不必要的阻塞；引入超时机制和依赖深度界限以确保活性和缓解级联延迟；通过多轮协调确定CAT的最终执行结果。

Result: 形式化分析证明了协议的强安全性和活性保证，对独立交易实现最小阻塞，对依赖交易实现有界阻塞时间。实验评估表明：当跨链交易占流量适度比例时CAT成功率较高，并刻画了CAT成功率与依赖交易延迟之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该协议在保持链自治的同时实现了快速、安全、确定性的跨链原子执行，为可扩展的区块链互操作性解决方案奠定了基础。

Abstract: We propose a protocol for cross-chain atomic transactions (CATs), enabling composable atomic execution across different blockchains. The protocol addresses the key interoperability challenge of providing atomicity guarantees in the presence of asynchronous communication and Byzantine actors. It preserves chain autonomy by allowing each blockchain to maintain its own execution model while participating in coordinated cross-chain operations. The design introduces a shared coordination layer involving sequencers, transaction processors, a coordinator, and a confirmation layer which together ensure that either all parts of a CAT succeed or none do.
  To prevent unnecessary blocking, we separate transaction execution into accepted and postponed sets, with the coordination layer resolving the outcomes of CATs within a few rounds. We further introduce timeouts and dependency-depth bounds for liveness and mitigation of cascading delays. Our formal analysis establishes strong safety and liveness guarantees and demonstrates that the protocol achieves minimal blocking for independent transactions while ensuring bounded blocking time for dependent transactions. Experimental evaluation shows high CAT success when cross-chain transactions are a modest share of traffic, and characterizes the CAT-lifetime trade-off between success and dependent-transaction latency.
  This protocol enables fast, secure, and deterministic atomic cross-chain execution while preserving chain autonomy, providing a foundation for scalable blockchain interoperability solutions.

</details>

*Bruno Cascaes Alves,Míriam Blank Born,Ulisses Gilioli Francescatto Júnior,Felipe Moura Goulart,Letícia Brandão Caldas,Marilton Sanchotene de Aguiar*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将大语言模型（LLM）集成到多智能体城市交通仿真中的混合架构，作为决策层指导路径重规划，相比传统基于规则的方法展现出更强的适应性和情境感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通建模在动态环境下的决策表示面临挑战。现有多智能体系统广泛采用基于规则的方法，但其依赖固定启发式策略，限制了智能体的自适应行为能力。

Method: 提出一种混合架构，通过API将GAMA仿真平台与外部LLM模块连接。LLM不替代路由算法，而是作为决策层判断是否需要进行路径重规划。系统引入持久记忆机制，使历史交互能够影响未来决策，促进行为一致性。在多种道路阻塞场景和人口规模下对比基于规则和LLM辅助的方法。

Conclusion: LLM可作为补充性认知层，丰富城市交通仿真中的行为表示，在空间多智能体系统中具有建模复杂决策的潜力。

Abstract: Urban mobility modeling faces challenges in representing decision-making in dynamic environments. Although Multi-Agent Systems are widely used, rule-based approaches rely on fixed heuristics that limit adaptive behavior. This work investigates the integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) as decision-making components in multi-agent simulations. We propose a hybrid architecture that connects the GAMA platform to an external LLM-based module through an API, enabling agents to determine whether route replanning is necessary. Rather than replacing routing algorithms, the LLM serves as a decision layer that guides replanning behavior. The approach incorporates persistent memory, allowing past interactions to influence future decisions and promote behavioral consistency. We compare rule-based and LLM-assisted approaches across multiple road-blockage scenarios and population scales. Results indicate that LLM-enabled agents exhibit greater adaptability and contextual awareness, particularly in scenarios with higher route flexibility. Memory influences performance and behavioral consistency, with effects varying across configurations. Overall, LLMs serve as complementary cognitive layers that enrich behavioral representations in urban mobility simulations and hold potential for modeling complex decision-making in spatial multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [41] [SovereignNegotiation-Bench: Evaluating User-Owned Personal Agents In Delegated Bargaining Under Privacy, Consent, Evidence, And Institutional Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02814)
*Dylan Zongmin Liu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了SovereignNegotiation-Bench基准，用于评估个人代理谈判能力。研究发现：仅追求协议达成率会损害用户利益（隐私泄露、违规承诺等），而考虑主权因素的FullSovereign方法虽然协议率较低，但能更好地保护用户效用、隐私和自主权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谈判基准主要关注协议达成、盈余或战略能力，但用户拥有的代理可能达成了协议却损害用户利益（如隐私泄露、同意违规、无证据支持的主张、过度让步、升级失败或审计困难）。需要建立能够评估谈判中用户主权保护的基准。

Method: 引入SovereignNegotiation-Bench轨迹级多轮基准，将代理可见的观测状态与仅评估者可见的标签分离，在私人效用、披露约束、证据要求和制度不对称条件下评估个人代理谈判。评估维度包括：协议成功、用户效用、隐私、同意、证据基础、让步纪律、升级和可审计性。通过240个场景、4个模型族、14个基线、13,440条冻结提示实时轨迹、61,135行解析动作进行验证，并对300项进行盲审3标注者审计。

Result: 最强的协议最大化基线达到最高协议率但用户效用低、隐私/同意风险高；FullSovereign方法虽然不最大化协议，但通过保护效用、最小化泄露、基于证据主张、减少未授权承诺获得了最佳主权谈判评分。结果表明协议成功对用户拥有型谈判代理来说是不够的。

Conclusion: 仅追求协议达成率不足以评估用户拥有型谈判代理的性能。需要在谈判中同时考虑用户效用、隐私保护、同意合规、证据基础、让步纪律、升级能力和可审计性等多个维度的主权保护因素。

Abstract: Personal agents will increasingly negotiate on behalf of users: splitting costs with other personal agents, appealing platform decisions, escalating support disputes, requesting refunds, changing subscriptions, and negotiating deadlines or reimbursements. Existing negotiation benchmarks emphasize agreement, surplus, or strategic competence, but a user-owned agent can reach an agreement while harming the user through privacy leakage, consent violation, unsupported advocacy, over-concession, failed escalation, or poor auditability. We introduce SovereignNegotiation-Bench, a trace-level multi-turn benchmark for delegated personal-agent negotiation under private utilities, disclosure constraints, evidence requirements, and institutional asymmetry. The benchmark separates agent-visible observable state from evaluator-only labels and evaluates agreement success jointly with user utility, privacy, consent, evidence grounding, concession discipline, escalation, and auditability. We report an artifact-backed validation over 240 scenarios, 4 model families, 14 baselines, 13,440 frozen-prompt live trajectories, 61,135 parsed action rows, and a blinded 3-annotator audit over 300 items. The strongest agreement-maximizing baseline achieves the highest agreement rate but low user utility and high privacy/consent risk; FullSovereign does not maximize agreement, but obtains the best sovereign negotiation score by preserving utility, minimizing leakage, grounding claims, and reducing unauthorized commitments. The results show that agreement success is insufficient for user-owned negotiation agents.

</details>

Result: 1) 仿真器实现了稳定的实时人在回路性能，在保持严格实时执行和高视觉保真度的同时，可扩展至大规模异构海事无人机集群；2) 分层流场模型与海洋再分析数据的对比验证表明，相较于常用的随机高斯-马尔可夫流场模型，准确性有显著提升。

Conclusion: LOTUSim验证了其作为操作员在回路海事机器人研究仿真平台的适用性，填补了大规模异构海事机器人实时交互仿真和高保真水下环境建模的空白。

Abstract: Simulation is essential for maritime robotics, supporting operator training, mission rehearsal, and human-vehicle interaction in environments where real-world testing is costly or hazardous. Existing simulators focus primarily on autonomy systems and often lack human-in-the-loop interaction and realistic environmental physics. This paper introduces LOTUSim, an open-source, real-time maritime simulator supporting multi-user interaction across aerial, surface, and underwater robotic systems for coordinated naval-style operations. The first contribution of this work is enabling real-time interactive performance for users while ensuring scalability to large fleets operating within a shared interactive simulation environment. Validation demonstrates robust human-in-the-loop performance, maintaining strict real-time execution and high visual fidelity while scaling to large heterogeneous maritime drone swarms. The second contribution is a computationally efficient, Ekman-inspired layered, underwater current model that captures wind-driven, depth-dependent flow dynamics with sufficient physical fidelity for large-scale simulations. Validation against ocean reanalysis data demonstrates substantially improved accuracy compared to commonly used stochastic Gauss-Markov current models. These results confirm LOTUSim's suitability as a simulation platform for operatorin-the-loop maritime robotics research.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 竞赛保留了野火扑救、网络安全和共享出行三个领域，新增了具有帧开放性的野火赛道（智能体设备状态如灭火剂容量和灭火范围随时间变化）。评估指标扩展为总任务完成数、平均任务完成时间和已完成任务的平均价值。唯一的参赛作品DLC采用规划和重规划方法解决乘客出现时的跨智能体路径规划问题。

Conclusion: 2026年竞赛成功延续了评估框架并引入了新的开放性挑战，尽管参赛队伍提交率较低，但展示了规划与重规划方法在动态共享出行场景中的有效性。

Abstract: We describe the 2026 Methods for Open Agent Systems Evaluation Initiative (MOASEI) Competition, a benchmark event for evaluating multi-agent decision-making under open-system conditions. Building on the inaugural 2025 competition, the 2026 edition retained wildfire fighting, cybersecurity, and ride-sharing domains while adding a bonus wildfire track with frame openness, in which agent equipment states such as suppressant capacities and firefighting range vary over time. The competition also expanded its reporting metrics to emphasize total task completions, mean task-completion time, and mean value of completed tasks. Participation in 2026 was limited: eight teams registered, but only one team submitted a final entry, and that entry targeted the ride-sharing track. The submitted DLC approach used planning and replanning to solve routing problems across agents as passengers appeared. This report summarizes the 2026 competition design, highlights differences from the previous year, and reports ride-sharing evaluation results against baseline policies. DLC is recognized as the 2026 ride-sharing track winner among submitted teams.

</details>


### [44] [MUTE: Return-Preserving Communication Unlearning for Efficient Multi-Agent Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03473)
*Rui Zuo,Qinwei Huang,Mingyang Li,Zhenhang Zhang,Simon Khan,Qinru Qiu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出MUTE框架，将多智能体强化学习中的通信稀疏化问题转化为价值引导的机器遗忘问题，通过反事实消息价值估计和双重目标机制，在保持性能的同时实现了80%-90%的带宽压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体强化学习方法在部分可观测环境下依赖智能体间通信进行协调，但现实世界的带宽约束要求稀疏通信。先前方法主要通过优化信息论代理目标来解决这一权衡，但这些统计代理与真实目标存在根本性错位——消息可能具有高信息量却与任务的联合回报无关。

Method: 提出MUTE（Message Unlearning for Targeted Efficiency）框架，将通信减少视为价值引导的机器遗忘问题：1）使用基于注意力的估计器严格量化反事实消息价值；2）通过双重目标机制系统性地从无通信约束策略中遗忘低价值消息的传输；3）在强制通信稀疏性的同时保持原始联合策略的回报。

Result: 在多个复杂多智能体环境中进行实验评估，MUTE实现了80%至90%的带宽压缩，同时保持了与最先进基线方法相当的性能表现。理论推导证明了稀疏化引起的性能差距存在上界，保证可控的回报退化。

Conclusion: MUTE成功解决了通信效率与任务性能之间的权衡问题，通过价值引导的遗忘机制而非信息论代理来优化通信稀疏性，为部分可观测协作任务中的带宽受限多智能体系统提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Inter-agent communication is critical for coordinating Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) agents under partial observability to perform effectively in cooperative games; however, real-world bandwidth constraints demand sparse interactions. Prior approaches primarily address this trade-off by optimizing information-theoretic surrogates. We argue that these statistical proxies are fundamentally misaligned with the true objective: a message can be highly informative yet irrelevant to the joint return of the task. In this work, we propose Message Unlearning for Targeted Efficiency (MUTE), a framework that views communication reduction as a value-guided machine unlearning problem. MUTE rigorously quantifies the Counterfactual Message Value using an attention-based estimator, and systematically unlearns the transmission of low-value messages from a policy trained without any communication constraints. This is achieved through a dual-objective mechanism that enforces communication sparsity while preserving the return of the original joint policy. We derive a theoretical upper bound on the performance gap induced by this sparsification, guaranteeing controlled return degradation. We also empirically evaluate MUTE on various complex multi-agent environments, achieving 80% to 90% bandwidth reduction while maintaining performance comparable to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [45] [Dynamic Airspace Management for UAVs in Evolving Urban Environments: Collaborative Coordination and Human Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04825)
*Lin Sun,Yuhang Wang,Fan Meng Hong,Haopeng Chen,Yan Jiao,Yongming Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Pharos系统，一个位于分布式感知与集中式控制之间的多无人机空域管理系统，有效降低了人类恐惧感并提升了空域利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空经济作为新兴行业具有巨大发展潜力，但在复杂城市地形和人口密集环境中，无人机操作安全面临关键挑战。现有的无人机空域管理需要在避免碰撞和保障人员安全之间寻求平衡，特别是需要考虑人类恐惧心理的影响。

Method: 提出Pharos协作式多无人机空域管理系统，介于分布式本地感知范式和集中式细粒度控制范式之间。系统采用MAPPO算法实现，因其相比其他典型的多智能体强化学习算法（HAPPO和HATRPO）具有更快的收敛速度和更高的奖励值。开发了基于真实城市数据的3D仿真系统进行评估，并设计了空间熵作为系统评估指标来量化空间利用率。

Result: 在3D仿真系统中验证了Pharos的有效空域协调能力。与基准方法Ipopt相比，人类恐惧感降低了52.72%；在空间利用率方面，与基准方法Ipopt和A-star相比，空间熵指标分别提升了70.82%和2.03%。

Conclusion: Pharos系统成功实现了多无人机在共享空域中的安全并行执行，创新性地考虑了人类恐惧因素的影响，在保障安全的同时显著提升了空域利用效率，为低空经济中的无人机空域管理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The low-altitude economy is an emerging industry with significant development potential, in which the safety of unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) operations is a critical challenge. Particularly within complex urban topographies and human-populated environments, UAV airspace management must prioritize collision avoidance and human safety. We propose Pharos, a collaborative multi-UAV airspace management system. Pharos lies between the distributed local perception paradigm and the centralized fine-grained control paradigm. Pharos coordinates the safe parallel execution of UAVs in shared airspace while innovatively accounting for the impact of human fear. Pharos is implemented using the MAPPO algorithm due to its faster convergence and higher rewards than other typical MARL algorithms (HAPPO and HATRPO). To evaluate Pharos, we developed a 3D simulation system using real urban data. Visualization results demonstrate its effective airspace coordination capability. Regarding performance verification, Pharos reduced human fear by 52.72% compared to the benchmark Ipopt. Moreover, we designed spatial entropy as a system evaluation metric to quantify space utilization, which improved performance by 70.82% and 2.03% compared to the benchmarks Ipopt and A-star, respectively. The source code is available at an anonymized repository: https://github.com/pharos-anonymized/source-code.git.

</details>


### [46] [PiSAs: Benchmarking Contextual Integrity in Multi-User Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05318)
*Shubham Gupta,Nazanin Mohammadi Sepahvand,Abhinav Kumar,Cem Subakan,Spandana Gella,Pierre-André Noël,Perouz Taslakian,Eugene Bagdasarian,Valentina Zantedeschi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了PiSAs基准，用于评估共享智能体系统中的跨用户数据泄露风险，发现现有模型在保护隐私方面存在严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体从单用户助手演变为共享的组织基础设施，出现了新的隐私风险：不当信息不仅可能通过输出暴露给外部接收者，还可能通过智能体间消息、共享内存和智能体在用户内部泄露。现有的基于情境完整性(CI)的隐私基准无法捕捉这些数据溢出风险，因为它们主要关注单用户设置或独立拥有智能体之间的交互。

Method: 引入PiSAs（Privacy in Shared Agentic systems）基准，具有双重CI标注：信息是否适合该任务，以及哪些用户可以合法访问它。这使得能够直接测量智能体系统组件和接口（如输出、智能体间通信和内存）之间的跨用户溢出。PiSAs与系统无关，支持跨不同智能体拓扑和内存机制进行评估。

Result: 尽管系统设计改善了CI合规性，但结果受到LLM错误判断的瓶颈限制：即使最先进的模型也无法可靠地过滤不当内容或限制向授权用户传输。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明需要超越本工作所研究的隐私保护策略，以应对共享智能体系统中的隐私挑战。

Abstract: As LLM agents evolve from single-user assistants into shared organizational infrastructure, new privacy risks emerge: inappropriate information may not only be exposed through outputs for external recipients, but also internally across users through inter-agent messages, shared memory and agents. These data spillage risks are not captured by existing privacy benchmarks grounded in contextual integrity (CI) as they focus primarily on either single-user settings or interactions between independently owned agents. We introducePiSAs (Privacy in Shared Agentic systems), a benchmark for assessing unintentional leaks with dual CI annotations: whether an information is appropriate for the task, and which users may legitimately access it. This enables direct measurement of cross-user spillage across agentic system components and interfaces, such as outputs, inter-agent communication, and memory. PiSAsis system-agnostic and supports evaluation across different agent topologies and memory regimes. We find that, although system design improves CI compliance, results are bottlenecked by incorrect LLM judgment calls: even state-of-the-art models fail to reliably filter inappropriate content or restrict transmission to authorized users. Our findings underscore the need for privacy-preserving strategies, beyond those studied in this work.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [47] [Auditing the Audit: Five Failure Modes in Benchmark-Validity Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02586)
*Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan,Zexin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于扰动的AI审计方法存在脆弱性，提出了五类审计管道失效模式，通过自审计案例展示了现有安全基准评估的不可靠性，并设计了一个六点尽职调查门槛作为质量保证协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI治理框架要求提供评估证据，其中基于扰动的构造有效性审计是常见形式。然而，这些审计本身可能存在脆弱性：审计结论可能被读者无法从报告数字中看到的实现细节所操纵，导致审计结果不可靠。

Method: 提出了五类审计管道失效（F1-F5）的分类法，设计了一个统一的六点尽职调查门槛作为质量保证协议，并在安全基准测试和开放权重指令微调模型上进行了自审计案例研究。

Result: 在案例研究中，所有评估单元都落入非确认性类别，没有任何单元达到确认性标准。这表明现有的基于扰动的安全评估可能无法提供可靠的质量保证证据。

Conclusion: 所提出的六点尽职调查门槛应定位为质量保证级证据的保留与披露协议，作为经典构造有效性证据的补充（而非替代），而不是通往基准有效性裁决的途径。F1-F5分类法仅为说明性起点，并非审计失效的穷尽分类。

Abstract: Governance frameworks ask AI providers and auditors for documented evaluation evidence, and perturbation-based construct-validity audits are a common form of that evidence. We argue the audits are themselves fragile: their conclusions can be silently manufactured by implementation details that readers cannot see in the reported numbers. We name five classes of pipeline failure and demonstrate each in a self-audit over safety benchmarks and open-weight instruction-tuned models. Under a unified six-point due-diligence gate, every cell lands in a non-confirmatory bucket, and no cell reaches confirmatory. The evidence here is a single two-model, five-benchmark case study, and F1--F5 is an illustrative, deliberately non-exhaustive starting taxonomy -- not a comprehensive partition of audit failures. We position the gate as a withholding and disclosure protocol for assurance-grade evidence, supplementary to (not a replacement for) classical construct-validity evidence, and not as a route to benchmark-validity verdicts.

</details>


### [48] [Evaluating Time Series Foundation Models for Electricity Price Forecasting: Contamination Risk, Distributional Shifts, and Covariate Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02623)
*Zhenghua Pan,Ahmed Aziz Ezzat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个电力价格预测（EPF）的双数据集基准测试框架，评估时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在非平稳、协变量驱动环境中的表现，发现TSFMs具有竞争力但高度依赖协变量支持，与领域专用方法的简单集成展现出巨大潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）的零样本预测能力已在平稳环境中得到验证，但其在协变量驱动、非平稳环境中的泛化能力尚未被充分探索。电力价格预测（EPF）因其复杂的时间依赖性、分布偏移以及对结构和上下文信息的强依赖性，成为检验TSFMs泛化能力的理想测试平台。

Method: 提出了一个针对EPF的双数据集基准测试框架，旨在降低数据污染风险并实现公平评估。研究系统考察了EPF的关键方面，包括点预测与概率预测性能、尾部行为、价格尖峰，以及与领域专用方法的对比，并进一步探索了TSFMs与领域专用方法的集成策略。

Result: 研究发现TSFMs在EPF任务中表现出强大的竞争力，常优于通用基准模型；但其性能高度依赖于协变量支持，且未能持续超越为EPF定制的领域专用方法。值得注意的发现是，TSFMs与领域专用方法的简单集成效果显著，表明两种方法捕获了互补的预测信息。

Conclusion: TSFMs在复杂非平稳环境中具备良好的应用前景，但协变量支持至关重要。领域专用方法在特定任务中仍有优势。未来研究方向应聚焦于TSFMs与领域专用方法的集成，以充分利用两者的互补优势，从而实现更优的预测性能。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) have shown strong zero-shot forecasting performance, but their generalization in covariate-driven, non-stationary settings is underexplored. Electricity price forecasting (EPF) presents a challenging testbed due to complex temporal dependencies, distributional shifts, and strong reliance on structural and contextual information. We propose a two-dataset-benchmarking framework for EPF to mitigate contamination risk and enable fair evaluation of TSFMs. We examine key aspects of EPF including point and probabilistic forecasting performance, tail behavior, price spikes, and comparisons against domain-specific methods. We find that TSFMs are highly competitive and often outperform general-purpose baselines. Yet, their performance depends critically on covariate support, and they do not consistently surpass domain-specific methods tailored to EPF. Interestingly, simple ensembles of TSFMs and domain-specific methods appear to have significant potential, suggesting that the two approaches capture complementary predictive information.

</details>


### [49] [QuantFlow: A Federated Mamba-Based Post-Transformer Foundation Model for Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02632)
*Shah Nawaz Haider,Steve Austin,Arnab Barua,Sarowar Morshed Shawon,Hadaate Ullah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QuantFlow是一个结合双向Mamba状态空间模型、分位数回归和联邦学习的概率时序预测框架，在保护隐私的同时实现了优秀的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时序预测基础模型多依赖中心化数据和Transformer注意力机制，限制了其在长序列、高维度和隐私敏感信号处理上的应用。需要开发既能处理长期依赖又能保护数据隐私的新型预测框架。

Method: 提出QuantFlow框架，采用反转序列嵌入、双向Mamba状态空间解码器进行序列处理，结合分位数回归输出五个条件分位数。引入TSMixup数据增强技术，通过Dirichlet加权重插值扩展时间多样性。支持联邦学习部署，实现去中心化训练。

Result: 在ETTm1数据集上获得0.2834的均方误差，Weather数据集上达到0.2218。20客户端非独立同分布部署在三轮通信后仍保持有效准确率，无需中心化原始数据记录。实验涵盖加密货币、交通、电力、ETT、流感和天气等多个领域数据。

Conclusion: 选择性状态空间建模是构建可扩展、不确定性感知和隐私意识时序预测系统的有前景基础。但在不规则流行病学信号和长期泛化方面仍存在局限性。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting supports decisions in finance, en-ergy, transportation, public health, and industrial monitoring. Recent foundation models improve transfer across forecast-ing tasks, but many depend on centralized data and Trans-former attention, which restricts their use for long, high-di-mensional, and privacy-sensitive signals. This paper presents QuantFlow, a probabilistic forecasting framework that com-bines inverted sequence embedding, bidirectional Mamba state-space decoders, quantile regression, and federated learning. Each variable is embedded over the complete ob-servation window, processed in forward and reverse direc-tions, and projected to five conditional quantiles. TSMixup expands temporal diversity through Dirichlet-weighted inter-polation while preserving sequence structure. Experiments cover cryptocurrency, traffic, electricity, Electricity Trans-former Temperature, influenza, and weather data. QuantFlow obtains mean squared errors of 0.2834 on ETTm1 and 0.2218 on Weather, and a 20-client non-IID deployment retains use-ful accuracy after three communication rounds without cen-tralizing raw records. The results indicate that selective state-space modelling is a promising basis for scalable, uncer-tainty-aware, and privacy-conscious time-series prediction, while also revealing limitations on irregular epidemiological signals and long-horizon generalization.

</details>


### [50] [GRAFT: Grafted Reference Audio for Fine-grained Pronunciation in Zero-shot Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02633)
*Antonis Asonitis,Francesco Verdini,Aref Farhadipour,Vijeta Avijeet,Pierre-Edouard Honnet,Marzieh Razavi,Juan Pablo Zuluaga Gomez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRAFT是一种用于文本转语音的字级发音调节机制，通过短语音样本控制特定单词的发音，在五语言基准上将目标词音素错误率降低22-39%，在盲听测试中获人类评价者首选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本转语音系统虽然达到较高的可懂度和自然度，但继承了文本的歧义性，经常误读罕见专有名词、外来词和技术术语。即使基于音素调节的模型也无法提供直接的字级发音声学控制手段。

Method: GRAFT通过短语音样本控制目标词发音，使用模型自身的语音分词器编码样本，并将其绑定到提示词中该词的位置。训练数据构建过程中采用声音转换技术，将提示说话人与目标说话人解耦，使提示可来自任意声音而输出保持目标声音。

Result: 在英语盲听研究中，人类评价者以明显优势将GRAFT排在首位，认为其对难词的发音最接近参考录音。在五语言客观基准上，GRAFT相比相同纯文本骨干将目标词音素错误率降低22-39%，在目标词发音方面优于竞争性开源零样本系统（包括音素和文本调节的），同时保持说话人相似度和自然度。

Conclusion: GRAFT成功实现了字级发音调节，显著改善了罕见词、专有名词等的发音准确性，在保持语音自然度和说话人相似度的同时，大幅降低了发音错误率，为文本转语音系统提供了有效的发音控制机制。

Abstract: We present GRAFT, a per-word pronunciation conditioning mechanism for text-to-speech neural codec language modeling. Existing systems reach high intelligibility and naturalness but inherit the ambiguity of text and mispronounce rare proper nouns, loanwords and technical terms. Even phoneme-conditioned models offer no direct acoustic handle for per-word pronunciation. GRAFT controls the pronunciation of a chosen word from a short spoken sample of it, encoded with the model's own speech tokenizer and bound to the word's position in the prompt. Voice conversion during training-data construction disentangles the hint speaker from the target speaker, so the hint may come from any voice while the output stays in the target voice. In a blind English listening study, human raters rank GRAFT first by a clear margin, judging its rendering of the difficult word closest to a reference recording of that word. On a five-language objective benchmark, GRAFT reduces target-word phoneme error rate by 22-39% over the identical text-only backbone and outperforms competitive open-source zero-shot systems, both phoneme- and text-conditioned, on target-word pronunciation, while preserving speaker similarity and naturalness.

</details>


### [51] [Federated Learning for Object Detection: Enabling Collaborative Drone Learning Without Centralizing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02636)
*Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Enrique Zuazua,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将联邦学习应用于无人机目标检测，在保持数据本地化和隐私的同时，实现了接近集中式训练的性能，显著优于单机训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键的无人机和边缘视觉系统中，目标检测需要大规模、持续更新的数据集。然而，传统方法需要集中化存储航拍图像，这带来了隐私、法规、存储和带宽方面的挑战，特别是在分布式无人机部署场景中，数据传输既不现实也不可取。

Method: 基于Sherpa.ai联邦学习平台实现了联邦目标检测流程，在KIIT-MiTA数据集上进行实验。使用IoU阈值为0.50和0.50-0.95的平均精度均值（mAP）作为评价指标，将联邦学习方法与单无人机训练和集中式训练基准进行对比。采用了包括YOLOv6 nano在内的轻量级模型。

Result: 联邦学习方法在性能上接近集中式训练，同时大幅优于单无人机训练。最佳的轻量级模型（YOLOv6 nano）在mAP@0.50和mAP@0.50:0.95上分别实现了52.89%和67.80%的相对提升，该模型适合部署在资源受限的边缘基础设施上。

Conclusion: 联邦学习能够在不集中数据的情况下，实现分布式无人机机群的可扩展、高性能且隐私保护的目标检测，为安全敏感的边缘视觉应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Object detection is a fundamental capability for AI-driven perception in safety-critical drone and edge-vision systems, including disaster response, operational security environments, infrastructure monitoring and defense applications. Robust model performance in such environments depends on large, continuously updated datasets. However, training high-performing detectors typically requires centralizing aerial imagery, which raises privacy, regulatory, storage, and bandwidth challenges. This is especially problematic in distributed drone deployments, where visual data is generated onboard and is often impractical or undesirable to transfer to a centralized infrastructure.
  In this work, we apply Federated Learning (FL) for object detection, enabling drones to improve a shared model while keeping image data local and private. We implement a federated object detection pipeline using the Sherpa.ai FL platform on the KIIT-MiTA dataset, and compare it with Single-drone and Centralized baselines using mean Average Precision (mAP) at IoU thresholds of 0.50 and 0.50-0.95. In our experiments, the proposed FL approach remains close to Centralized training while dramatically improving over Single-drone training, with the best lightweight model (YOLO26 nano), suitable for deployment even on very limited edge infrastructure, achieving relative gains of 52.89% and 67.80% in mAP@0.50 and mAP@0.50:0.95, respectively. These results show that FL enables scalable, high-performing, and privacy-preserving object detection across distributed drone fleets without data centralization.

</details>


### [52] [Post-Generation Curation of Synthetic Images via Homogeneous-Heterogeneous Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02637)
*Disheng Liu,Tuo Liang,Chaoda Song,Yu Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成器无关的后处理选择方法，通过识别并纠正生成器对“典型模式”的结构性偏好偏差，从固定的合成图像池中选择信息量更大的子集，从而在减少最多40%样本量的情况下匹敌真实数据性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有利用合成数据的方法通常需要训练/微调生成器或使用复杂的后验适配技术，使其依赖于特定生成器且需要专业知识。作者提出了一个互补性问题：在固定的合成图像池中，能否仅通过选择信息量大的子集来提升下游任务的效用？

Method: 1. 分析发现现代生成器存在结构性偏差：倾向于过度生成类内典型模式，而忽视类内多样性。
2. 将每个真实类别划分为同质（HO）子集（典型模式）和异质（HE）子集（非冗余变化）。
3. 设计保真度-多样性评分准则：奖励语义对齐，惩罚典型冗余。
4. 该方法无需重训练，与生成器类型无关。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，该方法持续优于现有最先进的数据选择基线；能够以减少最多40%的合成样本量达到与真实数据相当的性能；应用于更强的任务调优生成器时，在分类和分割任务上均获得增益。

Conclusion: 后生成选择并非更好生成器的替代品，而是提升合成数据效用的互补机制。该方法证明了仅通过选择策略即可有效改善合成数据的下游任务表现，且具有良好的通用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Recent generative models can produce high-quality synthetic images, offering scalable training training data for data-hungry models. Existing approaches to exploiting this potential typically involve 1) training or fine-tuning generators, or 2) using lightweight post-hoc adaptation like prompt engineering or inference-time guidance, making them generator-specific and expertise-intensive. We study a complementary question: given a fixed pool of generated images, can downstream utility be improved purely by selecting an informative subset? The answer is yes. We show that effective selection must counter a structural bias of modern generators: they tend to over-produce canonical modes of each class while underrepresenting intra-class variation. Building on this insight, we split each real class into a canonical Homogeneous (HO) subset and a non-redundant Heterogeneous (HE) subset, then score synthetic images by a fidelity-diversity criterion that rewards semantic alignment while penalizing canonical redundancy. The method is generator-agnostic and requires no retraining. Across multiple benchmarks, it consistently outperforms state-of-the-art data selection baselines and matches the real-data performance with up to 40% fewer synthetic samples. The same criterion remains effective when applied on top of stronger task-tuned generators, with gains on both classification and segmentation tasks. Post-generation selection is therefore not a substitute for better generators, but a complementary mechanism for improving the utility of synthetic data.

</details>


### [53] [A Granularity-Aware EEG Feature Framework for Psychopathology Dimension Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02670)
*Haofan Cheng,Jingjing Hu,Jingrong Pei,Shuaiqi Fu,Meilun Shen,Shuai Fang,Meng Wang,Dan Guo,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了粒度感知的EEG特征提取流程，利用HBN队列预测四种精神病理学维度，发现多尺度EEG特征包含微弱但可检测的信号，粒度感知特征选择可作为未来EEG表型研究的有效特征降维策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑电图(EEG)为研究维度化精神病理学的神经生理学相关性提供了非侵入性方法，但跨EEG范式和特征粒度的系统性证据仍然有限。现有研究缺乏对多尺度描述符的系统组织和评估。

Method: 开发了粒度感知的EEG特征提取流程，将多尺度描述符组织为全局、区域和通道三个层级。使用Healthy Brain Network (HBN)队列，基于四种EEG范式预测p因子、内化、外化和注意力问题四个精神病理学维度。采用树模型和粒度平衡特征选择方法。

Result: 树模型和粒度平衡特征选择在特定条件下相比传统方法显示出有希望的改进，但效应量仍然适中。选定的标记物可视化揭示了维度特异性的空间和频谱模式，与现有神经生理学知识广泛一致。在独立PEARL队列上的探索性交叉数据集验证表明所提选择原则在协议变化下仍技术可行。

Conclusion: 多尺度EEG特征包含与维度化精神病理学相关的微弱但可检测的信号。粒度感知选择可作为未来基于EEG表型研究的有用特征降维策略，为该领域的后续研究提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) offers a noninvasive approach for examining neurophysiological correlates of dimensional psychopathology, yet systematic evidence across EEG paradigms and feature granularities remains limited. Here, we develop a granularity-aware EEG feature pipeline that organizes multi-scale descriptors into global, regional, and channel levels. Using the Healthy Brain Network (HBN) cohort, we evaluate the prediction of four psychopathology dimensions: p-factor, internalizing, externalizing, and attention problems, across four EEG paradigms. Given the heterogeneity of pediatric psychopathology and the moderate reliability of questionnaire-derived scores, this setting represents a challenging feasibility test rather than a clinical screening scenario. Tree-based models and granularity-balanced feature selection showed promising improvements over conventional approaches in selected conditions, although effect sizes remained modest. Visualization of selected markers revealed dimension-specific spatial and spectral patterns that were broadly aligned with existing neurophysiological knowledge. An exploratory cross-dataset sanity check on the independent PEARL cohort suggested that the proposed selection principle remains technically feasible under protocol shifts, without claiming cross-dataset generalizability. Overall, multi-scale EEG features contain weak but detectable signals related to dimensional psychopathology, and granularity-aware selection may serve as a useful feature-reduction strategy for future EEG-based phenotyping studies.

</details>


### [54] [LiNO: Lifting based multiresolution neural operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02715)
*Himanshu Pandey,Subham Patel,Ratikanta Behera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Lifting Neural Operator (LiNO)，一种基于第二代小波提升方案的多分辨率神经算子，通过参数化提升变换直接从数据中学习多分辨率分解，实现了信息保持的多尺度算子学习，在多个物理基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经算子在同时捕获全局动态和精细尺度结构方面存在困难，缺乏有效的层次化多尺度分解框架来表示多尺度特征。需要设计一种能够自适应地表示多尺度特征的有效算子。

Method: 开发了Lifting Neural Operator (LiNO)，基于第二代小波提升方案构建多分辨率算子。通过参数化提升变换直接从数据中学习多分辨率分解，该变换对底层解函数具有自适应性且构造上完全可逆。在提升的多分辨率空间中，算子分别演化粗尺度和方向细节系数，实现对底层物理的尺度感知建模。

Result: 在多个基准测试上评估LiNO，包括Darcy流、Poisson方程、Allen-Cahn方程、可压缩Navier-Stokes方程和Gray-Scott反应扩散系统，涵盖了多尺度现象、传输主导动力学和混沌系统等广泛物理行为。LiNO与最先进的神经算子相比展现出强大的性能。

Conclusion: 自适应多分辨率算子为科学机器学习提供了一个有前景的方向，LiNO的成功表明基于提升方案的多尺度分解框架能够有效解决现有神经算子在多尺度特征表示方面的局限性。

Abstract: Recently, neural operators have shown promising outcomes for learning solution operators of differential equations directly from data. This framework learns a functional mapping from the parameter field to the solution field, enabling the prediction of an entire class of solutions rather than a specific instance. However, existing operators often struggle to capture both global dynamics and fine-scale structure simultaneously. To design an effective operator capable of representing multiscale features, a hierarchical multiscale decomposition framework is required. In this study, we develop the Lifting Neural Operator (LiNO), a multiresolution operator built on the second-generation wavelet lifting scheme. LiNO learns a multiresolution decomposition directly from data by parameterizing the lifting transform. This lifting transformation is adaptive to the underlying solution function and exactly invertible by construction, enabling information-preserving multiscale operator learning. In the lifted multiresolution space, the operator evolves coarse and directional detail coefficients separately, resulting in scale-aware modeling of the underlying physics. We evaluate LiNO on several benchmarks, including Darcy flow, the Poisson equation, the Allen-Cahn equation, the compressible Navier-Stokes equation, and the Gray-Scott reaction-diffusion system. Together, these benchmarks cover a wide range of physical behaviors, including multiscale phenomena, transport-dominated dynamics, and chaotic systems. LiNO demonstrates strong performance on these challenging benchmarks compared with state-of-the-art neural operators. These results suggest that adaptive multiresolution operators provide a promising direction for scientific machine learning.

</details>


### [55] [Weighted Conformal Prediction for Lab-to-Track Thermal Transfer in EV Motorsport Powertrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02722)
*Varshith Roy Kotla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高性能电动汽车动力系统热预测中的实验室到实测迁移问题，发现标准EnbPI方法在协变量偏移下覆盖率从95%降至70%，通过密度比加权改进后仅恢复至72%，证明了保形域适应在该问题上的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能电动汽车动力系统的热波动预测面临两个关键挑战：一是内部温度在实验室外难以观测，二是基于实验室驾驶循环校准的模型在面对真实世界负载时失效。这种实验室到赛道的迁移问题需要有效的 uncertainty quantification 方法。

Method: 1. 实现Ensemble Batch Prediction Intervals (EnbPI)方法，这是一种针对自相关时间序列的留一法自举集成保形预测方法；2. 使用真实的CALCE锂离子循环器数据(A123 SP20电池，FUDS剖面)进行校准；3. 引入加权EnbPI程序，将EnbPI的集成残差与密度比加权相结合，通过概率域分类器估计密度比；4. 在真实协变量偏移条件下(US06高速公路驾驶计划，45°C)评估性能；5. 应用到2023年F1遥测数据进行无监督分布外诊断。

Result: 未加权的EnbPI边界在分布内达到标称95%覆盖率(实测95.00%)，但在真实协变量偏移下经验覆盖率降至70.13%。加权EnbPI方法将覆盖率恢复至72.42%，仅是适度的改进而非完全修复。在F1遥测数据上，Monza和Silverstone的无监督标记率分别为65.6%和58.0%，远高于5%的分布内基准率，且标记与刹车/DRS区域之间的关联不一致。

Conclusion: 保形域适应是解决实验室到实测迁移问题的有前景工具，但目前仅部分解决该问题。论文诚实地报告了方法的局限性，详细说明了其失效的具体情况，强调需要进一步研究来完全解决热预测中的域适应挑战。

Abstract: Predicting thermal volatility in high-performance EV powertrains is difficult as internal temperatures are rarely observable outside the lab, and models calibrated on lab drive cycles fail when deployed against real-world loads. We study this lab-to-track transfer problem using conformal prediction, offering distribution-free uncertainty bounds. We implement Ensemble Batch Prediction Intervals (EnbPI; Xu & Xie, 2021), a leave-one-out bootstrap-ensemble conformal method for autocorrelated time series, and calibrate it on real CALCE lithium-ion cycler data (A123 SP20 cells, FUDS profile). We evaluate it under a genuine, measured covariate shift: a second real CALCE test condition (US06 Highway Driving Schedule at 45°C). The unweighted EnbPI bound, achieving its nominal 95% coverage in-distribution (measured: 95.00%), degrades to 70.13% empirical coverage under this real shift. We introduce a weighted EnbPI procedure combining EnbPI's ensemble residuals with density-ratio weighting (Tibshirani et al., 2019), estimating the density ratio via a probabilistic domain classifier. This recovers coverage to 72.42%, a modest, honestly-reported improvement, not a complete fix. We additionally apply the calibrated model to real 2023 Formula 1 telemetry (Monza and Silverstone, driver VER) as an unsupervised out-of-distribution diagnostic. Because no internal thermal channel exists in public trackside telemetry, we report only unsupervised flag rates (65.6% at Monza, 58.0% at Silverstone, well above the 5% in-distribution base rate) and note inconsistent associations between flags and braking/DRS zones. We conclude that conformal domain adaptation is a promising but only partially solved tool for this problem, detailing exactly where it falls short.

</details>


### [56] [Out-of-Distribution Generalization of Risk Aversion in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02755)
*Kristina Zhang,Junior Chinomso Okoroafor,Benjamin Maltbie,Andrew Lin,Abhitej Bokka,Elliott Thornley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RiskAverseOOD基准，测试AI的风险规避能力能否从低风险场景泛化到极高风险场景。实验表明，在低风险训练得到的风险规避可以部分泛化至98个数量级外的天文级高风险场景，但仍未达到可靠安全阀门的稳定性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练AI在资源使用上具有风险规避性可作为应对AI错位的故障安全机制。风险规避的错位AI更倾向于选择低风险低回报的合作策略，而非高风险高回报的反叛策略。然而关键问题在于：我们只能在低风险赌注上训练风险规避，其是否能泛化到天文级高风险赌注尚不明确。

Method: 提出RiskAverseOOD基准测试风险规避的分布外泛化能力；使用SFT+tie training、DPO、activation steering等方法训练Qwen3-8B在低风险场景下选择风险规避策略；设计实验测试模型在极高风险场景下的行为；微调奖励模型评估风险规避推理；在不同规模(Qwen3-1.7B、Qwen3-14B)和模型家族(Gemma-3-12B-IT、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct)上复现实验。

Result: 模型学习到的风险规避能力可以跨98个数量级部分泛化。安全"合作"选项选择率从基线2%提升至：SFT+tie training约70%、DPO约52%、activation steering约39%。微调奖励模型在风险规避推理评分上达到99.6%的成对准确率。效果在不同模型规模和模型家族上得到复现。

Conclusion: 低风险场景学习到的风险规避可以分布外泛化至极高风险场景，但泛化程度尚不足以作为可靠的故障安全机制。实现足够一致的风险规避泛化仍是一个开放问题，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Training AIs to be risk-averse in resources could offer a failsafe in the event that AIs turn out misaligned. Misaligned but risk-averse AIs would tend to prefer low-risk, low-reward strategies like cooperation over high-risk, high-reward strategies like rebellion, limiting the downsides of any misalignment. But we can only feasibly train AIs to be risk-averse on low-stakes gambles, and we will only be safe if their risk aversion generalizes to astronomically-high-stakes gambles. Will it? To shed light on this question, we introduce RiskAverseOOD: a benchmark for measuring how well risk aversion generalizes out of distribution. We then offer some initial results. Using a variety of methods to make Qwen3-8B choose risk-aversely when the stakes are low, we find that we can induce substantial risk aversion when the stakes are astronomically high. Our models' learned risk aversion generalizes at least partially across 98 orders of magnitude. From a baseline 2% rate of choosing a safe `Cooperate' option, we see rates around 70% (SFT and tie training), 52% (DPO), and 39% (activation steering). In another experiment, our fine-tuned reward model reliably scores risk-averse reasoning above risk-neutral or excessively risk-averse alternatives (99.6% pairwise accuracy). We replicate these effects at different scales (Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-14B) and across model families (Gemma-3-12B-IT and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct). Overall, we find that risk aversion learned at low stakes can generalize OOD to astronomically high stakes, though not yet consistently enough to serve as a reliable failsafe. Achieving that level of consistency is an open problem.

</details>


### [57] [Safe Inference-Time Alignment via Lagrangian Reward Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02781)
*Yaswanth Chittepu,Ativ Joshi,Sohini Chintala,Scott Niekum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了LARA框架，通过在推理时对偶化约束并求解一维凸优化问题来校准对偶变量，实现了在安全约束下对冻结语言模型的对齐，显著改善了有用性与无害性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时对齐方法通常优化单一标量分数，导致显式的安全约束要么被忽略，要么需要通过手动调整惩罚系数来编码，缺乏原则性的约束处理机制。

Method: 提出拉格朗日奖励增强（LARA）框架：从带有奖励模型和成本模型的KL正则化约束目标出发，通过对偶化约束将优化问题简化为关于非负对偶变量的一维凸问题，在小规模校准集上估计该对偶变量，定义出可即插即用的增强奖励信号，直接应用于现有的推理时对齐方法中。

Result: LARA在序列级（如Best-of-N重排序）和token级（奖励引导解码）推理时对齐方法上均改善了有用性-无害性权衡；其中Best-of-N在推理时方法中表现最佳，性能接近基于微调的直接对齐基线。

Conclusion: LARA为推理时对齐提供了一种通用且原则性的安全约束处理框架，成功桥接了约束优化与实际推理方法，使得冻结模型的推理时对齐能够在保证安全性的前提下达到与微调方法相当的性能水平。

Abstract: Inference-time alignment steers a frozen language model during decoding using auxiliary reward signals, avoiding the cost of repeated weight updates. However, existing inference-time alignment methods typically optimize a single scalar score, so explicit safety constraints must either be ignored or encoded through manually tuned penalties. We propose Lagrangian Reward Augmentation (LARA), a general inference-time alignment framework under safety constraints. Starting from a KL-regularized constrained objective with a reward model and a cost model, LARA dualizes the constraint and reduces the optimization problem to a one-dimensional convex problem over a nonnegative dual variable. Estimated on a small calibration set, this dual variable defines an augmented reward that can be used as a drop-in scoring signal within existing inference-time alignment methods. For sequence-level sampling methods, such as Best-of-N reranking, the calibrated dual variable corresponds to the solution of the expected-cost constrained problem. For token-level reward-guided decoding methods, the same construction yields a principled dual-calibrated heuristic rather than an exact constrained-policy guarantee. We evaluate LARA on both sequence-level and token-level inference-time alignment methods, and find that LARA improves the helpfulness-harmlessness tradeoff, with Best-of-N achieving the best performance among inference-time methods, approaching finetuning-based direct alignment baselines.

</details>


### [58] [HiFA4: Training-Free 4-bit FlashAttention on Ascend HIF4 NPUs for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04302)
*Hui Dong,Yanzhao Li,Jie Gao,Chunlu Li,Zhiyuan Zhang,Yupeng Sun,Zhenyuan Chen,Zhiqiang Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiFA4是首个在昇腾NPU上利用HIF4 4-bit量化执行FlashAttention的算子级设计，通过Smooth-QK和P-Reordering两种机制，有效降低了量化导致的精度损失和决策漂移，在多个大语言模型上显著恢复了准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM推理中，FlashAttention算子中的QK^T和PV矩阵乘法计算密集，现有量化方法在低比特（4-bit）量化时会导致显著的精度下降和决策漂移。本文旨在设计一种面向昇腾NPU的4-bit量化方案，在保持在线softmax状态为FP16精度的同时，最大程度恢复因量化损失的模型性能。

Method: 提出HiFA4后训练算子级设计，将FlashAttention中的QK^T和PV均实现为4-bit HIF4 Cube GEMM。包含两个核心机制：(1) Smooth-QK：在RoPE之后对Q和K应用校准静态的逐通道等效重缩放，将量化难度从K转移到Q，避免推理时的逐分块在线归约；(2) P-Reordering：使用与PV GEMM相同的量化注意力权重P_hat累积softmax归一化器，而非更高精度重建，同时允许将归一化器融合到PV Cube GEMM中。该方法还揭示了不一致公式会引入相干输出缩放误差。

Result: 在五个LLM上验证有效性。Qwen3-8B上恢复37.5%的量化精度损失，准确率损失从1.12个百分点降至0.70个百分点，与BF16不一致的MMLU预测从16.3%降至8.2%，MMLU准确率回归减少57%（从1071降至465）。Gemma2-9B上保持与BF16相差0.7个百分点内的精度。LLaMA3.1-8B、Mistral-7B和Phi-4B上，MMLU回归减少41-52%。初步指令调度分析显示关键路径延迟可减少35.4%。

Conclusion: HiFA4成功实现了在昇腾NPU上对FlashAttention的4-bit量化，在多个主流LLM上显著降低了量化导致的性能退化，证明了通过精细的量化策略设计可以在保持推理效率的同时有效恢复模型精度，为LLM的低比特量化推理提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We present HiFA4, a post-training operator-level design that executes both QK^T and PV in FlashAttention as 4-bit HIF4 Cube GEMMs for LLM inference on Ascend NPUs, while maintaining the online softmax state in FP16. To our knowledge, HiFA4 is the first Ascend-HIF4-targeted design of this kind evaluated on standard NLP benchmarks.
  HiFA4 combines two mechanisms. Smooth-QK applies a calibration-static per-channel equivalent rescaling to Q and K after RoPE, transferring quantization difficulty from K to Q without per-tile online reduction at inference. P-Reordering accumulates the softmax normalizer from the same quantized attention weights P_hat used in the PV GEMM, rather than from a higher-precision reconstruction. We show that this inconsistent formulation introduces a coherent output-scaling error, and validate the effect on a Qwen3-8B Layer-0 MMLU trace, where all 3.6M measured attention tiles exhibit net probability-mass loss with median epsilon_bar = -0.064. P-Reordering also allows the normalizer to be fused into the PV Cube GEMM.
  Across five LLMs, HiFA4 reduces quantization-induced decision drift. On Qwen3-8B, it recovers 37.5% of the accuracy gap introduced by direct HIF4 quantization, narrows the sample-weighted accuracy loss from 1.12 pp to 0.70 pp, reduces BF16-inconsistent MMLU predictions from 16.3% to 8.2%, and cuts MMLU accuracy regressions by 57% (1071 to 465). On Gemma2-9B, mild smoothing keeps HiFA4 within 0.7 pp of BF16 while reducing MMLU regressions by 27%. On LLaMA3.1-8B, Mistral-7B, and Phi-4B, where Smooth-QK is disabled, P-Reordering with the adopted Q-Mean auxiliary still reduces full-set MMLU regressions by 41-52%. A preliminary instruction-scheduling analysis projects a 35.4% critical-path latency reduction relative to BF16 by fusing the softmax normalizer into the PV Cube GEMM; on-hardware validation is left to future work.

</details>


### [59] [Induction Heads Interpolate N-Grams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02800)
*Francesco D'Angelo,Oguz Kaan Yuksel,Swathi Shree Narashiman,Nicolas Flammarion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Transformer中归纳头的两种统计平滑机制：软上下文匹配（类似Jelinek-Mercer平滑）和BOS token引入的伪计数（类似Dirichlet平滑），表明归纳头实现的是正则化的上下文估计而非简单计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 归纳头被认为是在Transformer中实现上下文学习的注意力电路，但目前对其实现的具体估计器缺乏精确刻画。已有研究未能清晰地阐明归纳头如何在统计意义上进行预测和估计。

Method: 在order-$k$马尔可夫链上训练Transformer，分析归纳头的注意力模式。构建一个解耦的Transformer来分离两种平滑机制：（1）有限注意力权重尺度下的软上下文匹配；（2）BOS token引入的加性伪计数。

Result: 发现两种互补的平滑机制：软上下文匹配估计器聚合精确和部分上下文匹配，按重叠度指数加权，产生数据依赖的跨上下文阶数插值；BOS token引入伪计数，恢复Dirichlet风格平滑。训练后的Transformer恢复了预测的注意力模式，并在多种设置下匹配或超越经典的基于计数的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该研究将归纳头的机制可解释性与经典统计平滑方法联系起来，揭示了Transformer学习的是正则化的上下文估计策略，而非简单的计数。这一发现加深了对Transformer上下文学习机制的理解。

Abstract: Induction heads are attention circuits believed to underlie in-context learning in transformers, yet a precise characterization of the estimators they implement remains elusive. We study transformers trained on order-$k$ Markov chains and identify two complementary smoothing mechanisms. First, at finite attention-weight scale, the circuit implements a soft context-matching estimator: it aggregates contributions from exact and partial context matches, weighted exponentially by their overlap, and induces a data-dependent interpolation across context orders analogous to Jelinek-Mercer smoothing. Second, a beginning-of-sequence (BOS) token induces additive pseudo-counts, recovering Dirichlet-style smoothing. We construct a disentangled transformer implementing both mechanisms and show that trained transformers recover the predicted attention patterns. Across settings where pseudo-count smoothing is optimal or lower-order contexts provide structured evidence, trained transformers match or outperform classical count-based baselines. Our results bridge mechanistic interpretability of induction heads with classical statistical smoothing, revealing that transformers learn to regularize in-context estimation rather than simply count.

</details>


### [60] [SMART: A Machine Learning and Monte Carlo Framework for Rapid Analysis of Stochastic Transistor Aging and Process Variation in Digital Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05187)
*Arash Esshaghi,Siavash Es'haghi,Gholamreza Shahabadi,Alireza Moradi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SMART框架，结合机器学习与蒙特卡洛仿真，实现快速高保真可靠性分析，分析时间减少94.54%，误差仅1.63%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着CMOS技术进入深纳米尺度，数字电路可靠性面临BTI和PV随机效应联合威胁。传统可靠性分析方法依赖计算密集型仿真或大型查找表，无法高效扩展至大规模设计，成为设计空间探索的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出SMART框架，将机器学习与蒙特卡洛仿真集成。采用随机森林回归直接预测门延迟分布，绕过耗时的原子模型参数提取；利用贝叶斯优化自动调节超参数，确保跨不同库的预测鲁棒性。

Result: 在ISCAS85基准电路上的实验验证表明，与现有最先进方法相比，SMART实现了94.54%的分析时间减少，同时保持仅1.63%的平均精度误差。

Conclusion: 通过将计算复杂度转移至离线训练阶段，SMART框架为设计弹性的可靠性感知数字系统提供了可扩展、精确的解决方案，有效解决了传统方法的可扩展性瓶颈。

Abstract: As CMOS technology scales into the deep nanometer regime, digital circuit reliability is increasingly threatened by the combined stochastic effects of Bias Temperature Instability (BTI) and Process Variation (PV). Traditional reliability analysis methods, which rely on computationally intensive simulations or extensive lookup tables, fail to scale efficiently for large designs, creating a critical bottleneck in design space exploration. To address this, we propose SMART, a novel framework that integrates Machine Learning (ML) with Monte Carlo simulation to enable rapid, high-fidelity reliability analysis. SMART employs Random Forest regression to predict gate delay distributions directly, bypassing time-consuming atomic model parameter extractions. Crucially, the model utilizes Bayesian Optimization for automated hyperparameter tuning, ensuring maximum predictive robustness across diverse libraries. Experimental validation on ISCAS85 benchmark circuits demonstrates that SMART achieves a 94.54% reduction in analysis time compared to state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining a remarkable average accuracy error of just 1.63%. By shifting computational complexity to an offline training phase, the proposed framework offers a scalable, accurate solution for designing resilient, reliability-aware digital systems.

</details>


### [61] [Relational Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Dynamic Pricing in High-Speed Railway Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05179)
*Enrique Adrian Villarrubia-Martin,David Muñoz-Valero,Luis Rodriguez-Benitez,Giovanni Montana,Luis Jimenez-Linares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于实体图的图神经网络多智能体强化学习方法，用于在部分可观测的放松管制铁路系统中实现动态定价，在复杂市场环境中实现了比基准方法更高的收入和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在放松管制的铁路系统中，运营者必须在部分可观测环境下动态定价，由于监管限制禁止竞争对手之间通信以防止合谋，智能体必须仅从可观测的市场数据推断战略互动。现有的多智能体强化学习方法通常将观测视为非结构化向量，忽视了支配战略互动的潜在市场拓扑结构。

Method: 提出实体图建模方法，将环境表示为运营单元图而非决策智能体或静态基础设施，编码实体间的竞争、协调和连接关系。在此基础上，扩展多智能体双延迟深度确定性策略梯度算法（TD3），结合基于图的表示学习：通过多层关系图卷积网络处理实体特征，并通过学习的注意力机制进行聚合。

Result: 在铁路定价强化学习环境中的实验表明，该框架在两种不同市场复杂性设置下，相比代表性的关系和非关系基准方法，实现了更高的收入和稳定性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了部分可观测市场中战略互动推断的挑战，证明了将市场拓扑结构显式建模为实体图并进行关系学习的有效性，为放松管制市场中的动态定价问题提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: In liberalised railway systems, operators must set prices dynamically in an environment with partial observability, as they retain private information about their objectives and performance, where regulatory constraints prohibit communication or direct information exchange between competitors to prevent explicit collusion. Consequently, agents must learn to infer strategic interactions only from observable market data which presents a significant challenge for multi-agent reinforcement learning, where standard approaches typically treat observations as unstructured vectors, ignoring the underlying market topology that governs strategic interactions. To address this, an entity graph modelling approach is proposed, which represents the environment as a graph of operational units, rather than decision-making agents or static infrastructure, encoding competition, coordination, and connectivity relations between entities. Then, an extension of the multi-agent twin delayed deep deterministic policy gradient algorithm with graph-based representation learning processes the features of the entities through a multi-layer relational graph convolutional network and aggregates them via a learnt attention mechanism. Experimental results in a rail pricing reinforcement learning environment show that this novel framework achieves higher revenue and stability in two different settings of increasing market complexity compared to a representative selection of relational and non-relational baselines. The code is publicly available at: https://github.com/Kinrre/RelationalRailPricing-RL

</details>


### [62] [Training Hybrid Block Diffusion Language Models with Partial Bidirectionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02805)
*Pranshu Chaturvedi,Parth Shroff,Tarun Suresh,Hangoo Kang,Kaiyue Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BDLM Mamba-Attention混合架构，通过将反向Mamba扫描限制在活跃去噪块内实现精确缓存，解决了扩散模型与Mamba结合时的缓存复用问题，显著提升了长上下文生成的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文大语言模型生成面临内存带宽瓶颈问题：每个解码步骤都需要从内存流式传输累积的KV缓存，带宽需求随上下文长度增长而仅生成一个token。现有两种并行方案——基于Mamba的高效注意力变体和线性时间混合器、以及并行生成token块——在结合时遇到技术挑战：早期混合扩散模型（如DiffuMamba）使用双向Mamba混合，其反向扫描需要扫描整个序列，导致状态非前缀独占，无法在逐块扩散时精确复用为缓存。

Method: 提出BDLM Mamba-Attention混合架构，通过将反向Mamba扫描限制在活跃去噪块内，实现了跨块的精确缓存机制，解决了双向Mamba在因果生成中缓存复用的技术难题。

Result: 在87M参数DCLM扫描中，BDLM Mamba-H相比BDLM attention和全序列基线取得最佳C4-en验证困惑度；在350M参数规模下与BDLM attention保持竞争力。在长上下文推理中，BDLM Mamba-H在65K token时吞吐量达到全序列DiffuMamba-H的19.7倍，在262K token时达到BDLM attention的3.7倍。

Conclusion: BDLM Mamba-Attention混合架构成功解决了扩散模型与Mamba结合时的缓存复用难题，证明Mamba混合架构是一种有潜力的长上下文扩散架构，为高效长上下文生成提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: High-throughput long-context generation is one of the central challenges for large language models. Generation is typically memory-bandwidth-bound rather than compute-bound: each decoding step must stream the accumulated key/value (KV) cache from memory, so bandwidth demand grows with context length while only one token is emitted. Two parallel approaches have therefore emerged: reducing memory access with efficient attention variants and linear-time mixers such as Mamba, or increasing parallel computation by generating blocks of tokens at once. However, technical challenges arise when combining these two ideas. Earlier hybrid diffusion models such as DiffuMamba use bidirectional Mamba mixing, including a reverse-direction scan relative to causal generation. This reverse scan needs to scan the entire sequence, so its states are not prefix-only and cannot be precisely reused as a cache even when diffusion is performed block by block. We propose a BDLM Mamba--attention hybrid that addresses this challenge by restricting the reverse Mamba scan to the active denoising block, which enables exact caching across blocks. In an 87M-parameter DCLM sweep, BDLM Mamba-H achieves the best C4-en validation perplexity compared to BDLM attention and full-sequence baselines. At 350M parameters, it remains competitive with BDLM attention. For long-context inference, BDLM Mamba-H reaches 19.7x the throughput of full-sequence DiffuMamba-H at 65K tokens and 3.7x the throughput of BDLM attention at 262K, showing that Mamba hybrids are a potential long-context diffusion architecture.

</details>


### [63] [Less Tokens, Better Forecasts: Sparse Residual Routing for Efficient Weather Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02829)
*Janet Wang,Yunbei Zhang,Lin Zhao,Xi Xiao,Jihun Hamm,Xiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Sparse-Reslim：一种无参数的即插即用路由模块，通过仅处理25%的空间token并采用残差更新机制，在保持固定网格要求的同时，实现了约2.5-3.18倍训练加速和2.2倍内存降低，且在所有评估变量上均提升了预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于ViT的天气预报模型对所有空间token执行统一计算，但邻近大气网格点往往数值相似且大区域随时间平滑演化，导致大量中间token计算冗余。传统token效率方法（剪枝或合并）通过移除或融合token降低成本，但天气预报是时空密集预测问题，需要将历史大气状态映射到原始经纬度网格的未来值，每个网格单元必须保持物理意义表征，特别是在自回归推演场景下。

Method: 引入Sparse-Reslim无参数插件式路由模块：仅路由25%的空间token通过昂贵的中间transformer块，将这些块视为残差更新，计算路由token的变化量并将该增量散射回完整序列；未被选中的token精确保留路由前的表征，不丢弃或用掩码token替换任何网格单元，不引入融合层或额外参数。

Result: 在ERA5分辨率（最高达业务化0.25°标准）和两个模型家族（确定性Transformer和扩散模型）上，Sparse-Reslim在所有评估变量上提升预测精度；主设置下训练速度提升约2.5倍，0.25°分辨率下达到3.18倍加速，峰值内存降低超过2.2倍；对照分解实验表明精度增益主要来自稀疏路由本身，随机token选择在无选择器开销下提供额外正则化收益。

Conclusion: Sparse-Reslim成功解决了ViT天气预报模型的计算冗余与固定网格要求之间的矛盾，通过稀疏路由与残差更新机制实现了精度与效率的双重提升，证明了token高效处理策略在时空密集预测任务中的有效性，为大规模气象预报模型的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Existing ViT-based weather forecasting models apply uniform computation across all spatial tokens, even though nearby atmospheric grid points often contain similar values and large regions evolve smoothly over time. This makes much of the intermediate per-token computation redundant. Standard token-efficiency methods, such as pruning or merging, reduce cost by removing or fusing tokens. However, weather forecasting is a spatiotemporal dense prediction problem in which a history of atmospheric states must be mapped to future values on the original latitude-longitude grid. Thus, every grid cell must retain a physically meaningful representation, especially under autoregressive rollout. We introduce Sparse-Reslim, a parameter-free plug-in routing module that makes sparse token processing compatible with this fixed-grid requirement. Sparse-Reslim routes only 25% of spatial tokens through the expensive middle transformer blocks and treats those blocks as residual updates: it computes the change produced for the routed tokens and scatters only this delta back to the full sequence. Unselected tokens keep their pre-routing representations exactly, so no grid cell is dropped or replaced by a mask token, and no fusion layer or additional parameters are introduced. Across ERA5 resolutions up to the operational 0.25\textdegree{} standard and two model families, a deterministic Transformer and a diffusion model, Sparse-Reslim improves forecast accuracy on every evaluated variable while substantially reducing cost: training is about 2.5x faster in the main settings and reaches 3.18x speedup at 0.25\textdegree{}, with over 2.2x lower peak memory. A controlled decomposition shows that the accuracy gain comes primarily from sparse routing itself, while random token selection provides an additional regularization benefit without selector overhead.

</details>


### [64] [On the Design Space of Discrete Diffusion Online Adaptation for Molecular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02834)
*Trevor Chen,Ariel Dai,Jason Yang,Riccardo De Santi,Daniel Khalil,Wenda Chu,Nate Gruver,Pranav Murugan,Alexander F. G. Goldberg,Maruan Al-Shedivat,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对离散扩散模型在分子优化中的在线适应问题，提出了一套高效反馈的实用方案（包含获取函数、奖励整形、去偏、回放和有效性控制），在有限预算下显著优于离线微调和推理时搜索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子优化通常从预训练生成模型开始，但在测试时需要利用有限的预言机预算将生成过程转向特定任务的高奖励分子。对于离散扩散模型，这一在线适应过程涉及多个选择（候选评估、奖励更新、反馈重用、偏离预训练先验的程度），这些选择此前多被孤立研究，尚不清楚它们在全在线循环中是互补、冗余还是相互干扰。

Method: 作者在六个小分子结合亲和力任务和三个蛋白质适应度任务上进行了控制实验，系统研究了在线适应循环中的多种组件，包括获取函数、奖励整形、模型去偏、经验回放以及有效性惩罚。通过对比实验分析各组件的独立作用和相互影响。

Result: 研究发现：获取函数、奖励整形和模型去偏提供了互补的性能提升路径（尤其对小分子）；经验回放进一步稳定学习过程；有效性惩罚使小分子探索保持在有效分子流形上。整合这些组件的方案在相同预言机调用预算和GPU时间核算下，显著优于离线微调和推理时搜索基线，尤其当高奖励候选需要较大幅度偏离预训练先验时优势最大。

Conclusion: 研究提出了一套实用高效的分子优化配方：结合获取函数、奖励整形、去偏、回放和有效性控制的在线微调方法。该方案在有限反馈预算下实现了更优的分子优化性能，为离散扩散模型的在线适应提供了系统性的指导原则。

Abstract: Molecular optimization often starts from a pretrained generative model that captures a broad prior over valid molecular structures. At test time, however, the goal is not to sample from this prior, but to use a limited oracle budget to shift generation toward task-specific high-reward molecules. We study this adaptation problem for discrete diffusion models. Each online round couples several choices. The loop must decide which candidates to evaluate, how rewards become model updates, which feedback to reuse, and how far to move beyond the pretrained prior. These choices have mostly been studied in isolation, leaving open whether they complement one another, become redundant, or interfere inside a full online adaptation loop. We conduct controlled studies across six small-molecule binding-affinity tasks and three protein-fitness tasks. We find that acquisition, reward shaping, and model debiasing provide complementary routes to higher reward, especially for small molecules. Replay further stabilizes learning, while validity penalties keep small-molecule exploration on the valid molecular manifold. Together, these findings point to a practical recipe for feedback-efficient molecular optimization: online fine-tuning with acquisition, reward shaping, debiasing, replay, and validity control. This recipe outperforms offline fine-tuning and inference-time search baselines under matched oracle-call budgets and GPU-hour accounting. The gains are largest when high-reward candidates require larger shifts from the pretrained prior.

</details>


### [65] [Labeled-Data-Free Meta-Learning: Efficient Task Generation Using Pre-trained Models and Unlabeled Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02850)
*Lei Sun,Yusuke Tanaka,Tomoharu Iwata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合预训练模型和未标记数据的无标记数据元学习方法，避免了高成本的模型反演过程，实现了最高104倍加速和8.4%-36.4%的分类精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中获取标记数据成本高昂或受隐私限制，现有的无数据元学习（DFML）方法依赖模型反演生成训练数据，但该过程在高维数据上计算开销大且困难。

Method: 提出一种新的元学习设定，联合利用预训练模型和未标记数据，通过预训练模型为未标记数据分配软标签来生成元训练任务，并引入基于任务置信度和类别分布平衡的任务加权机制以保证元学习的有效性。

Result: 实验表明该方法显著降低了计算成本，在少样本分类任务上相比最先进的DFML方法实现了最高104倍的速度提升，精度提升8.4%至36.4%。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功避免了模型反演的高成本问题，通过有效利用未标记数据和预训练模型，在保持或提升性能的同时大幅提高了计算效率，为无标记数据元学习提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Meta-learning without labeled data is crucial for real-world applications, where obtaining labeled datasets can be expensive or restricted due to privacy concerns. Data-Free Meta-Learning (DFML) addresses this challenge by leveraging pre-trained models without access to training data. However, existing DFML methods rely on model inversion to generate training data, a process that is generally difficult and computationally expensive due to the need to generate high-dimensional data matching the original distribution. To address this limitation, we propose a novel meta-learning setting that avoids model inversion by jointly leveraging pre-trained models and unlabeled data. Our method generates meta-training tasks by assigning soft labels from pre-trained models to unlabeled data. Since the quality of these tasks can vary, we introduce a task-weighting mechanism based on task confidence and class distribution balance to ensure effective meta-learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach substantially reduces computational cost and improves generalization, achieving up to 104-fold speedup and 8.4 percent to 36.4 percent improvements in few-shot classification accuracy compared to state-of-the-art DFML methods.

</details>


### [66] [Trading Confidence: Comprehensive Uncertainty Estimation in Algorithmic Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02864)
*Lin Li,Li Rong Wang,Hsuan Fu,Xiuyi Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不确定性感知的强化学习框架，通过整合分布不确定性、认知不确定性和随机不确定性估计，显著提升了金融交易策略在动态市场环境中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习模型在动态金融市场中难以适应突然的市场扰动，导致次优交易决策。金融市场存在高度不确定性，包括随机波动、模型局限性和制度转变等因素，传统方法无法有效应对这些挑战。
