With this work, we show that if the goal of the mined LPMs is to understand a process, using the top-scoring LPMs as an optimal sample is a poor choice because of high repetition. We propose a framework for grouping LPMs and creating an optimal sample by taking one representative LPM for each group. We measure similarity between models via established process model similarity measures or by comparing the context in which an LPM appears. The context is formed using data attributes available in the underlying event logs. We demonstrate the usefulness of grouping on multiple event logs by comparing repetition and coverage between samples comprised of the top-scoring models and the representatives of discovered groups.

</details>


### [242] [Air Quality Downscaling with Station-Guided Pseudo-Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05292)
*Guorun Wang,Simone Foti,Andreas D. Demou,Leonidas Kotoulas,Theodoros Christoudias,Alexandros Koliousis,Mihalis Nicolaou,Stefanos Zafeiriou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种站点引导的高分辨率PM₂.5降尺度框架，通过多尺度Transformer网络和时空高斯混合策略，将欧洲CAMS大气组分场超分辨率重建至约1公里（40倍），有效恢复精细空间结构并校正局地偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气场降尺度面临空间支持不匹配的核心挑战：栅格像素代表区域平均值，而地面观测站点是连续空间信号上的离散、非对齐样本。现有方法难以有效利用稀疏的地面站点数据监督高分辨率重建。

Method: 提出站点引导框架：1）融合粗分辨率CAMS大气组分场与多源异构辅助数据（人类活动、土地覆盖、高程、卫星气溶胶观测、风场）；2）构建多尺度Transformer网络，实现40倍超分辨率（至约1公里）与偏差校正，不依赖时序建模；3）引入时间无关的传播策略，采用空间高斯混合插值OpenAQ观测，解决稀疏原位数据的密集监督问题。

Result: 在欧洲范围的广泛定性和站点级评估表明：模型能够有效恢复精细空间结构，并成功缓解CAMS数据的局地偏差问题。

Conclusion: 该框架有效弥合了栅格区域平均值与离散站点观测之间的空间支持差异，为欧洲高分辨率PM₂.5降尺度提供了可行方案，证明了站点引导策略在解决稀疏监督问题上的有效性。

Abstract: Super-resolving coarse atmospheric fields to local PM$_{2.5}$ variations is uniquely challenged by a mismatch in spatial support: while pixels represent regional averages, ground-truth observations are discrete, unaligned samples of a continuous spatial signal. To bridge this gap, we present a station-guided framework for high-resolution PM$_{2.5}$ downscaling over Europe. Taking coarse CAMS atmospheric composition fields alongside heterogeneous side information (i.e., human activity, land cover, elevation, satellite aerosol observations, and wind fields) our framework jointly super-resolves ($\times 40$, $\approx$ 1 km) and bias-corrects CAMS rasters, without relying on temporal sequence modelling. To address the challenge of densely supervising our multi-scale transformer network with sparse in-situ data, we introduce a time-agnostic propagation strategy that utilises spatial Gaussian blending of interpolated OpenAQ observations. Extensive qualitative and station-level evaluations across Europe demonstrate that our model recovers fine-grained spatial structures and effectively mitigates localised CAMS biases.

</details>


### [243] [Enhancing the Forecasting Capability of Multi-Model Blending Algorithms for Extreme Precipitation via Joint Use of Station and Gridded Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04862)
*Yu Wang,Yong Cao,Kan Dai,Yue Shen,Xiaoqing Zeng,Ruixia Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于U-Net的两阶段融合框架，通过站点-网格联合监督机制融合6个数值天气预报模式，显著提升极端降水预报准确率，暴雨TS评分提高38.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值天气预报模式在极端降水预报中存在系统性强度低估和空间位移问题，传统多模式像素级融合方法会导致降水范围扩大和极值平滑，难以准确捕捉极端降水事件。

Method: 提出基于U-Net的两阶段框架（概率分类+数值重建），融合六个主要NWP模式预报；创新性地引入站点-网格联合监督机制，将中国2411个国家气象站观测数据集成到损失函数中，同时约束空间结构和峰值强度。

Result: 使用2025年汛期独立样本验证，暴雨(≥50mm)TS评分较最优NWP提高38.4%；对于温带气旋和副热带高压驱动的极端事件(≥100mm)，TS提升至0.1以上；模型具有数据驱动的空间校正能力，有效修正系统性雨带位移；站点观测的引入使暴雨TS额外提升10.4%并有效平衡Bias。

Conclusion: 多源联合监督在增强极端降水事件捕捉方面具有显著效果，该方法成功将原本参考价值极低的极端降水预报转化为具有一定业务实用价值的预报产品。

Abstract: Accurate extreme precipitation forecasting is critical for disaster mitigation but remains challenging for numerical weather prediction (NWP) models due to systemic intensity underestimation and spatial displacement. Traditional precipitation multi-model blending algorithms perform pixel-by-pixel blending on the forecast field based on weights, which may lead to the expansion of precipitation areas and the smoothing of extreme values. This study proposes an U-Net based two-stage framework: probability classification followed by value reconstruction, to blend forecasts from six major NWP models. A novel station-grid joint supervision mechanism is introduced by integrating observations from 2411 national meteorological stations in China into the loss function, simultaneously constraining spatial structures and peak intensities. Evaluations using independent samples from the 2025 flood season demonstrate that our model significantly outperforms both individual NWPs and current operational products. For rainstorms (>=50 mm), the Threat Score (TS) improved by 38.4% compared to the best NWP. Notably, for extreme events (>=100 mm) driven by extratropical cyclones and the subtropical high, the model successfully elevated the TS to above 0.1, transforming forecasts from having negligible reference value into those with certain operational utility. Furthermore, the model exhibits data-driven spatial correction capabilities, effectively realigning systematic rainbelt displacements with actual precipitation centers. The inclusion of station observations specifically enhanced the TS for rainstorms by 10.4% and effectively balanced the Bias. These results highlight the efficacy of multi-source joint supervision in enhancing the capture of extreme precipitation events.

</details>


### [244] [TREK: Distill to Explore, Reinforce to Refine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05339)
*Yuanda Xu,Zhengze Zhou,Kayhan Behdin,Jelena Markovic-Voronov,Hejian Sang,Xiaomin Li,Wenhui Zhu,Xinchen Du,Aida Rahmattalabi,Ran He,Sen Na,Zhipeng Wang,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TREK方法，通过教师引导的前向KL阶段扩展学生模型的探索支持，解决GRPO在难题上因正确解超出策略支持而停滞的问题，在数学推理和智能体任务上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO方法在当前策略能够采样到有用推理轨迹时表现有效，但对于难题（correct solution modes位于学生模型的策略支持之外）会停滞不前。这种情况下，学生模型难以通过自身探索找到正确解。

Method: TREK采用分阶段程序：(1)识别学生模型通过率极低的提示；(2)向提议源（教师模型）查询以生成验证过的候选解；(3)按当前学生模型似然保留top-r个提议；(4)应用前向KL阶段将这些验证模式拉入学生模型的支持中；(5)返回标准策略内GRPO精炼。该方法仅消耗验证过的输出轨迹，因此可使用外部黑盒/白盒教师或同一模型的额外推理时上下文。

Result: 数学推理任务上，使用DeepSeek-V4提议的TREK在AIME 2025和AIME 2024上全面提升Qwen3模型各规模性能；Qwen3-8B上AIME 2025从36.9提升至40.3，AIME 2024从47.9提升至51.1(avg@16)，自上下文变体分别达38.5和49.6。智能体任务上，ALFWorld成功率从75.8提升至82.8，ScienceWorld成功率从12.5提升至26.7；在最难任务类型上，TREK在训练早期即达到高成功率，而标准GRPO需要更多优化步骤。

Conclusion: TREK通过将蒸馏用于探索支持扩展而非简单模仿，有效解决了GRPO在难题上的停滞问题。该方法具有通用性，可适配不同类型的教师源，在数学推理和智能体任务上均取得显著改进，尤其加速了困难任务的学习进程。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is effective when the current policy already samples useful reasoning trajectories, but it stalls on hard prompts whose correct solution modes lie outside the student's on-policy support. We propose TREK (Teacher-Routed Exploration via Forward KL), a simple staged procedure that uses distillation not for imitation but for exploration support expansion. A key advantage of TREK is its generality: because it only consumes verified output trajectories, it can use an external black-box teacher, a white-box teacher, or the same model given additional inference-time context, and it can efficiently identify which hard-prompt samples are most worth consolidating even when teacher internals are unavailable. TREK first identifies prompts where the unaided student has very low pass rate, queries a proposal source to produce verified candidate solutions, keeps the top-$r$ proposals ranked by current student likelihood, applies a short forward-KL phase to pull those verified modes into the student's support, and then returns to standard on-policy GRPO refinement. On mathematical reasoning, TREK with DeepSeek-V4 proposals improves Qwen3 models across all tested scales on AIME 2024 and AIME 2025; for Qwen3-8B, it improves AIME 2025 from 36.9 to 40.3 and AIME 2024 from 47.9 to 51.1 (avg@16), while the self-context variant reaches 38.5 and 49.6 without an external teacher. On agentic tasks, TREK raises ALFWorld success rate from 75.8 to 82.8 and ScienceWorld success rate from 12.5 to 26.7; notably, on the hardest task types, TREK achieves high success rates early in training while unaided GRPO requires substantially more optimization steps to reach comparable levels.

</details>


### [245] [Active Learning on Adversarially Corrupted Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04869)
*Marco Bressan,Nicolò Cesa-Bianchi,Tommaso d`Orsi,Emmanuel Esposito,Silvio Lattanzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示图的顶点扩展性（vertex expansion）是决定对抗性结构攻击下主动学习算法查询复杂度的关键因素，并提出高效近似算法恢复被篡改顶点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中恶意实体会篡改现有网络结构。为模拟此威胁，论文定义了对抗模型：攻击者在图中隐藏一组“腐败顶点”，通过添加边（包括腐败顶点之间及与原图之间的边）来扩大其在原图中的邻域范围。研究动机是设计高效的主动学习算法，在少量标签查询下识别出这些被篡改的顶点。

Method: 1. 将问题建模为对抗性结构攻击下的主动学习问题，攻击者能力用腐败顶点在原图中的邻域大小度量。2. 设计多项式时间算法，通过精心适配求解最小扩展问题的平方和（sum-of-squares）算法，在基数约束下找到具有小顶点扩展性的集合。3. 理论分析查询复杂度与攻击者能力、顶点扩展性的多项式依赖关系。

Result: 提出了高效算法，能够在查询复杂度与攻击者能力、原图顶点扩展性呈多项式关系的条件下，近似恢复被篡改顶点集合。这是首次证明顶点扩展性在对抗性结构攻击下的主动学习算法查询复杂度中起决定性作用。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了图顶点扩展性与对抗鲁棒主动学习查询复杂度之间的理论联系，为理解结构攻击下的学习极限提供了新视角。所提出的基数约束下寻找小顶点扩展集合的多项式时间算法具有独立的理论价值。

Abstract: Motivated by real-world scenarios where malicious entities tamper with existing networks, we define a model where an adversary seeks to hide a set of \emph{corrupted vertices} inside a graph $G^*$. To this end, the adversary can add edges between the corrupted vertices, as well as edges between the corrupted vertices and $G^*$, and its power is then measured by the size of the \emph{neighborhood} of the corrupted vertices in $G^*$. Our goal is to design an active learning algorithm that efficiently finds the subset of corrupted vertices using a small number of label queries. We devise an efficient algorithm that approximately recovers the corrupted vertices with a query complexity that depends polynomially on both the power of the adversary and the \emph{vertex expansion} of $G^*$, a fundamental measure of graph connectivity. At the heart of this result is a polynomial-time algorithm, obtained by carefully adapting sum-of-squares algorithms for approximating minimum expansion, that finds a set with small vertex expansion subject to cardinality constraints. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first time that the vertex expansion is shown to play a key role in determining the query complexity of active learning algorithms robust to structural adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [246] [What Does a Discrete Diffusion Model Learn?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05381)
*Rodrigo Casado Noguerales,Bernhard Schölkopf,Thomas Hofmann,Aran Raoufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为离散扩散模型建立了严格的CTMC ELBO理论框架，证明了"Oracle Distance"定理（负ELBO精确等于数据熵加路径KL散度），揭示了去噪器、bridge plug-in和score三种参数化坐标的等价关系与转换公式，统一解释了现有方法并解决了均匀扩散ELBO发散问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型存在多种参数化方式（去噪器、score ratio、bridge plug-in predictor），但缺乏统一的理论框架来理解它们的本质联系。现有文献中对不同损失函数优化的目标、ELBO的真实含义以及不同参数化的优劣缺乏严格分析，导致理论理解不完整，实践中出现如均匀扩散ELBO在初始化时发散等问题。

Method: 从任意噪声过程的连续时间马尔可夫链(CTMC) ELBO的严格推导出发（包含边界项），证明了Oracle Distance定理。理论分析了信息率-$\frac{d}{dt}I(Z_0; Z_t)$与不可约成本的关系。针对token因子化噪声的序列，推导了三种精确坐标（去噪器、cavity/bridge plug-in、score）及其闭式转换公式。通过可精确求解的模型进行数值验证。

Result: 证明了负ELBO精确等于数据熵加oracle逆向过程到学习过程的路径KL散度（非仅上界）；唯一优化器是给定当前噪声状态的真实逆向跳变率的条件期望；所有噪声过程共享相同的最佳可达负ELBO（数据熵）。建立了三种参数化的闭式转换关系，识别了文献中各损失的实际优化目标，恢复MDM、UDM、SEDD、GIDD为特例。解释了去噪器和cavity在mask扩散中重合但在均匀扩散中不重合的原因；证明去噪器参数化使均匀ELBO在初始化时发散而bridge plug-in保持有限。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型的多种参数化在跳变率层面实为同一对象的不同坐标表示。Oracle Distance定理揭示了ELBO的本质结构，指明了最优目标的理论下界。三种参数化坐标各有适用场景：去噪器参数化在均匀扩散中存在初始化发散问题，bridge plug-in参数化更为稳定。该框架为理解和改进离散扩散模型提供了统一的理论基础。

Abstract: What does a discrete diffusion model learn: a denoiser, a score ratio, or a bridge plug-in predictor? At the level of jump rates, these are one object in different coordinates, and reading a neural network in the wrong coordinate changes the process being trained and sampled. Starting with a rigorous derivation of the continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) ELBO for any noising process, boundary terms included, we prove the \emph{Oracle Distance} theorem: the negative ELBO is exactly equal to the data entropy plus the path KL from the oracle reverse process to the learned one, not merely a bound. Its unique optimizer is therefore the conditional expectation of the true reverse jump rate given the current noisy state, and its irreducible cost is the rate at which the forward process $Z_t$ destroys information about the clean data $Z_0$, $-\tfrac{d}{dt}I(Z_0; Z_t)$, so every noising process shares the same best achievable negative ELBO: the data entropy. For sequences with token-factorizing noise, the oracle projection yields three exact coordinates for the optimizer: denoiser, cavity (bridge plug-in), and score, with closed-form conversions among them. This framework identifies which law each loss in the literature actually optimizes, recovering MDM, UDM, SEDD, and GIDD as special cases; explains why denoiser and cavity coincide for masked diffusion but not for uniform diffusion; proves that a denoiser parameterization makes the uniform ELBO diverge at initialization while the bridge plug-in stays finite; and calibrates ELBO implementations exactly at initialization. Every identity is verified numerically, without approximation, on an exactly solvable model.

</details>


### [247] [RL-Ballast: Ship Ballast Water Path Planning and Clog Prediction via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04906)
*Ming-Kuan Lin,Yi-Chung Lai,Ming-Hsin Chiang,Tsung-Wei Pan,Jung-Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RL-Ballast框架，基于图深度强化学习实现压载水路径自适应规划和传感器节俭的堵塞候选评分，在有限感知条件下完成异常场景重路由并实现高准确率的故障定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在航运4.0范式下，自主和减员船舶需要智能内部系统维持运行安全。传统压载水控制方法对液压异常（阀门故障、管道堵塞）适应性有限，且依赖密集传感器进行诊断，存在局限。

Method: 将阀门排列问题转化为54条可行流体传输路线（图论+深度优先搜索）；使用帧堆叠水位和动作结果近似部分可观测环境；推理时采用失败动作记忆和动态动作掩码避免重复无效动作；累积失败传输历史对可疑阀门或管道段进行评分排序。

Result: Monte Carlo仿真显示：RL-Ballast完成所有单堵塞场景，决策步数从61.0降至41.5（对比Dijkstra规则基线）；故障历史评分方案实现100% Top-3命中率、66.7%严格Top-1命中率、83.3% Top-1平局命中率。

Conclusion: RL-Ballast在有限感知条件下实现了自适应重路由和维护导向的堵塞诊断，为自主船舶压载水系统提供了智能解决方案。

Abstract: Under the Shipping 4.0 paradigm, autonomous and reduced-crew vessels require intelligent internal systems to maintain operational safety and structural stability. Ballast-water control is essential for ship trim and integrity, but conventional rule-based or manual approaches have limited adaptability to hydraulic anomalies such as valve failures and pipe blockages, and often depend on dense pressure or flow sensors for diagnosis. To address these limitations, this paper proposes RL-Ballast, a graph-based deep reinforcement learning framework for adaptive ballast-water path planning and sensor-frugal blockage candidate scoring. The valve-permutation problem is transformed into 54 feasible fluid-transfer routes generated using graph theory and depth-first search. The partially observable ballast environment is approximated with frame-stacked tank levels and action outcomes, allowing the agent to infer hidden blockage effects without explicitly modeling a high-dimensional POMDP. During deterministic inference, episode-level failed-action memory and dynamic action masking prevent repeated ineffective actions and support immediate rerouting. Failed transfer histories are further accumulated to rank suspicious valves or pipe segments without dense instrumentation. Monte Carlo simulations show that RL-Ballast completes all unexpected single-blockage scenarios and reduces average decision steps from 61.0 to 41.5 compared with a Dijkstra rule-based baseline. For diagnostic support, the failure-history scoring scheme achieves a 100% Top-3 hit rate, a 66.7% strict Top-1 hit rate, and an 83.3% Top-1 tie-hit rate under serially indistinguishable blockage conditions. These results suggest that RL-Ballast enables adaptive rerouting and maintenance-oriented blockage diagnosis under limited sensing conditions.

</details>


### [248] [Weak-to-Strong Generalization via Direct On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05394)
*Shiyuan Feng,Huan-ang Gao,Haohan Chi,Hanlin Wu,Zhilong Zhang,Zheng Jiang,Bingxiang He,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Direct-OPD方法，通过将弱模型的强化学习策略偏移转化为隐式奖励信号，高效地将RL训练成果迁移至强模型，在Qwen3-1.7B上实现14.1%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习可验证奖励（RLVR）能有效提升语言模型推理能力，但随着模型规模扩大，在强模型上重复RL训练的计算成本高昂——目标模型在训练中需生成大量rollout，后训练本身成为瓶颈。现有弱到强的知识蒸馏方法仅模仿弱教师模型的最终策略，无法有效区分有用的RL增益与弱模型自身局限。

Method: 提出Direct On-Policy Distillation（Direct-OPD）：不直接蒸馏弱教师的最终策略，而是转移教师因RL产生的策略偏移。具体做法是比较弱模型RL前后的checkpoint，将其对数比率（log-ratio）作为密集隐式奖励信号应用于强学生模型的on-policy状态。该方法直接复用弱模型的RL监督信号，无需训练显式奖励模型或在目标模型上运行稀疏奖励RL。

Result: 在多个实验中验证了Direct-OPD能持续利用弱教师改善强目标模型；在Qwen3-1.7B上仅用8块A100 GPU训练4小时，AIME 2024基准性能从48.3%提升至62.4%；该方法优于步数匹配的直接RL训练，并支持多个策略偏移的序列组合。

Conclusion: RL训练成果可以作为隐式奖励信号跨模型规模复用，而非仅作为最终模型进行模仿，为后训练阶段的高效模型改进提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is a powerful recipe for improving language-model reasoning, but it is expensive to repeat on every new strong model because the target model must generate many rollouts during training. As models scale, post-training itself becomes a bottleneck. We study a weak-to-strong alternative: run RL on a smaller model where rollouts are cheaper, then reuse what that RL run learned to improve a stronger target model. Directly distilling the post-RL weak teacher is not enough, because the teacher's final policy mixes useful RL gains with the limitations of the smaller model. We propose Direct On-Policy Distillation (Direct-OPD), which transfers the teacher's RL-induced policy shift instead. Direct-OPD compares the post-RL teacher with its own pre-RL reference and treats their log-ratio as a dense implicit reward for the student. In plain terms, the checkpoint pair tells us which actions RL made the weak model more or less likely to take, and Direct-OPD applies that signal on the stronger student's own on-policy states. This directly reuses the weak model's RL supervision signal without training an explicit reward model or running sparse-reward RL on the target model. Empirically, Direct-OPD consistently leverages weaker teachers to improve stronger target models; notably, it boosts Qwen3-1.7B from 48.3% to 62.4% on AIME 2024 in just 4 hours on 8 A100 GPUs. It outperforms step-matched direct RL and enables the sequential composition of multiple policy shifts. Our results show that RL outcomes can be reused across model scales as implicit reward signals, not merely as final models to imitate.

</details>


### [249] [When Do Foundation Models Pay Off? A Break-Even Analysis of Pretrained Time Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04919)
*Nicholas Tan Jerome,Frank Simon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统分析了时间序列基础模型的投入产出比，发现在30个数据集中有15个基础模型无条件优于传统方法，并提出了一套无需训练即可快速决策的部署框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署时间序列基础模型需要GPU基础设施和工程开销，且不能保证优于XGBoost等传统方法。目前缺乏系统性的盈亏平衡分析来指导实践者何时应该投资基础模型。

Method: 在30个基准数据集上，比较零样本和LoRA微调的基础模型（Chronos、Moirai、Lag-Llama）与经典基线方法（Naive、ETS、ARIMA、XGBoost），测试6种训练集规模（从2%到100%）。

Result: 基础模型在15/30数据集上无条件优于传统方法；在6个数据集上，传统方法仅需2%训练数据（21-2,768样本）即可超越零样本基础模型；其余9个数据集的盈亏平衡点为24-8,361样本。提出了稳健部署规则：当n_train < 700且季节性显著时，直接使用零样本FM而跳过微调，可解决10/30的部署决策。

Conclusion: 提出了两步决策框架：先计算数据集长度和季节性强度，仅在必要时进行5-10%试点实验，使实践者能在投入全部基础设施前做出FM与传统方法的选择决策。发现LoRA微调可能降低短序列性能。代码和决策工具已开源。

Abstract: Deploying a time series foundation model requires GPU infrastructure, engineering overhead, and carries no guarantee of improvement over XGBoost. We provide the first systematic break-even analysis answering when this investment pays off. Across 30 benchmark datasets, we compare zero-shot and LoRA fine-tuned foundation models (Chronos, Moirai, Lag-Llama) against classical baselines (Naive, ETS, ARIMA, XGBoost) at six training set sizes from 2% to 100% of available data. Foundation models outperform classical methods at every evaluated training fraction on 15 of 30 datasets -- GPU deployment is unconditionally justified on these regardless of data volume. On 6 datasets, classical methods surpass zero-shot foundation models with as little as 2% of training data (21-2,768 samples); on the remaining 9, break-even ranges from 24 to 8,361 samples. One robust deployment rule requires no model training: if n_train < 700 and seasonality is non-negligible, use FM zero-shot and skip fine-tuning -- this resolves 10 of 30 deployment decisions immediately. Contrary to common practice, LoRA fine-tuning can actively degrade performance on short series. We operationalise these findings as a two-step decision framework -- compute dataset length and seasonality strength, run a brief 5-10% pilot only if needed -- enabling practitioners to make the FM-versus-classical decision before committing to full infrastructure. Four dataset features motivate mechanistic hypotheses for the remaining cases, though reliable automated prediction at this benchmark scale remains an open problem. Code, benchmark, and decision tools are available at https://github.com/nicolaisi/fm-breakeven.

</details>


### [250] [Lightweight ML-Based Automatic Sleep Staging Framework with Constrained CNN and Mamba for Small-Sample EEG Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04934)
*Zihao Wei,Yulin Gong,Yudan Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GamSleepNet，这是一个用于单通道EEG的轻量级、低延迟自动睡眠分期框架。该模型仅用30.86k参数就在Sleepedf数据集上实现了87.86%的整体准确率，达到SOTA水平，并显著提升了N1和REM等困难阶段的识别精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动睡眠分期是睡眠障碍精准诊断、治疗及长期家庭睡眠监测的关键技术。便携式EEG设备因数据采集便捷而成为研究焦点，但现有方法面临三大挑战：大参数规模易在小数据集上过拟合、N1和REM等困难阶段分类准确率低、最佳训练数据集规模不明确以及部署困难。

Method: 提出GamSleepNet框架，包含以下核心组件：1）FEB模块，结合改进的Gabor核与可学习滤波器进行特征提取；2）基于Mamba架构构建时序分类网络；3）引入新颖的对比损失函数；4）采用两阶段训练策略；5）实验验证了单通道EEG睡眠分期模型的最佳数据集规模。

Result: 在Sleepedf数据集上，模型仅用30.86k参数实现了87.86%的整体准确率，所有指标均达到SOTA水平，显著提升了困难睡眠阶段（N1和REM）的识别准确率，成功解决了参数规模与性能之间的平衡问题。

Conclusion: GamSleepNet成功解决了便携式EEG设备自动睡眠分期中的关键问题，实现了轻量化与高精度的统一，为小数据集训练和实际部署提供了可行方案，并明确了最佳训练数据集规模，具有重要的临床应用价值和实际部署意义。

Abstract: Automatic sleep staging is a key technology for precise diagnosis and treatment of sleep disorders as well as long-term home sleep monitoring. Portable electroencephalogram (EEG) devices have become the focus of research due to their convenience in data collection. However, current methods still face three major challenges: large parameter sizes that easily lead to overfitting on small datasets, low accuracy in classifying difficult stages such as N1 and REM, unclear optimal training dataset size, and difficulty in deployment. This paper proposes GamSleepNet, a lightweight and low-latency automatic sleep staging framework for single-channel EEG. The framework features the FEB module, which combines improved Gabor kernels with learnable filters for feature extraction, uses the Mamba architecture to build a temporal classification network, introduces a novel contrastive loss and a two-stage training strategy, and experimentally validates the optimal dataset size for single-channel EEG sleep staging models. On the Sleepedf dataset, this model achieves an overall accuracy of 87.86 percent with only 30.86 thousand parameters, with all metrics reaching SOTA levels and significantly improving the identification accuracy of challenging sleep stages.

</details>


### [251] [Sensitivity Sampling with Predictions for k-Means Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04949)
*Cristian Boldrin,Fabio Vandin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用预测来近似敏感度值，从而加速大规模数据集的k-means聚类coresets构建过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于敏感度采样的coresets方法虽然提供了理论质量保证，但由于需要计算每个输入点相对于整个数据集的重要性，计算成本过高，在大规模数据集上难以实际应用。

Method: 首先从理论上证明coresets构建对敏感度近似精度要求可放宽，允许使用较粗糙的近似值；然后针对同一问题在时间序列上进行聚类的场景，提出利用前序数据集的最优中心作为后续数据集敏感度采样的预测器。

Result: 证明了当序列数据集来自同一分布时，在一个数据集上产生低误差的中心可用于后续数据集的敏感度采样并保证质量；实验表明，在聚类代价与运行时间的权衡上，该方法显著优于均匀采样和现有最先进的敏感度采样方法。

Conclusion: 基于预测的敏感度近似方法能够在保持聚类质量的同时显著降低计算开销，为大规模数据集上的k-means聚类提供了一种实用且理论有保证的高效解决方案。

Abstract: We study the problem of k-means clustering on large datasets. The state-of-the-art for the problem is given by coresets-based approaches, which build small weighted summaries of the input and derive approximate solutions with rigorous quality guarantees from them. One of the most popular and advanced approaches to derive coresets for k-means is sensitivity sampling. However, sensitivity sampling requires to compute the importance of each input point with respect to the whole dataset over all possible choices of centers. Since the exact computation of such quantities is unfeasible, current approaches work by approximating the sensitivity values. Nevertheless, the runtime of such approaches is still impractical for large datasets.
  In this work, we propose to reduce the runtime of sensitivity-based approaches for k-means by leveraging predictions to approximate the importance of input points. We first formally prove that current theoretical results on coresets construction via sensitivity sampling hold for coarser approximations of sensitivities compared to the one required by existing approaches. This implies that even fairly noisy predictors can be leveraged for sensitivity-sampling approaches. We then propose a natural predictor, which applies to the common scenario where clustering is performed (over time) on a sequence of datasets from the same problem. We prove that when the datasets in the sequence come from the same (unknown) distribution, centers resulting in a low error on one dataset can be used as predictions for sensitivity sampling in subsequent datasets, with guarantees on their quality. We perform an extensive experimental evaluation showing that our approach significantly improves, in terms of clustering cost vs runtime, over uniform sampling and state-of-the-art sensitivity sampling approaches when applied to sequences of datasets.

</details>


### [252] [Train Smarter, Not Longer: Memorization-Guided Data Reuse for Efficient LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04969)
*Jingwei Zuo,Cong Zeng,Ilyas Chahed,Maksim Velikanov,Dhia Eddine Rhaiem,Pasquale Balsebre,Abhay Kumar,Younes Belkada,Hakim Hacid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“记忆指导的数据复用”训练范式，通过观察模型的“记忆窗口”信号，自适应地决定数据复用的时机与方式，初步实验表明在远超当前惯例（如4轮限制）的重复次数下性能仍持续提升，为LLM的训练调度提供了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着高质量数据有限，LLM训练范式从单轮训练转向多轮训练，但过度重复会导致过拟合与收益递减。如何有效确定数据复用的时机与次数成为亟待解决但尚未充分探索的问题。

Method: 提出“记忆指导的数据复用”范式，通过损失保持动态和下游评估得分推导出的“记忆窗口”信号，自适应地决定何时、如何复用数据，从而有原则地确定训练轮次并调度数据回放。

Result: 初步实验揭示了稳定的记忆驱动规律：性能在远超当前惯例（如常用的4轮限制）的重复次数下仍持续提升，证明该方法的潜力。

Conclusion: 本方法为记忆感知的训练调度奠定基础，有助于确定复用预算、实现更智能而非更长的LLM训练，完整调度器设计留作未来工作。

Abstract: The training paradigm of large language models has shifted from traditional one-pass training to multi-epoch training, as reasonable reuse of limited high-quality data can improve both model performance and sample efficiency. Meanwhile, excessive repetition introduces the risk of overfitting and diminishing returns. Determining when and how to reuse data effectively thus emerges as a natural but under-explored question. Through a novel observation of model's "Memorization Window" signals derived from loss retention dynamics and downstream evaluation scores, we propose "Memorization-guided Data Reuse", a training paradigm that adaptively determines when and how data should be reused, enabling principled decisions on the number of training epochs and the scheduling of data replays. Our preliminary experiments reveal a consistent memorization-driven regime: performance continues to improve with repetition far beyond current practice (e.g., the commonly cited four-epoch limit). While a full scheduler remains future work, these insights provide a foundation for memorization-aware training schedules, helping to determine reuse budgets and move toward training LLMs smarter rather than longer with limited high-quality data.

</details>


### [253] [Geometry-Aware Bayesian Quantification via Compositional Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04977)
*Alejandro Moreo,Pablo González,Juan José del Coz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Aitchison几何和收缩正则化的几何感知KDE方法用于多类别标签分布估计，在多个数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于核密度估计（KDE）的量化方法通常依赖欧几里得高斯核，忽略了概率单纯形的几何结构，且会错误地将概率质量分配到单纯形边界之外，从而影响标签偏移场景下目标标签分布的准确估计。

Method: 提出了一种几何感知的KDE模型，利用对数比表示和Aitchison几何来正确处理单纯形几何；引入收缩正则化以提升边界附近的鲁棒性；结合最大似然解释，推导了类别流行度的点估计和贝叶斯推断过程。

Result: 在涵盖表格、文本和图像领域的42个数据集上的实验表明，该方法与现有最优量化方法具有竞争力，在标准KDE基线上有显著提升，在贝叶斯量化方法中也表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过正确建模概率单纯形的几何特性，所提出的几何感知KDE方法能够更准确地估计目标标签分布，为标签偏移适应提供了更有效的量化工具。

Abstract: Accurately estimating the unknown target label distribution is the critical first step for adapting to label shift. This task, widely known as quantification or class prevalence estimation, has recently seen significant advances through continuous KDE-based methods which model the density of multiclass classifier posteriors. Posterior vectors might be regarded as compositional data, since they lie on the probability simplex. However, existing KDE-based quantifiers typically rely on Euclidean Gaussian kernels, which ignore simplex geometry and incorrectly assign probability mass outside its boundaries. We introduce a geometry-aware KDE model for multiclass quantification based on log-ratio representations and Aitchison geometry, together with a shrinkage regularization that improves robustness near the simplex boundary. Combined with a maximum-likelihood interpretation of KDE-based quantification, we derive both point-estimation and Bayesian inference procedures for class prevalences. Experiments on 42 datasets across tabular, text, and image domains show that the proposed method is competitive with state-of-the-art quantifiers, often improving over standard KDE-based baselines, while also yielding strong results among Bayesian quantification methods.

</details>


### [254] [Qantara: Bridge-Flow Training for Multi-Paradigm JEPA Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04978)
*Ruslan Rakhimov,George Bredis,Yuriy Maksyuta,Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Qantara是首个多范式JEPA世界模型，单一检查点支持三种推理方式（潜在规划、行为克隆、逆动力学），无需重训练即可根据部署约束灵活选择，在控制任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA世界模型在训练时就固定了单一推理范式（轨迹优化或行为克隆），无法适应不同部署场景下的约束变化（如推演成本、观测可及性）。需要一种方法推迟范式选择到推理阶段，根据实际约束动态决定最优策略。

Method: 提出Qantara，采用联合训练目标：状态轴上连续干净潜变量之间的Brownian-bridge插值，动作轴上的噪声到数据流匹配。训练集中在(动作时间,状态时间)噪声方块的边缘，推理时可从同一权重出发查询三种范式：潜在规划、行为克隆动作采样，以及通过视频逆组合（先预测下一潜变量再提取动作）实现的逆动力学。

Result: 在Push-T任务中，潜在规划达到90.1 SR；将边缘采样替换为均匀内部采样导致性能下降至53.3 SR。在LeWM控制套件上达到91.2 SR的三种子平均；在OGBench-Cube上比DINO-WM高7.7 SR，比LeWM高19.7 SR。行为克隆和视频逆路径分别达到82-83 SR（Push-T）和71-73 SR（Cube）。

Conclusion: Qantara成功将JEPA世界模型从单范式规划器扩展为多范式控制器，证明了通过精心设计的训练目标，单一模型检查点可以同时支持多种推理范式，为实际部署提供了更大的灵活性。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) underpin a growing family of latent world models for control from raw pixels, but every existing JEPA world model commits at training time to a single inference paradigm: either trajectory optimisation in a learned dynamics model, or direct behaviour cloning. A single checkpoint that serves both would defer this choice to inference, when deployment constraints (rollout cost, observation accessibility) determine which path wins. We present Qantara, an end-to-end JEPA whose joint training objective pairs a Brownian-bridge interpolant between consecutive clean latents on the state axis with noise-to-data flow matching on the action axis. The same checkpoint serves three inference paradigms without retraining: latent planning, behaviour-cloning action sampling, and inverse dynamics, which we query through a video-inverse composition that first predicts the next latent without action conditioning, then extracts the action. Training concentrates mass on the edges of the (action-time, state-time) noise square, where inference queries the predictor: replacing it with uniform interior sampling drops Push-T planning from 90.1 to 53.3 SR at matched compute. On the LeWM control suite, Qantara reaches a 91.2 SR three-train-seed average and sets new SOTA on OGBench-Cube (+7.7 SR over DINO-WM, +19.7 over LeWM). From the same weights, the behaviour-cloning and video-inverse paths reach 82-83 SR on Push-T and 71-73 SR on Cube. These results move JEPA world models from single-paradigm planners to multi-paradigm controllers.

</details>


### [255] [Data-Driven Soft Labeling Scales DNA Read Classification to Whole-Body Cell-Type Deconvolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04987)
*Dmytro Rizdvanetskyi,Nathan Ross,Pavlo Lutsik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Syto框架，通过数据驱动的软标签策略解决读级别细胞类型反卷积中的可扩展性问题，在39种人体细胞类型上将MSE降低2.56倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于DNA甲基化的细胞类型反卷积方法主要依赖聚合的甲基化估计，忽略了单个DNA读段携带的模式级信息；现有的读级别方法稀缺且仅限于少类别场景，难以扩展，因为在规模扩大时，非判别性读段占主导且硬标签与甲基化模式到细胞类型的多对多映射冲突，导致分类器无法收敛。

Method: 提出数据驱动的软标签策略，估计每个读段的条件细胞类型分布，并将其集成到Syto模块化框架中，实现基于读级别分类的反卷积。

Result: 在包含39种人体细胞类型的全图谱上，Syto将MSE相比SOTA降低2.56倍，且在覆盖16种组织的分布外数据集上保持优势。

Conclusion: Syto为建模更大规模的细胞类型面板奠定了基础，在生物学和医疗健康领域具有更优的应用前景；所提软标签方案可推广至任何信号-标签多对多映射的场景。

Abstract: Cell-type deconvolution, the task of estimating the proportions of constituent cell types in a heterogeneous biological sample, is a core problem in computational biology. Methods that rely on epigenetic marks such as DNA methylation typically operate on aggregated methylation estimates, discarding the pattern-level information carried by individual DNA reads. Existing read-level approaches that exploit this information are scarce, and all remain restricted to few-class settings; scaling them further is an open problem because, at scale, non-discriminative reads dominate and hard labels conflict with the many-to-many mapping between methylation patterns and cell types, preventing classifier convergence. To overcome this, we propose data-driven soft labels that estimate the conditional cell-type distribution for each read, and integrate this scheme into Syto, a new modular framework for read-level classification-based deconvolution. On a whole-body atlas of 39 human cell types, Syto reduces MSE by 2.56$\times$ over SoTA, with gains transferring to an out-of-distribution dataset spanning 16 tissues. Syto lays the foundation for modeling increasingly large cell-type panels, with improved applications in biology and healthcare. The proposed soft-labeling scheme is further translatable to any setting with a many-to-many signal-to-label mapping.

</details>


### [256] [Non-Convex Sparse Reinforcement Learning via Non-Monotone Inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04990)
*Kyohei Suzuki,onstantinos Slavakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合PMC惩罚的LSTD方法用于强化学习特征选择，并发展了FRBS算法在非单调包含问题上的收敛理论，数值实验表明该方法显著优于现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统正则化方法在强化学习特征选择中存在估计偏差问题，现有的LSTD方法需要改进以实现更高效的特征选择，同时需要解决由此产生的非单调包含问题的理论挑战。

Method: 在经典LSTD策略评估基础上引入稀疏诱导的非凸PMC惩罚；将问题建模为非单调包含问题（单调Lipschitz算子与亚单调算子之和）；发展forward-reflected-backward splitting (FRBS)方法用于此类非单调包含问题，建立Lyapunov稳定性条件和收敛性保证。

Result: 在温和条件下证明了FRBS迭代序列的Lyapunov稳定性和极限点存在性；在弱Minty变分不等式假设下保证了精确收敛；基准数据集上的数值实验表明，所提方法在存在大量噪声特征时显著优于现有特征选择方法。

Conclusion: 成功将PMC惩罚与LSTD结合解决了估计偏差问题，同时发展了FRBS在非单调包含问题上的收敛理论，为强化学习中的高效特征选择提供了理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: This work delivers two key contributions: one to efficient feature selection in reinforcement learning
  (RL), the other to the theory of non-monotone inclusions. On the RL side, the estimation bias inherent
  in conventional regularization schemes is addressed by augmenting classical least-squares
  temporal-difference (LSTD) policy evaluation with the sparsity-inducing, non-convex projected minimax
  concave (PMC) penalty. Because the PMC penalty is weakly convex, the resulting fixed-point problem is
  no longer monotone; instead, it falls under a broader class of non-monotone inclusions involving the
  sum of a monotone Lipschitz operator and a hypomonotone operator. On the theory side, novel convergence
  conditions are developed for the forward-reflected-backward splitting (FRBS) method applied to this
  broader class of non-monotone inclusion problems. Under mild conditions, Lyapunov stability and the
  existence of a limit point of the sequence of FRBS iterates are established; alternatively, under the
  weak Minty variational inequality assumption, exact convergence is guaranteed. Numerical tests on
  benchmark datasets show that the proposed FRBS iterates, applied to the non-convexly regularized LSTD
  problem, substantially outperform state-of-the-art feature-selection methods, especially when many
  noisy features are present.

</details>


### [257] [Beyond Modality Fusion: Deep Ensembles for Multimodal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05019)
*Ilya Burenko,Dmitry Vetrov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 深度集成模型在多模态分类中优于晚融合方法，无需显式模态融合即可有效分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有晚融合方法在模态不平衡情况下性能受限，需要复杂的正则化技术来平衡学习过程。本文旨在探索是否可以通过简单的深度集成策略替代复杂的融合方法来解决模态不平衡问题。

Method: 提出使用单模态网络的深度集成模型进行多模态分类，无需显式模态融合；设计选择每个模态模型数量的启发式方法；构建可控的合成多模态框架进行系统分析；使用缩放定律预测渐近性能。

Result: 在相同参数量下，深度集成模型一致性地优于现有晚融合方法；该优势在中间融合和混合方法上同样成立；提出了有效的模态选择策略：极端不平衡和小集成规模下优先使用强模态模型，规模扩大后引入弱模态模型有益；合成和真实数据集上结果一致。

Conclusion: 深度集成模型是多模态分类的有效方法，无需复杂的融合机制即可处理模态不平衡问题；为多模态模型优化提供了新的视角和实用指导。

Abstract: In multimodal classification, late-fusion approaches classify concatenated modality-specific features extracted by unimodal neural networks.
  When modality imbalance is pronounced, various regularization techniques have been proposed to balance the learning process and overcome the inferior performance of late-fusion networks.
  In contrast, this work demonstrates that multimodal data can be effectively classified without any explicit modality fusion, using deep ensembles of unimodal networks.
  We systematically compare deep ensembles to late-fusion networks at equal parameter count and show that ensembles consistently outperform state-of-the-art late-fusion methods designed to address modality imbalance.
  This advantage also holds over intermediate-fusion techniques we evaluated and over hybrid methods that combine unimodal and multimodal predictions.
  We propose and empirically validate a method for selecting the number of models per modality in an ensemble, avoiding computationally expensive exhaustive search.
  Under extreme modality imbalance and small ensemble sizes, the heuristic indicates that ensembles of unimodal models trained solely on the stronger modality are preferable; as the ensemble scales up, incorporating models from the weaker modality becomes beneficial.
  Both predictions align with our empirical findings.
  To systematically explore the challenges of optimizing multimodal models, we propose a synthetic multimodal framework that allows control over both the number of modalities and their predictive strength; our findings are consistent across synthetic and real-world datasets.
  Finally, by fitting scaling laws to bimodal datasets, we estimate the asymptotic performance of ensembles.

</details>


### [258] [Uncertainty-aware damage identification in short-span bridges via physics-informed variational autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05025)
*Ana Fernandez Navamuel,A. Javier Omella,Diego Zamora-Sanchez,David Pardo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息高斯Copula变分自编码器（PI-GCVAE）框架，用于土木基础设施的振动损伤识别，通过嵌入可微特征值求解器和采用Gaussian Copula建模潜在变量，实现了可靠的不确定性量化，在桥梁结构健康监测中达到77.2%的覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于振动的土木基础设施损伤识别是一个具有挑战性的不适定逆问题，面临测量噪声、稀疏传感器阵列和环境变异性等困难。现有深度学习方法缺乏可靠的不确定性量化能力，可能产生物理不一致的结果，因此需要开发能够融合物理约束并提供可靠不确定性估计的方法。

Method: 提出物理信息高斯Copula变分自编码器（PI-GCVAE）框架：1）将可微数值特征值求解器直接嵌入VAE架构中，消除对数据驱动代理模型的需求，确保潜在空间样本满足结构动力学控制方程；2）用Gaussian Copula替代传统的潜在变量独立性假设，捕捉相邻结构单元之间复杂的物理依赖空间互相关性，同时考虑系统固有变异性和测量误差；3）相比高斯混合模型等替代方案，基于Copula的VAE为高维、强相关的潜在空间提供了高效的分布模型。

Result: 使用承受各种损伤场景并受随机高斯噪声污染的简支桥梁合成数据集进行验证。结果表明PI-GCVAE能够准确恢复真实后验分布，达到77.2%的覆盖率，有效捕捉了潜在变量之间的复杂相关性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为运营桥梁的早期损伤诊断提供了一个可靠、可扩展的工具，成功解决了传统方法在不确定性量化方面的不足，为土木基础设施的结构健康监测提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Vibration-based damage identification in civil infrastructure is a challenging, ill-posed inverse problem due to measurement noise, sparse sensor arrays, and environmental variability. While deep learning is powerful for system identification, deterministic approaches lack reliable uncertainty quantification and can yield physically inconsistent results. This work proposes a robust probabilistic Scientific Machine Learning (SciML) framework: a physics-informed Gaussian copula variational autoencoder (PI-GCVAE) for structural health monitoring (SHM). First, we eliminate the need for data-driven surrogates by embedding a differentiable numerical eigenvalue solver directly into the VAE architecture. This ensures that latent space samples satisfy the governing equations of structural dynamics, reducing the trainable parameter space and improving generalization. Second, we replace the conventional independence assumption of latent variables with a Gaussian copula. This model captures complex, physics-dependent spatial cross-correlations between adjacent structural elements, defining feasible solutions while accounting for inherent system variability and measurement errors. Third, compared with alternatives such as Gaussian mixtures, our copula-based VAE provides an efficient distributional model for high-dimensional, strongly correlated latent spaces. We validate the approach using a synthetic dataset of a simply supported bridge subjected to various damage scenarios and corrupted with stochastic Gaussian noise. Synthetic data enables exhaustive validation against ground-truth stiffness values unavailable in practice. Results demonstrate that the PI-GCVAE accurately recovers the true posterior distribution, achieving 77.2% coverage. The proposed framework provides a reliable, scalable tool for early-stage damage diagnosis in operating bridges.

</details>


### [259] [CollabEval: Statistically Efficient Collaborative Model Evaluation via Matrix Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05046)
*Adam Fisch,Daniel Deutsch,Joshua Maynez,Alekh Agarwal,Jonathan Berant,William Cohen,Amir Globerson,Jacob Eisenstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CollabEval方法，利用不同模型在同一任务上的历史评估结果之间的依赖关系，通过矩阵补全和控制变量技术，在保证无偏估计和有效置信区间的前提下，显著降低生成式AI模型评估的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI模型的评估过程是常规但资源密集的，在模型开发过程中需要反复进行。现有的评估方法效率低下，每次评估都需要大量标注工作，因此需要一种能够提高统计效率、减少标注成本的方法。

Method: 将模型评估建模为M×N评估分数矩阵的矩阵补全问题（M为模型数量，N为评估提示数量）。对目标模型仅在少量比例p的提示上进行标注，利用预测驱动推断的近期成果构建分数矩阵的低秩近似，并将重构值作为控制变量，从而保证真实评估指标均值的无偏估计和统计有效的置信区间。

Result: 在多种数据集、模型和稀疏度水平p下，CollabEval在相同标注预算下，相比基线方法显著降低了平均置信区间大小和点估计的均方误差。

Conclusion: CollabEval是一种简单、有效且有理论依据的方法，能够通过利用历史评估结果之间的依赖关系大幅提高生成式AI模型评估的统计效率，为模型开发过程中的资源优化提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Evaluating generative AI models is a routine, but resource-intensive, process that is conducted over and over again during the course of model development. In this work, we propose Collaborative Evaluation (CollabEval), a simple, effective, and principled method for exploiting dependencies between historical runs of different models on the same tasks to improve statistical efficiency. Specifically, our approach treats model evaluation as a matrix completion problem over an $M \times N$ matrix of evaluation scores, where $M$ is the total number of models and $N$ is the total number of evaluation prompts. We assume that a subset of these $M$ models are targeted for evaluation. For these target models only a small fraction, $p$, of prompts has been annotated with evaluation scores. Leveraging recent results in prediction-powered inference, we build a low-rank approximation of the score matrix, and use the reconstructed values as control variates in a manner that guarantees unbiased estimates of the true evaluation metric mean, in addition to statistically valid confidence intervals. Empirically, across a wide range of datasets, models, and sparsity levels $p$, we find that CollabEval substantially reduces the mean confidence interval size, and the mean squared error of the point estimate, compared to baseline methods at the same annotation budget.

</details>


### [260] [KVpop -- Key-Value Cache Compression with Predictive Online Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05061)
*Lukas Hauzenberger,Niklas Schmidinger,Anamaria-Roberta Hartl,David Stap,Thomas Schmied,Sebastian Böck,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KVpop方法，通过监督学习实现固定预算的KV缓存淘汰，在数学推理任务上以75%-88%的压缩率保持97%-98%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归解码中KV缓存随上下文长度线性增长，导致内存和带宽瓶颈。现有KV淘汰方法依赖静态启发式或代理分数，难以追踪未来token效用，在相关性变化时导致不稳定的淘汰决策。

Method: 1. 引入KVpop，通过直接监督保留或淘汰决策来学习固定预算的KV淘汰策略；
2. 训练评分器使用新颖的未来注意力目标，无需物化密集注意力图即可高效计算；
3. 提出延迟记忆评分器，推迟固定步数进行评分以利用近期未来上下文，这是学习型淘汰方法中的独特设计。

Result: 在AIME和HMMT数学推理任务上：Qwen3-4B在75% KV缓存压缩时保持98%的全注意力性能，在88%压缩时保持97%性能；Qwen3-8B表现更强，接近完全教师性能。一致优于现有淘汰基线方法。

Conclusion: 使用未来注意力信号监督淘汰决策，能够在保持模型质量的同时显著降低内存成本，为解决KV缓存增长瓶颈提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) cache growth is a major bottleneck in autoregressive decoding, as memory and bandwidth scale linearly with context length. Existing KV eviction methods often rely on static heuristics or proxy scores, which poorly track future token utility and cause brittle eviction as relevance shifts. To address this, we introduce KVpop, which learns a fixed-budget KV eviction policy by directly supervising the keep-or-drop decision. The scorer is trained against a novel future-attention target, computed efficiently without materializing dense attention maps. We further introduce a delayed memory-based scorer that, uniquely among learned eviction methods, defers scoring for a fixed number of steps to exploit near-future context. On AIME and HMMT mathematical reasoning, KVpop retains 98% of full-attention performance on Qwen3-4B at 75% KV cache compression and 97% at 88% compression, consistently outperforming established eviction baselines. Qwen3-8B shows even stronger results, reaching near-full teacher performance. These results show that supervising eviction with future-attention signals cuts memory costs while maintaining quality.

</details>


### [261] [Computing Monetary Risk Measures in Linear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05078)
*Palash Agrawal,Gersi Doko,Maeve Burwell,Marek Petrik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了QuickVaR和QuickDivergence两种新算法，可在期望线性时间内计算离散随机变量的VaR和φ-散度风险度量（包括CVaR），实现了数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VaR和CVaR等货币风险度量在表达决策者风险厌恶方面应用广泛，但需要高效的计算方法。现有方法在处理大定义域的离散随机变量时计算效率有待提升，因此需要开发更快速的算法。

Method: 提出QuickVaR算法，改编经典的Quickselect算法用于计算离散随机变量的VaR；在此基础上构建QuickDivergence算法，利用多面体优化算法计算包括CVaR在内的φ-散度风险度量类。

Result: 数值实验表明，新算法对大定义域问题可提供数量级的加速。算法的库实现已在GitHub上公开。

Conclusion: 所提出的期望线性时间算法显著提升了离散随机变量风险度量的计算效率，为大规模风险管理应用提供了有效的计算工具。

Abstract: Monetary risk measures have gained popularity for expressing decision-makers' risk aversion. Value-at-Risk (VaR) and Conditional-Value-at-Risk (CVaR), in particular, are used commonly for this purpose. This paper proposes new efficient algorithms to compute these risk measures for a discrete random variable in expected linear time with respect to the size of its domain. First, we propose a QuickVaR algorithm that computes the VaR of a discrete random variable. Then, we leverage QuickVaR to propose QuickDivergence, an algorithm for computing a class of $\varphi$-divergence risk measures, including the popular CVaR risk measure. The QuickVaR algorithm adapts the well-known Quickselect algorithm, while QuickDivergence builds on polymatroid optimization algorithms. Numerical results show that our new algorithms offer an order-of-magnitude speedup for large domains, and a library implementation of the algorithms is available at https://github.com/RiskAverseRL/RiskMeasures.jl.

</details>


### [262] [FAST: A Holistic Framework for Optimizing Memory-I/O, Computation, and Sampling in Temporal GNN Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05095)
*Yushu Cai,Qingrui Zhu,Lei Liu,Kai Sheng,Hao Chen,Xin He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FAST框架，通过联合优化采样、内存I/O和计算三个瓶颈，实现大规模时序图神经网络训练的端到端加速，平均获得2.1倍加速（最高4.7倍），且不损失模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序图神经网络（TGNN）在大规模动态图上的训练面临内存I/O、不规则计算和时序邻居采样三个相互关联的性能瓶颈。现有系统通常孤立地优化这些阶段，导致存在显著的性能优化空间。

Method: FAST框架采用三方面联合优化：（1）SlimCache：利用批次内压缩和跨批次缓存技术，在有限的GPU内存预算下减少主机-设备间数据传输；（2）线程高效的图算子：针对稀疏时序子图设计，改善GPU缓存局部性，降低聚合和边softmax延迟；（3）拓扑感知采样策略：提高CPU缓存局部性，加速时序邻居采样过程。

Result: 在真实世界大规模动态图上的广泛实验表明，FAST相比最先进系统平均实现2.1倍加速，最高达到4.7倍加速，同时不牺牲模型精度。

Conclusion: 通过整体优化采样、内存I/O和计算三个关键瓶颈，FAST框架成功加速了端到端TGNN训练过程，证明了联合优化策略在大规模动态图学习中的有效性，为推荐系统、社交网络分析和交通预测等应用提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Temporal Graph Neural Networks (TGNNs) are widely used for learning from dynamic graphs in applications such as recommendation, social network analysis, and traffic forecasting. However, scaling TGNN training to large dynamic graphs remains challenging due to three intertwined bottlenecks: memory I/O, irregular computation, and temporal neighbor sampling. Existing systems often optimize these stages in isolation, leaving substantial performance headroom on the table. We present FAST, a holistic framework that accelerates end-to-end TGNN training by jointly optimizing sampling, memory I/O, and computation. FAST introduces SlimCache, which exploits within-batch compression and cross-batch caching to reduce host-device data movement under limited GPU memory budgets. It further designs thread-efficient graph operators tailored to sparse temporal subgraphs, improving GPU cache locality and reducing the latency of aggregation and edge softmax. In addition, FAST employs a topology-aware sampling strategy that improves CPU cache locality and accelerates temporal neighbor sampling. Extensive experiments on real-world large dynamic graphs show that FAST achieves an average of 2.1x (up to 4.7x) speedup over state-of-the-art systems without sacrificing model accuracy.

</details>


### [263] [Functional Bilevel Optimization for Predictive Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05098)
*Ieva Petrulionyte,Julien Mairal,Michael Arbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对连续高维敏感属性场景，提出DPVar（条件均值预测方差）优化方法，通过FBO和ITD两种算法直接优化公平性-准确性权衡，在实验中显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的公平性研究主要集中在离散敏感属性，而当敏感属性为连续高维数据（如人口统计评分向量、属性后验分布、年龄或收入曲线）时，强制完全统计独立过于严格。现有放松方法依赖间接依赖惩罚或对抗方案，无法直接针对公平性-准确性权衡进行优化。

Method: 提出均值人口统计平等方法，使用DPVar（敏感属性条件下的条件均值预测方差）作为优化目标，将问题转化为函数双层优化问题。开发了两种算法：1）FBO算法：针对平方损失情况推导闭式伴随公式，获得精确超梯度；2）ITD算法：通过展开内部步骤进行微分，可扩展到非平方损失情况。

Result: 在合成数据和基于60个表格回归数据集构建的新型半合成基准测试上，两种方法均达到最低或接近最低的公平性-准确性总体遗憾值，并持续匹配或优于强HSIC、对抗、线性依赖和广义DP基线方法。

Conclusion: DPVar方法为连续高维敏感属性场景提供了有效的公平性优化解决方案，所提出的FBO和ITD算法能够直接优化公平性-准确性权衡，在实验验证中表现出优越性能，填补了现有方法在处理连续高维敏感属性时的不足。

Abstract: When sensitive attributes are continuous and high-dimensional $-$ demographic score vectors, posteriors over attributes, age or income profiles $-$ enforcing full statistical independence is often too restrictive, and existing relaxations rely on indirect dependence penalties or adversarial schemes that do not directly target the fairness-accuracy trade-off. We instead consider mean demographic parity through DPVar, the variance of the conditional-mean prediction given the sensitive attribute, and show that optimizing it yields a functional bilevel problem. We propose two algorithms for this problem: FBO, which uses a closed-form adjoint we derive for the squared-loss case to obtain an exact hypergradient, and ITD, which differentiates through unrolled inner steps and extends beyond squared loss. On synthetic data and a new semi-synthetic benchmark built from 60 tabular regression datasets, both methods achieve the lowest or near-lowest aggregate fairness-accuracy regret, and consistently match or outperform strong HSIC, adversarial, linear-dependence, and generalized-DP baselines.

</details>


### [264] [Counterfactual Methods for Detecting Unfairness in Anti-Money Laundering Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05101)
*Lea Multerer,Michele Inchingolo,David Kletz,Adrian Cosma,Alessandro Antonucci,Martina Gogova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了反洗钱（AML）系统中机器学习算法的公平性问题，发现通过反事实路径特定效应分析，模型预测性能的提升往往伴随着更明显的公平性违规，揭示了预测准确性与公平性之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管机器学习预测算法在反洗钱领域应用日益广泛，且通常基于交易数据和敏感客户信息进行训练，但AML检测系统从公平性角度的研究仍严重不足。现有基于反事实的深度分析方法能够分解敏感特征对模型预测的直接和间接影响，为公平性评估提供了可能，因此有必要填补这一研究空白。

Method: 使用合成IBM AMLSim交易数据集，构建账户国家及其平均行为等额外特征，提升从基线决策树到最先进图神经网络等多种机器学习模型的预测性能。采用反事实路径特定效应分析方法，评估这些特征相关的潜在不公平性，分解敏感特征对模型预测的影响路径。

Result: 预测性能从扩展特征中获益最多的模型，其公平性违规往往更为明显。这一发现揭示了AML应用中预测准确性与公平性之间的具体权衡实例，表明性能提升可能以牺牲公平性为代价。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在反洗钱等关键领域进行系统性公平性分析的紧迫性，揭示了准确性与公平性之间的内在张力，为AML系统的公平性评估和改进提供了重要参考。

Abstract: The application of machine learning-based predictive algorithms to Anti-Money Laundering (AML) has grown rapidly, driven by the vast volume of financial transaction data available to banks. These algorithms are typically trained not only on transactional data but also on sensitive client information, which may raise fairness concerns. Despite this, AML detection systems remain largely underexplored from a fairness perspective, even though deeper analytical methods based on counterfactuals are now available. Such techniques enable the decomposition of the direct and indirect effects of potentially sensitive features on model predictions, thereby supporting the evaluation of whether their influence is acceptable from a fairness perspective. Closing this gap, we consider the synthetic IBM AMLSim transaction dataset and construct additional features of the country of an account and its average behaviour. This improves the predictive performance of diverse machine learning models, ranging from baseline decision trees to state-of-the-art graph neural networks. We assess the potential unfairness associated with these features through a counterfactual, path-specific effect analysis. This reveals that fairness violations tend to be more pronounced for models whose predictive performance benefits the most from the extended features. Such a finding highlights a concrete instance of the trade-off between predictive accuracy and fairness in AML applications, thus underscoring the urgency of a systematic fairness analysis in such critical domains.

</details>


### [265] [Choosing a parallel heterogeneous ensemble method for tabular classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05103)
*Vassili Maillet,Gustavo,Angulo,Pierre Jouvelot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于56个中小型表格分类任务的对比实验，提出了并行集成方法的最佳实践建议，并在额外28个任务上验证了其有效性。关键发现包括：Blending和Stacking的不一致性相互独立；鲁棒软投票在多分类任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为平行集成方法在表格分类任务中的实际应用提供基于实证研究的最佳实践建议，解决不同集成方法选择和应用中的不确定性问题。

Method: 在OpenML CC18基准数据集上选择56个中小型表格分类任务进行系统性比较，基于实验观察推导最佳实践建议，随后在TabArena的预计算数据上使用额外28个任务验证这些推荐的有效性。

Result: 推荐方法集合显著优于单一最佳方法，且匹配或超越单个集成方法。关键发现：1) Blending和Stacking表现不一致，但其不一致性相互独立且发生在不同任务上；2) 硬投票的概率分类能力较弱（因为使用投票比例作为后验估计），而鲁棒软投票的概率分类特别成功，尤其在多分类情况下。

Conclusion: 通过大规模实证研究建立了平行集成方法的最佳实践指南，证明了鲁棒软投票在概率分类任务中的优越性，为表格分类任务中集成方法的选择和应用提供了可靠的理论指导。

Abstract: Parallel ensemble methods were compared on $56$ small-to-medium tabular classification tasks drawn from OpenML CC18. A set of ``best practice'' recommendations on the use of ensemble methods was derived from these observations. It was later validated on 28 additional tasks using TabArena's precomputed data, where the recommendation set significantly outperformed Single Best and matched or exceeded individual ensemble methods.
  Two key observations were made. First, Blending and Stacking are inconsistent, but their inconsistencies are independent and happen on different tasks. Second, while Hard Voting's probabilistic classification is rather weak, a consequence of using vote proportions as posterior estimates, Robust Soft Voting's probabilistic classification is particularly successful, especially in the multiclass case.

</details>


### [266] [Physiological Noise Augmentation Improves Non-Invasive Brain-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05165)
*Benjamin Ballyk,Teyun Kwon,Miran Özdogan,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了生理噪声增强（PNA）方法，通过分解脑电信号并重新混合噪声成分来生成训练数据，显著提升了非侵入式脑电解码的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非侵入式脑机接口（BCI）旨在为神经退行性疾病患者恢复交流能力，且无手术风险。然而，基于MEG和EEG的现有方法由于信噪比较低，词错误率仍然很高。

Method: 提出生理噪声增强（PNA）方法：首先利用独立成分分析（ICA）将脑电记录分解为纯净数据和噪声伪迹，然后缩放并重新混合以生成生物物理上真实、标签保留的训练样本。该方法近似各向异性正则化，惩罚解码器沿伪迹主导方向的敏感性。

Result: 在包含12k次试验的想象数字MEG数据集MegNIST上，PNA结合10次试验平均使EEGNet解码准确率相比仅使用真实数据训练提高了4.7个百分点（绝对值）。

Conclusion: 伪迹感知增强与试验平均是提升非侵入式语音BCI鲁棒性的互补工具，PNA方法通过显式训练解码器对任务无关伪迹保持不变性来改善性能。

Abstract: Non-invasive brain-to-speech decoding aims to restore communication to patients suffering from neurodegenerative disease, without the risks of neurosurgery. Existing MEG- and EEG-based methods, while scalable, continue to suffer from high word error rates driven by relatively low signal-to-noise ratios compared to invasive recordings. We propose physiological noise augmentation (PNA), a data augmentation method that explicitly trains decoders to become invariant to task-agnostic artifacts (e.g. ocular and cardiac activity). PNA draws inspiration from automatic speech recognition systems, where environmental noise (e.g. dogs barking, city traffic) is added to clean speech to improve robustness. Analogously, we decompose brain recordings into clean data and noise artifacts using independent component analysis (ICA), before scaling and remixing to generate biophysically realistic, label-preserving training examples. We show that PNA approximates anisotropic regularization, penalizing decoder sensitivity along artifact-dominated directions. On MegNIST, a 12k-trial imagined-digit MEG dataset, PNA with 10-trial averaging improves EEGNet decoding accuracy by 4.7 percentage points (absolute) over training on real data alone. Our results suggest that artifact-aware augmentation and trial averaging are complementary tools for improving robustness in non-invasive speech BCIs.

</details>


### [267] [MeGA-MP: Metric Graph Advection Message Passing -- A Physics-Informed Message Passing Operator for Advection-Dominated Metric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05167)
*Janine Strotherm,Luca Hermes,André Artelt,Barbara Hammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理知识的消息传递算子，用于在度量图上建模时空动态，特别是平流-反应动力学，实现了无需训练即可恢复纯平流动态，并在水分配系统等实际应用中取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中许多系统（如水分配系统、电网、交通流网络）天然地组织为网络结构，其时空动力学沿着连接连续展开。这些系统通常建模为度量图，其中边对应于一维欧几里得子空间。然而，度量图独立于底层全局欧几里得空间，限制了典型PINN和算子学习方法的直接应用。此外，像平流这样的输运动力学需要能够捕获图上的反对称和长程依赖关系，这本身就是一个挑战。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的基于物理知识的消息传递算子，将度量图上的线性平流编码为归纳偏置。在纯平流设置下，该算子理论上可证明能够精确恢复动力学，误差仅为理论推导的离散化误差，且无需训练。结合MLP等可训练组件，该消息传递算子扩展到水分配系统中现实的平流-反应动力学建模。

Result: 在纯平流场景中，无需训练即可理论证明精确恢复动态，误差仅来自离散化；在真实水分配系统的平流-反应动力学建模中，相比基准方法取得了更优越的性能，并在不同图拓扑结构上实现了零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了度量图上时空动力学建模的关键挑战，通过引入物理归纳偏置，既保证了理论上的精确性，又具备实际应用的灵活性，为网络化系统的动力学建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Many real-world systems are organized as networks where spatio-temporal dynamics unfold along connections and not discretely between nodes. Examples include utility networks such as water distribution systems or gas networks, electrical grids, and traffic flow networks. Such systems are naturally modeled as metric graphs, where edges correspond to one-dimensional Euclidean subspaces connected at vertices. Metric graphs are independent of an underlying global Euclidean space, limiting direct application of typical PINNs and operator-learning methods. Especially transport dynamics like advection require a methodology able to capture antisymmetric and long-range dependencies on graphs, which is itself a challenge. We propose a novel physics-informed message passing operator that encodes linear advection on metric graphs as an inductive bias. In the purely advective setting, the operator provably recovers the exact dynamics up to a theoretically derived discretization error without any training. Combined with trainable components like MLPs, our message passing operator extends to realistic advection-reaction dynamics in water distribution systems, where we achieve superior performance compared to baselines and zero-shot generalization across different graph topologies.

</details>


### [268] [Platonic Projection Structures: Operator-Induced Observability in Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05175)
*Kazuo Ishii,Bishnu Prasad Gautam,Jieling Wu,Javaid Saher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Platonic Projection Structures (PPS)框架，通过算子理论分析部分观测下的表示可观测性，揭示了基于输出的可解释性方法存在结构性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表示学习方法通常将可观测输出视为潜在表示的直接反映，忽略了观测过程本身的限制。需要建立理论框架来刻画部分观测条件下的表示可访问性，并理解可解释性方法固有的局限性。

Method: 提出PPS框架，将系统建模为三元组$(H, Π, O)$，其中$H$为潜在表示空间，$Π\succeq 0$为观测算子，$O(v)=\langle v,Πv\rangle$为诱导标量可观测量。通过商几何$H/\ker(Π)$刻画可观测性，将量子测量与表示推断纳入统一的算子理论结构。

Result: 证明了量子测量与线性观测模型下的表示推断共享算子理论结构；表示迁移和知识蒸馏可解释为通过$ΦΠ_T \approx Π_S Φ$近似保持可观测量几何；揭示了基于输出的可解释性存在结构性限制：$\ker(Π)$中的潜在分量无法从诱导可观测量访问。实验验证了核不变可观测性、投影诱导的归因差距等现象。

Conclusion: PPS框架通过算子诱导的商几何为可观测性提供了显式刻画，为表示可访问性、可解释性和投影中介推断提供了统一视角，并指出了基于输出的可解释性方法本质上无法访问潜在表示的所有维度。

Abstract: We characterize observability in representation learning through Platonic Projection Structures (PPS), an operator-theoretic framework for analyzing representation accessibility under partial observation. Rather than treating observable outputs as direct reflections of latent representations, PPS models observation through a self-adjoint positive semidefinite operator acting on a latent representation space. A system is represented as a triple $(H, Π, O)$, where $H$ is a latent representation space, $Π\succeq 0$ is an observation operator, and $O(v)=\langle v,Πv\rangle$ defines an induced scalar observable. Observability is characterized by the quotient geometry $H/\ker(Π)$, representing equivalence classes of latent states indistinguishable under observation. We show that quantum measurement and representation inference under linear observation models share this operator-theoretic structure while differing in the algebraic properties of their observation operators; the correspondence is structural rather than physical. Representation transfer and knowledge distillation can likewise be interpreted as approximate preservation of observable geometry through $ΦΠ_T \approx Π_S Φ$. PPS also reveals a structural limitation of output-based interpretability: latent components in $\ker(Π)$ are inaccessible from induced observables, imposing intrinsic constraints on attribution and explanation methods. Controlled empirical validations demonstrate kernel-invariant observability, projection-induced attribution gaps, and rank-controlled observable geometry in latent representation spaces. PPS thus provides an explicit characterization of observability through operator-induced quotient geometry and a unified perspective on representation accessibility, interpretability, and projection-mediated inference.

</details>


### [269] [Latent Programming Horizons in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05188)
*André Silva,Han Tu,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现编码代理中的语言模型内部状态线性编码了程序的属性（如是否能解析、通过测试等），且这些内部表示能超前预测未来多达25步的编辑结果，揭示了代理潜在的“编程视野”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的编码代理在解决软件工程任务时会经历数十步的推理、代码编辑和测试运行，但对其底层的语言模型内部如何表征正在处理的程序知之甚少。本研究旨在探索语言模型内部状态是否编码了程序的相关属性及其演变过程。

Method: 研究者在语言模型的残差流上训练逻辑回归探针，用于解码当前代码的多种属性（如是否可解析、是否通过测试套件、是否减少失败测试数量、是否引入回归等），并进一步训练探针预测未来编辑步骤的结果。实验覆盖了两个模型和两个基准测试，并验证了探针在基准间的迁移能力。

Result: 实验表明，模型隐藏状态线性编码了程序属性，逻辑回归探针在正确性预测上达到最高0.83的AUC。更令人惊讶的是，探针能在编辑实际发生前预测未来结果，性能高于随机水平，预测范围可超前约25步，定义为“潜在编程视野”。此外，探针无需重新训练即可在不同基准间迁移。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了语言模型在编码代理中具有丰富的内部程序表征和前瞻能力，为编码代理的机制可解释性研究提供了新的方向，呼吁更多相关研究。

Abstract: A coding agent solving a software-engineering task spends dozens of steps reasoning, editing code, and running tests, yet little is known about what the underlying language model internally represents about the program it is working on. We show that the residual streams of language models under coding agents linearly encode properties of the evolving program: a logistic-regression probe on hidden states is able to decode whether the current code parses, passes its test suite, reduces the number of failing tests, and introduces regressions, reaching AUC up to 0.83 for correctness across two models and two benchmarks. Our second finding is more surprising: these representations run ahead of the agent's own edits. Probes trained to predict the outcome of future edits (before they are materialized and written on disk) achieve performance above chance up to roughly 25 steps in advance. We call this the agent's latent programming horizon. As a proof of external validity, we show that the probes transfer across benchmarks without retraining. Our positive results open calls for more research in mechanistic interpretability of coding agents.

</details>


### [270] [FlatManifold: Robust Continual Learning under Severe Label Noise and Domain Shifts via Intrinsic Manifold Flattening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05201)
*Rai Hisada,Kanji Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FlatManifold框架，通过基于核技巧和正交化再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）投影的Nyström流形扁平化映射，在非平稳流式环境中同时实现持续学习和缓解严重标签噪声的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非平稳流式环境中，同时适应复杂的非线性域偏移（持续学习）并缓解严重、未校准标签噪声的灾难性影响是一个基础的数学挑战。传统方法依赖复杂且易错的样本过滤流程，效果有限。

Method: 提出FlatManifold框架，利用基于核技巧的Nyström流形扁平化映射，将特征分布投影到固定的正交目标拓扑上，并结合岭正则化器。框架通过流形扁平化空间本身的内在数学鲁棒性来平滑和对抗极端标签噪声的影响，同时使用基于过去经验协方差矩阵的持续拓扑制动项来防止灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在真实世界多会话机器人数据集上的广泛评估表明，即使在40%对称标签噪声的严重条件下，FlatManifold成功缓解了梯度损坏。在跨越不同季节和光照条件的极端跨会话域偏移下，该框架展现出高泛化能力，显著优于标准顺序优化基线。

Conclusion: 结构线性化本身可作为对抗分布式标签损坏的强大数学屏障，证明了所提框架在处理持续学习和标签噪声问题上的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: In non-stationary streaming environments, simultaneously adapting to complex, non-linear domain shifts via continual learning while mitigating the catastrophic effects of severe, uncalibrated label noise poses a fundamental mathematical challenge. In this paper, we propose \FlatManifold{}, a novel, streamlined robust continual learning framework that utilizes a Nyström manifold flattening map based on the kernel trick and projection onto an orthogonalized Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space (RKHS).
  Unlike traditional methods that rely on complex, error-prone sample-filtering pipelines, the proposed approach exploits the intrinsic mathematical robustness of the flattened space itself. By mapping feature distributions onto a fixed orthogonal target topology with a ridge regularizer, the framework naturally smoothes and counteracts the influence of extreme label noise during the optimization process. Concurrently, catastrophic forgetting is prevented via a continual topology brake term that leverages the covariance matrix of past experiences.
  Extensive evaluation on real-world multi-session robotics datasets demonstrates that even under severe conditions featuring 40\% symmetric label noise, \FlatManifold{} successfully mitigates gradient corruption. Under extreme cross-session domain shifts spanning various seasons and lighting conditions, the proposed framework establishes high generalization capabilities, significantly outperforming standard sequential optimization baselines and proving that structural linearization itself serves as a powerful mathematical barrier against distributed label corruption.

</details>


### [271] [Video-based detection of cessation of breathing in pre-term infants using machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05230)
*Dineo Serame,Lionel Tarassenko,Mauricio Villarroel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究验证了非接触式视频监测可检测早产儿呼吸暂停相关的呼吸停止(COBE)，结合常规生理信号后检测准确率提升至90.6%，为新生儿重症监护病房提供了一种互补的呼吸监测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早产儿因呼吸控制不成熟易发生潜在有害的呼吸暂停，但新生儿重症监护病房(NICU)中可靠的呼吸监测仍具挑战性。接触式测量方法受运动伪影、传感器位移和皮肤脆弱性等因素影响而可靠性受限。非接触式视频监测作为一种不依赖粘附传感器的补充方法，可提供额外的呼吸信息。

Method: 研究采集了30名早产儿的视频和临床记录数据。通过动态追踪躯干区域提取呼吸运动，生成相机衍生的时间序列信号。构建了两种模型：(1)仅使用相机的残差网络(ResNet)架构模型；(2)将视频衍生信号与阻抗呼吸描记(IP)、心电衍生呼吸(EDR)和PPG衍生呼吸包络相结合的混合模型。

Result: 仅使用相机的模型实现了76.9%的平衡准确率，证明了非接触式COBE检测的可行性。将视频衍生特征与IP结合后，平衡准确率提升至90.6%，优于任一单独模态，表明视频提供了超越标准生理信号的呼吸信息。

Conclusion: 视频衍生信号包含临床相关的呼吸特征，与常规生理信号结合可增强COBE检测能力。这支持将非接触式视频作为自动化COBE检测的补充模态，并凸显了其提高新生儿呼吸监测稳健性的潜力。

Abstract: Pre-term infants are susceptible to potentially harmful apnoea-related cessations of breathing due to immature respiratory control. However, reliable respiratory monitoring in the neonatal intensive care unit (NICU) remains challenging because motion artefacts, sensor displacement, and skin fragility can compromise contact-based measurements. Non-contact video monitoring offers a complementary approach that does not depend on adhesive sensors while providing additional respiratory information.
  We investigated whether camera-based signals can detect apnoea-related cessation of breathing (COBE) and provide complementary information to routinely acquired physiological signals. Using video and clinical recordings from 30 pre-term infants, respiratory motion was extracted from dynamically tracked torso regions to generate camera-derived time-series signals. Camera-only models were trained using residual network (ResNet) architectures, while hybrid models combined video-derived signals with impedance pneumography (IP), ECG-derived respiration (EDR), and the PPG-derived respiratory envelope.
  Camera-only models achieved a balanced accuracy of 76.9%, demonstrating the feasibility of non-contact COBE detection. Combining video-derived features with IP improved balanced accuracy to 90.6%, outperforming either modality alone and indicating that video provides respiratory information beyond standard physiological signals.
  These findings show that video-derived signals contain clinically relevant respiratory features and enhance COBE detection when combined with conventional physiological signals. This supports non-contact video as a complementary modality for automated COBE detection and highlights its potential to improve the robustness of neonatal respiratory monitoring.

</details>


### [272] [FUSE: FK-Steered Multi-Modal Flow Matching for Efficient Simulation-Based Posterior Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05252)
*Weichen Qin,Yufan Xie,Peihao Wang,Chia-Jui Chou,Minghui Du,Peng Xu,Ziren Luo,Yi Yang,Jingyi Yu,Bo Liang,Jiakai Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FUSE方法，一种基于Feynman-Kac引导的多模态流匹配方法，用于高效的模拟后验估计，通过双轨架构和FK引导采样策略解决了现有方法在多模态建模方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于模拟推断（SBI）生成模型方法在处理多模态建模时存在困难，通常依赖粗暴的融合策略，忽略了参数和观测数据之间的结构性差异，从而限制了估计的保真度。

Method: 提出FUSE方法，采用双轨架构来保持多模态输入的不同特征，同时促进动态交互；并提出FK引导采样策略，利用中间观测似然来引导生成轨迹，有效提升推理过程中的样本质量。

Result: FUSE在标准SBI基准测试中优于最先进基线，生成的后验分布与真实MCMC结果高度吻合；在真实系外行星轨道估计任务中，成功解决了困扰现有方法的复杂参数简并问题。

Conclusion: FUSE在解决多模态建模挑战方面展现出显著优势，其在天体物理学等复杂科学发现任务中具有加速研究和应用的潜力。

Abstract: Simulation-Based Inference (SBI) is critical for scientific discovery, with generative models offering a promising path toward efficient inference. However, existing methods struggle with effective multimodal modeling. They often rely on brute-force fusion strategies that ignore the structural disparities between parameters and observations, thus limiting estimation fidelity. In this work, we introduce FUSE (Feynman-Kac steered mUlti-modal flow matching for efficient Simulation-based posterior Estimation). Unlike prior work, FUSE employs a dual-track architecture that preserves the distinct features of multimodal inputs while facilitating dynamic interaction. Additionally, we propose an FK-steered sampling strategy that leverages intermediate observation likelihoods to guide the generative trajectories, effectively improving the sample quality during inference. Our approach outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on standard SBI benchmarks, producing posteriors that closely match ground-truth MCMC. Furthermore, in a real-world exoplanet orbital estimation task, FUSE successfully resolves complex parameter degeneracies that challenge existing methods, highlighting its potential to accelerate complex scientific discoveries in astrophysics and beyond.

</details>


### [273] [GeoFlow: Geo-Aware Modeling of Inter-Area Relationships in Origin-Destination Flow Prediction and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05257)
*Zherui Huang,Guanjie Zheng,Hao Xue,Linghe Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GeoFlow框架，通过融合地理空间属性和几何-内在编码器，解决了现有图方法在OD流建模中忽视地理特征的问题，在预测精度和生成质量上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的OD流建模方法往往忽略显著的地理属性，限制了其对长距离和多区域依赖关系的建模能力，亟需一种能充分整合地理信息的方法。

Method: 提出GeoFlow框架：1) 用地理空间属性（相对位置、k跳距离、测地距离）增强区域表示；2) 设计几何-内在融合编码器，结合图注意力机制和坐标感知编码器；3) 采用轴向-全局注意力解码器捕获OD特定的竞争依赖；4) 结合流匹配模型进行OD流生成。

Result: GeoFlow在预测精度上取得优越性能，同时在生成真实性和多样性方面有显著提升，消融研究证实了各组件的贡献。

Conclusion: GeoFlow通过有效融合地理空间属性和设计专门的网络架构，成功提升了OD流建模的预测和生成能力，为城市规划和移动性分析提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Origin-destination (OD) flow modeling underpins urban planning and mobility analysis, but prevailing graph-based methods often neglect salient geographic attributes, limiting their ability to model long-range and multi-area dependencies. In this paper, we introduce GeoFlow, a novel framework that (i) augments area representations with geospatial attributes, including relative positions, k-hop and geodesic distances, (ii) employs a specialized geometric-intrinsic fusion encoder design that combines graph attention for intrinsic area signals with coordinate-aware encoders for global structure, and (iii) adopts an axial-global attention decoder to capture OD-specific competitive dependencies. For OD flow generation, GeoFlow is paired with flow matching models to produce more authentic and diverse mobility samples. Empirically, GeoFlow achieves superior performance in predictive accuracy, while substantially improving generative fidelity and diversity. Ablation and analytical studies confirm the contribution of each component. Code is available at https://github.com/ZheruiHuang/GeoFlow.

</details>


### [274] [Target-Guided Selective Reweighting for Physics-Informed Neural Network Inverse Problems: A Transfer Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05271)
*Qian Hu,Bin Fan,Yao Xiao,Zhicheng Lin,Meixin Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对PINN逆问题迁移学习中的负迁移问题，提出TGSR-PINN方法，通过目标引导的神经元选择性重加权，在保持场精度同时提升目标物理参数恢复准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN在偏微分方程逆问题中面临不适定优化、损失竞争和参数补偿问题。迁移学习虽可复用源任务表征，但当源域和目标域的主导物理机制、控制参数或观测噪声存在差异时，直接微调会导致负迁移：模型获得较低场误差但恢复出错误的目标物理参数。

Method: 提出Target-Guided Selective Reweighting PINN (TGSR-PINN)方法：仅迁移源PINN的权重和偏置，目标物理参数独立初始化；经过短暂数据适配期后，利用一阶泰勒敏感性和固定评分批次上的预激活方差计算神经元目标分数；通过带秩回退的高斯混合模型(GMM)将低分神经元相关证据转化为连续弱适应信号；对低分神经元的输入权重行和偏置施加选择性软衰减，而非硬剪枝或随机重置。

Result: 在高Péclet数二维对流扩散任务和Allen-Cahn到Burgers跨PDE族迁移任务中，TGSR-PINN在保持相当场精度的同时改善了目标参数恢复；在5%噪声反应扩散案例中提供了源-目标失配较温和情况下的补充证据。消融研究表明神经元目标评分、弱适应信号估计、层保护和选择性软衰减共同贡献了性能提升。

Conclusion: TGSR-PINN通过目标证据驱动的表征校正机制，有效缓解了PINN逆问题迁移学习中的负迁移问题，在跨物理机制、跨PDE族的迁移场景下实现了场精度与参数恢复准确性的平衡，为物理信息神经网络的迁移学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) encounter ill-posed optimization, loss competition, and parameter compensation in partial differential equation (PDE) inverse problems. Transfer learning can reuse representations from source tasks, but direct fine-tuning may introduce negative transfer when dominant physical mechanisms, governing parameters, or observation noise differ between source and target domains: the model achieves low field error yet recovers incorrect target physical parameters. To mitigate, we propose Target-Guided Selective Reweighting PINN (TGSR-PINN), a target-evidence-driven representation correction method for PINN inverse transfer learning. TGSR-PINN transfers only the weights and biases from the source PINN, while target physical parameters are independently initialized; after a short target-adaptation phase, the method computes neuron target scores using first-order Taylor sensitivity and pre-activation variance on fixed scoring batches, and converts evidence associated with low-scoring neurons into continuous weak-adaptation signals via a Gaussian mixture model (GMM) with rank fallback. TGSR-PINN then applies selective soft decay to input weight rows and biases of low-scoring neurons instead of hard pruning or random resetting. In experiments, TGSR-PINN improves target parameter recovery while maintaining comparable field accuracy in the high-Péclet 2D advection-diffusion task and in the Allen--Cahn to Burgers cross-PDE-family transfer task; a 5%-noise reaction--diffusion case provides supplementary evidence under milder source-target mismatch. Ablation studies suggest that neuron target scoring, weak-adaptation signal estimation, layer protection, and selective soft decay jointly contribute to the benefits.

</details>


### [275] [Advances in Neural Controlled Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05280)
*Benjamin Walker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文通过提出三种改进方法（Log-NCDEs、Linear NCDEs和Structured Linear NCDEs），成功解决了神经控制微分方程（NCDEs）训练成本高昂和可扩展性差的问题，将每次训练步骤的时间减少了三个数量级，并在多个时间序列基准测试中达到了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的许多系统都是连续演化的，但大多数机器学习模型将时间序列视为离散序列。神经控制微分方程（NCDEs）作为一种连续时间方法，虽然能够自然处理不规则采样或过采样数据，并且具有极强的表达能力，但其非线性向量场导致前向传播计算成本高昂且本质上是顺序的，限制了其可扩展性和实际应用价值。因此，如何提高NCDEs的训练效率和可扩展性成为亟待解决的问题。

Method: 本论文通过三个互补的贡献推进了NCDEs的训练和可扩展性：（1）Log-NCDEs：基于神经粗糙微分方程，应用Log-ODE方法在训练期间高效近似NCDE的解；（2）Linear NCDEs：用线性向量场替换非线性向量场，实现闭式解和时间并行计算；（3）Structured Linear NCDEs：使用结构化线性向量场进一步提升效率。这三种方法在保持理论表达能力的同时，分别从不同角度优化了NCDEs的计算效率。

Result: 这些方法共同作用，将NCDE每次训练步骤的时间减少了最多三个数量级，同时在多样化时间序列基准测试中达到了最先进的性能表现。证明了在保持甚至提升模型性能的前提下，大幅提升计算效率的可行性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功解决了NCDEs实际应用中的关键瓶颈问题，通过创新的数学方法和模型架构设计，在保持理论表达能力的同时显著提升了计算效率。这为连续时间神经网络在时间序列分析等实际应用场景中的广泛部署铺平了道路，证明了理论优雅性与实际效率可以兼得。

Abstract: Many real-world systems evolve continuously, yet most machine learning models interpret time series as discrete sequences. Continuous-time approaches instead treat time series as samples from an underlying input path, a formulation that naturally accommodates irregularly sampled or oversampled data. Among these, Neural Controlled Differential Equations (NCDEs) are a maximally expressive class of models that parametrise a vector field using a neural network and evolve their hidden state by solving a dynamical system driven by the input path. NCDEs typically use a non-linear vector field, so their expressive power and continuous-time flexibility come at the cost of a forward pass that is both computationally expensive and inherently sequential, limiting their scalability and practical applicability.
  This thesis advances the training and scalability of NCDEs through three complementary contributions. First, building on neural rough differential equations, Log-NCDEs apply the Log-ODE method to efficiently approximate an NCDE's solution during training, improving both computational speed and empirical performance. Second, Linear NCDEs replace the non-linear vector field with a linear one, enabling closed-form solutions and parallel-in-time computation without sacrificing theoretical expressivity. Third, Structured Linear NCDEs use structured linear vector fields to further enhance efficiency while maintaining theoretical expressiveness and empirical performance.
  Collectively, these methods reduce the time per training step for an NCDE by up to three orders of magnitude while achieving state-of-the-art performance across diverse time series benchmarks.

</details>


### [276] [Learning Only What Valid Adapters Can Express: Subspace-Constrained Adaptation Against Fine-Tuning Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05300)
*Fabien Polly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于受信适配器池子空间的约束微调方法，通过限制参数更新方向来防御数据投毒和后门攻击，在保持性能的同时显著提升了安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调（如LoRA）仍然存在广阔的行为改变更新空间，使得投毒目标可以被表示和优化。作者希望找到一种替代方案来限制这种潜在的恶意更新。

Method: 提出将适应过程约束在从受信任务适配器池估计的子空间中。通过分析196个公共LoRA适配器，发现适配器的功能相关内容位于低维共享子空间中，在此子空间内进行梯度适应。

Result: 在flan-t5-large上的实验表明：（1）适配器30-38%的权重范数是冗余的；（2）在128维子空间上的约束适应在干净数据上匹配完整LoRA微调，而针对标签反转攻击，LoRA崩溃到3-26%准确率，约束学习器保持62-96%；（3）约束学习器无法拟合损坏数据，损失相差120倍；（4）对自适应后门攻击者，攻击成功率从LoRA的100%降至8%（目标行为与池不相似时）或85%（目标与池行为重合时）。

Conclusion: 该方法以牺牲峰值可塑性为代价换取安全性：对于适配器池覆盖良好的任务提供有效保护，但在池覆盖不佳的任务上无约束微调更优。保护效果取决于目标行为与池中方向的接近程度，且假设池本身是受信任的。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning still leaves a broad space of behavior-changing updates reachable, so a poisoned objective can be represented and optimized.
