*Raj Jaiswal,Dhruv Jain,Rishabh Dhawan,Sree Krishna Uppalapati,Shin'ichi Satoh,Tanuja Ganu,Rajiv Ratn Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种步骤级奖励框架，通过识别首个推理错误、生成针对性反馈并引导模型自我修正，显著提升了小型语言模型在物理推理任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型在物理推理任务中存在结构性失败：任何步骤的错误都会向前传播，破坏后续所有推理。领域知识有限、多步推导中的幻觉问题以及分布敏感性共同导致了这一失败。现有的依赖标注的步骤级方法需要构建偏好数据，成本较高。

Method: 提出了一种步骤级奖励框架。该框架首先识别推理过程中的第一个错误，然后生成针对性的结构化反馈，最后通过带有KL正则化的策略梯度方法训练模型修正其解决方案。整个过程不将真实答案作为生成目标，无需构建偏好数据，且外部验证器仅在训练阶段使用。

Result: 在五个物理基准测试中，该框架相比思维链提示准确率提升了17-20%，相比最强基线提升了10-16%。计算错误率从56.9%降至23.5%，理解错误率从22.3%降至12.0%。概念错误率从89.7%降至68.7%，但仍是所有条件下最难解决的失败模式。

Conclusion: 该方法有效缓解了小型语言模型在物理推理中的错误传播问题，特别是在计算和理解错误方面效果显著。然而概念性错误仍是最具挑战性的失败模式，表明该领域仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Physics reasoning fails structurally in small language models: an error at any step propagates forward, corrupting every inference that follows. Limited domain knowledge, hallucination under multi-step derivation, and distributional sensitivity compound this failure. We propose a step-level reward framework that identifies the first reasoning error, generates targeted structured feedback, and trains the model to revise its solution via policy gradient with KL regularization, without exposing it to ground truth solutions as generation targets. Unlike annotation-dependent step-level methods, no preference data construction is required and the external verifier operates exclusively at training time. Across five physics benchmarks, our framework delivers accuracy gains of 17-20% over CoT prompting and 10-16% over the strongest baseline, reduces calculation errors from 56.9% to 23.5%, and reduces miscomprehension errors from 22.3% to 12.0% in the best observed cases. Conceptual errors reduce from 89.7% to 68.7%, yet persist as the hardest failure mode across all conditions.

</details>


### [381] [EvoAgentBench: Benchmarking Agent Self-Evolution via Ability Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05202)
*Xingze Gao,Chuanrui Hu,Hongda Chen,Pengfei Yao,Zhao Wang,Yi Bai,Zhengwei Wu,Yunyun Han,Xiaofeng Cong,Jie Gui,Yafeng Deng,Teng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了EvoAgentBench，首个评估大模型智能体通过可复用程序进行自我演化的基准测试，揭示了当前方法在跨领域程序迁移方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试仅关注单次任务解决能力，记忆基准测试聚焦信息留存而非程序复用，缺乏对智能体通过可重用程序（如搜索、调试、验证等能力）实现自我演化的评估手段。

Method: 构建EvoAgentBench基准：(1)从智能体执行轨迹中提取能力(Abilities)并规范化为可操作单元；(2)构建领域特定的能力图谱，链接具有程序重叠的任务；(3)覆盖网络研究、算法推理、软件工程、知识工作四个领域；(4)采用528/267的训练/测试分割，确保每个测试任务都有训练端的能力支持。

Result: 精心筛选的能力内容可以在不同模型家族间可靠迁移，但现有自动方法均无法在所有设置中维持正向增益，表明当前智能体在经验编码、路由和吸收方面存在明显不足。

Conclusion: EvoAgentBench将自我演化评估从宏观准确率比较转向对经验编码、路由和吸收的细粒度诊断，为智能体持续学习和能力迁移研究提供了新的评估范式。

Abstract: Agent self-evolution in long-horizon LLM systems is largely procedural: useful experience is not merely stored information, but reusable procedures for searching, debugging, and verification. Yet current evaluations do not isolate this form of transfer. Agent benchmarks test single-episode task solving; memory benchmarks target information retention rather than procedural reuse. We introduce EvoAgentBench, a benchmark for agent self-evolution via Ability-guided transfer across four agentic domains: web research, algorithmic reasoning, software engineering, and knowledge work. EvoAgentBench extracts trace-grounded Abilities from agent executions, canonicalizes them into operational units, and builds domain-specific Ability Graphs linking tasks that share procedural overlap. By design, every test task is backed by verified training-side Ability support. Across a 528/267 train/test split, two scaffolds, and three backbones, curated Ability content transfers reliably across model families, but no current automatic method sustains positive gain in all settings. EvoAgentBench shifts self-evolution evaluation from aggregate accuracy comparison to fine-grained diagnosis of experience encoding, routing, and uptake. The benchmark is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/EverMind-AI/EvoAgentBench.

</details>


### [382] [MoP-JEPA: Hard-Assigned Predictor Mixtures for Stochastic JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05238)
*Zhi Song,Ximing Xing,Zhenchao Tang,hanbo Huang,Tianxu Lv,minghao Yang,Zhongzheng Niu,He Bing,Lusheng Wang,Jianhua Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了JEPA世界模型在随机环境中的结构性缺陷：单预测器会在分支转移时坍缩到条件均值，导致预测无效状态。提出的MoP-JEPA采用硬分配的混合预测器，每个预测头收敛到转移分布的一个模式，可枚举且支持规划。实验中在OGBench任务上成功率达0.85，显著超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA世界模型使用单一确定性预测器进行潜在回归，在随机环境中的分支转移处会预测出条件均值（不存在于真实状态空间中的无效状态点），导致规划失败。需要解决这一结构性坍缩问题。

Method: 提出MoP-JEPA方法，使用硬分配的混合预测器架构。每个预测头独立收敛到转移分布的一个模式，可在单次前向传播中枚举所有可能的下一状态。设计了完整的验证协议防止评估作弊，包括输入无关的码本控制、打乱上下文测试、路由门控读出、转移精度保护和验证路由准则。

Result: 在OGBench官方离线数据集上，单预测器规划成功率仅0.02-0.09，而MoP-JEPA可达0.85。在三个迷宫任务上性能超越最强软分配基线2-5倍。验证协议识别出基线方法原始分数中包含通过不存在的预测转移路径。在真实环境执行中，方法在七个基线中排名第二。

Conclusion: 多模态动态特性决定了JEPA世界模型是否能够有效规划；硬分配的混合预测器是一个最小化且可验证的解决方案，能够有效避免确定性预测器在随机环境中的结构坍缩问题。

Abstract: JEPA world models predict the next latent state with a single deterministic predictor trained by latent regression. We show that this fails structurally when the environment is stochastic: at a branching transition, the regression-optimal predictor outputs the conditional mean of the successor embeddings, a point between the true next states that corresponds to no state at all. We prove this collapse for deterministic and gated mixture-of-experts predictors, and prove that MoP-JEPA's hard-assigned predictors converge instead to a quantizer of the transition distribution: one head per successor mode, enumerable in a single forward pass, which is the interface a planner consumes. On official OGBench offline data with leak-free evaluation, planning over single-predictor rollouts performs poorly ($0.02$--$0.09$ success) while planning over our predicted modes reaches up to $0.85$, ahead of deterministic, gated-MoE, and variational predictors on every task. Because multi-prediction evaluation invites coverage freeloading, a verification protocol is part of the method: an input-agnostic codebook control, a shuffled-context test, router-gated readouts, transition-precision guards, and a verified-route criterion in which the model proposes its transition graph blind and ground truth is used only to check the result. Under this criterion our method outperforms the strongest soft alternative on all three mazes ($2$--$5\times$), and the protocol identifies the remaining gap in that baseline's raw scores as routes through predicted transitions that do not exist. The same model executes in the real environment, placing second of seven against the published OGBench baselines on the hardest maze. Multimodal dynamics decide whether a JEPA world model can plan at all; a mixture of predictors with hard assignment is a minimal and verifiable fix.

</details>


### [383] [MetaSkill-Evolve: Recursive Self-Improvement of LLM Agents via Two-Timescale Meta-Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05297)
*Zefeng Wang,Minxi Yan,Jinhe Bi,Sikuan Yan,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MetaSkill-Evolve提出了一种递归式的智能体技能改进框架，通过双层时间尺度机制使技能改进过程本身也能自我进化，在三个基准测试上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自我改进智能体仅优化任务技能（做什么），而改进流程（如何改进）是固定的、手工编写的，无法适应任务多样性，改进能力本身无法随经验提升。

Method: 提出MetaSkill-Evolve双时间尺度框架：快速循环改进任务技能s，慢速循环改进元技能m。元技能包含五个组件(ψ,σ,α,π,ε)，分别参数化改进流水线中的Analyzer、Retriever、Allocator、Proposer和Evolver五个智能体，元技能通过应用于自身来进化，无需额外模型或目标。

Result: 在OfficeQA、SealQA和ALFWorld三个智能体基准测试上，相比原始骨干模型分别提升+23.54、+16.09和+1.92个百分点的测试准确率，全面优于无技能、静态技能和单层进化基线。

Conclusion: 通过使智能体技能改进递归化，MetaSkill-Evolve实现了改进流程的自我优化，证明了元技能进化在提升智能体长期性能方面的有效性，为构建自适应学习系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent LLM agents tackle increasingly long-horizon, open-ended tasks, and external skills, reusable procedural knowledge supplied to the agent, further extend this capability. However, a fixed, hand-authored skill is rarely optimal, and cannot adapt to the diversity of tasks an agent encounters. Self-improving agents address this by rewriting their own skill files from execution traces, yielding meaningful gains on challenging benchmarks. Yet such self-evolution remains non-recursive: it improves only the task skill (what the agent does) while the improvement procedure (how it improves) is authored once and held fixed. We introduce MetaSkill-Evolve, a two-timescale framework that makes agentic skill improvement recursive: every branch carries both a task skill $s$ and a branch-local meta-skill $m=(ψ,σ,α,π,\varepsilon)$ whose five components parameterise the Analyzer, Retriever, Allocator, Proposer, and Evolver agents of the improvement pipeline. Task skills evolve on a fast loop while the meta-skill evolves on a slower one under the same pipeline applied to itself, with no additional model or objective. With all five pipeline agents sharing a single frozen backbone, MetaSkill-Evolve outperforms no-skill, static-skill, and single-level evolution baselines on three agentic benchmarks (OfficeQA, SealQA, ALFWorld), improving held-out test accuracy over the raw backbone by +23.54, +16.09, and +1.92 points respectively.

</details>


### [384] [Evaluating and Understanding Model Editing for Medical Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05310)
*Guli Zhu,Chenwei Wu,Liyue Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了M3Bench，这是首个面向医学视觉语言模型编辑的临床基准测试，包含16,276个问题，覆盖多种解剖结构、模态和专业。研究发现现有编辑方法无法在所有标准上表现优异：基于梯度的方法泛化性强但局部性差，基于记忆的方法局部性好但组合泛化能力弱且对超参数敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态模型编辑基准主要针对通用任务，未能反映真实的临床领域需求和变异性。医学视觉语言模型在部署后可能出现错误，需要快速、精准的修正方法避免昂贵的重新训练，但目前缺乏针对临床场景的模型编辑评估基准。

Method: 构建了M3Bench基准测试，包含16,276个问题，涵盖多样化的解剖结构、医学模态和临床专科。该基准评估模型编辑在图像和文本变化、模态和协议转换、临床知识组合以及时间进程等挑战下的可靠性、精确性和泛化能力，支持单次和序列编辑。在6个医学和通用视觉语言模型上评估了4种代表性编辑方法。

Result: 实验发现没有任何一种方法能在所有标准上表现卓越：基于梯度的编辑器实现了强迁移能力，但存在严重的局部性违规问题；基于记忆的方法保持了局部性，但缺乏组合泛化性，且表现出高度依赖骨干网络的超参数敏感性。研究将这些失败归因于视觉语言模型的潜在空间几何结构以及不同编辑方法如何改变其景观。

Conclusion: M3Bench为多模态模型编辑建立了严格的临床压力测试标准，为更安全的部署后适应提供了可操作的指导。基准测试已公开可用，推动医学AI模型编辑领域的发展。

Abstract: Model editing promises a fast, targeted way to correct post-deployment mistakes in medical vision-language models (VLMs) without costly retraining. However, existing multimodal model editing benchmarks focus on general-purpose tasks and do not reflect realistic clinical domain requirements and variability. To address this, we introduce M3Bench, a clinically grounded benchmark for multimodal model editing that evaluates whether an edit remains reliable, precise, and generalizable under the challenges of image and text variation, modality and protocol shifts, clinical knowledge composition, and temporal progression. M3Bench contains 16,276 questions spanning diverse anatomy, modalities, and specialties, and supports both single and sequential edits.
  By evaluating 4 representative editors across 6 medical and general VLMs, we find that no method excels across all criteria. Gradient-based editors achieve strong transfer but suffer from catastrophic locality violations, whereas memory-based methods preserve locality but lack compositional generality and exhibit high backbone-dependent hyperparameter sensitivity. We further attribute these failures to the latent space geometry of VLMs and how different editing methods shift its landscape. Overall, M3Bench establishes a rigorous clinical stress test for multimodal model editing and offers actionable guidance for safer post-deployment adaptation. The benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/BioMed-AI-Lab-U-Michgan/M3Bench .

</details>


### [385] [Graph Sparse Sampling: Breaking the Curse of the Horizon in Continuous MDP Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05359)
*Idan Lev-Yehudi,Vadim Indelman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出图稀疏采样(GSS)算法，通过在图中共享采样未来而非为每个动作分支采样，避免了树形规划的指数深度依赖，在连续控制任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续域不确定性规划对自主系统至关重要，但传统树搜索方法（如MCTS）的分支结构在最坏情况下需要随预测深度指数增长的采样预算，连续状态或动作空间使规划器面临无限分支层次的挑战。

Method: 提出图稀疏采样(GSS)在线规划算法，采用无分支图结构在多个候选决策间共享采样未来，而非为每个候选动作单独采样后继；结合启发式方法聚焦计算，生成大规模GPU友好的批次；通过平滑备份证明有限样本性能保证。

Result: 在适当的重叠、正则性和动作覆盖条件下，算法的边界具有关于规划时域的多项式依赖性，形式化证明了共享未来可以避免树形稀疏采样的指数时域依赖；在连续控制仿真中，GSS在长时域任务上大幅优于树形规划器或达到近最优性能。

Conclusion: 无分支图规划作为在线控制的补充设计原则具有有效性，能够在保持性能保证的同时显著提升计算效率，为连续域不确定性规划提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Planning under uncertainty in continuous domains is essential for autonomous systems, yet computationally demanding. Tree-based search methods such as Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) remain popular, but their branching structure can require sampling budgets that grow exponentially with lookahead depth in the worst case. From a tree perspective, continuous state or action spaces become especially challenging, since the planner must decide where to search in an infinite branching hierarchy. We propose Graph Sparse Sampling (GSS), an online planning algorithm that shares sampled futures across many candidate decisions, rather than sampling separate successors for each candidate action. This branch-free graph exposes large GPU-friendly batches, while using heuristics to focus computation. We prove finite-sample performance guarantees for GSS covering full-rank or low-rank generative simulators via smoothed backups, and discrete or sampled continuous action spaces. Under suitable overlap, regularity, and action-coverage conditions, these bounds have polynomial dependence on the planning horizon, formalizing when shared futures can avoid the exponential horizon dependence of tree-shaped sparse sampling. We demonstrate continuous-control simulations where GSS substantially outperforms tree-based planners on long horizons or achieves near-optimal performance, supporting no-branching graph planning as a complementary design principle for online control.

</details>


### [386] [SovereignPA-Bench: Evaluating User-Owned Personal Agents under Evolving Intent, Platform Mediation, and Consent Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05363)
*Dylan Zongmin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SovereignPA-Bench基准测试，用于评估个人代理是否能在保护用户主权（隐私、同意、证据、用户负担、抵抗操控）的同时完成用户意图，发现全主权脚手架方法能显著提升主权得分并降低违规行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估工具使用、网页导航、个性化等功能，但很少关注代理是否保护用户主权——即在尊重隐私、同意、证据要求、用户负担和抵抗操控的前提下推进用户利益。随着个人代理成为持久的用户自有中介，这一评估缺口亟待填补。

Method: 构建了SovereignPA-Bench可执行基准，设计了120个主权压力场景，分离代理可见的可观察状态与仅评估者可见的隐藏标签，报告任务成功、对齐、隐私、同意、证据、操控、负担和可审计性等组件指标，并在4个模型家族和8个策略基线上进行评估，生成3840条冻结提示轨迹。

Result: 全主权脚手架方法的主权得分优于直接调用、仅记忆、仅同意、仅证据、ReAct/工具使用、安全提示和判断守护等基线，同时降低了隐私泄露、同意违规、过度让步和操控捕获。人工审计显示隐私和同意判断一致性高，而操控判断一致性较低，揭示了平台劝说判断的主观边界。

Conclusion: 个人代理评估必须超越任务完成度，转向具有代表性、同意感知、证据驱动的行动评估，以真正保护用户主权。

Abstract: Personal agents are becoming persistent user-owned intermediaries: they remember preferences, filter platform-mediated information, use tools, and negotiate with services. Existing benchmarks evaluate tool use, web navigation, desktop control, personalization, recommendation, and evolving context, but rarely ask whether an agent preserves user sovereignty: advancing the user's current interests while respecting privacy, consent, evidence, user burden, and resistance to manipulative incentives. We introduce SovereignPA-Bench, an executable benchmark for evaluating user-owned personal agents under evolving intent, platform mediation, privacy boundaries, consent constraints, evidence requirements, and burden tradeoffs. The benchmark separates agent-visible ObservableState from evaluator-only HiddenLabels, reports component metrics for task success, alignment, privacy, consent, evidence, manipulation, burden, and auditability, and preserves paired scenario ordering for model and policy comparisons. We evaluate 120 sovereignty stress scenarios across 4 model families and 8 policy baselines, yielding 3,840 frozen-prompt trajectories with raw prompts, outputs, provider-form responses, parsed actions, recomputable metrics, hard-set analyses, qualitative cases, and a blinded 3-annotator audit over 240 items. Full-sovereign scaffolding improves sovereignty score over direct, memory-only, consent-only, evidence-only, ReAct/tool-use, safety-prompt, and judge-guard baselines while reducing privacy leakage, consent violation, over-concession, and manipulation capture. Human audit shows high agreement on privacy and consent and lower agreement on manipulation, identifying the subjective frontier of platform-persuasion judgments. These results show that personal-agent evaluation must move beyond task completion toward representative, consent-aware, evidence-grounded action.

</details>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [387] [Characterizing and Bridging the Diagnostic Gap in eBPF Verifier Rejections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02748)
*Yusheng Zheng,Zhengjie Ji,Weichen Tao,Xiangyu Gao,Jianchang Su,Wei Zhang,Andi Quinn,Dan Williams*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出bpfix工具，通过重构验证器日志定位eBPF程序证明丢失位置，生成诊断信息，显著提升LLM修复eBPF验证错误的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eBPF验证器拒绝程序时的错误信息不清晰，仅报告验证停止位置而非证明丢失位置。实证研究发现47%拒绝仅返回EINVAL错误，同一错误字符串可能对应9种不同根因，且10/12种根因是eBPF特有的，导致修复困难。

Method: 1) 对235个复现的拒绝案例进行实证研究，量化错误信息不清晰问题；2) 开发bpfix工具，从验证器日志重构证明建立和丢失的位置，生成类Rust诊断信息；3) 构建75个LLM修复任务的基准测试评估效果。

Result: 当前LLM模型使用原始日志的one-shot成功率为0-37%，用bpfix定位信息替换日志后，修复成功率提升11-21个百分点，证明定位证明丢失位置对指导修复至关重要。

Conclusion: 定位证明丢失位置是指导eBPF程序修复的关键。bpfix通过重构验证器日志提供精确定位，显著提升LLM修复能力，为eBPF开发者提供有效调试支持。

Abstract: eBPF lets developers run custom programs inside the Linux kernel, where a verifier proves each program safe. However, when the verifier rejects a program, the unclear error makes repair challenging: the error reports where verification stopped, not where the program lost the proof the verifier required. To quantify this gap, we conduct an empirical study of 235 reproduced rejections, showing that 47% of rejections return only EINVAL, one error string maps to as many as nine distinct root causes, and 10 of the 12 root causes are eBPF-specific. Repair thus requires both domain knowledge and locating where the proof was lost, yet existing tools only help developers read the error. We present bpfix, which reconstructs where the required proof was established and where it was lost from the verifier log, and prints a Rust-like diagnostic. To evaluate bpfix and the ability of LLMs to help repair, we construct a benchmark of 75 LLM repair tasks. Current models achieve 0-37% one-shot success with the raw log, and replacing the log with the bpfix localization improves repair by 11-21pp, suggesting that locating where the proof was lost is key to guiding repair. bpfix is available at https://github.com/eunomia-bpf/bpfix

</details>


### [388] [Elastic Gang: Per-Token Membership Change for a Hard-Barriered LLM Inference Gang Co-Scheduled with OS Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04668)
*Daeyeon Son*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出了Anima OS中的弹性gang调度机制，解决了设备端LLM解码与OS其他进程竞争CPU核心的问题。通过ACK锁存的epoch协议，实现了推理gang在token之间动态改变核心成员资格，在保证推理输出位精确的同时，相比静态核心分区在中等推理负载下可将通用吞吐量提升1.28-1.75倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端LLM解码是具有屏障的CPU-SIMD计算，需要所有核心参与每个token的生成，但OS的其他进程也需要这些核心。传统的抢占式调度器无法处理这种带屏障的gang调度：未通知的离开会导致屏障死锁，未通知的到达会静默破坏logits。需要一种机制在保证正确性的前提下实现核心资源的弹性共享。

Method: 提出ACK锁存的epoch协议作为核心机制，该协议从不等待特定核心。采用seqlock风格的带生成标签的锁存器与RCU/epoch风格的成员资格同意机制组合，使得每个token的参与者集合是gang请求的核心与确认当前epoch的核心的交集。未确认的核心被排除在当前token之外，最多在一个token后加入。被置换的通用进程迁移并继续运行，核心在生成结束后立即归还。

Result: 在AMD Zen 5机器（8C/16T）上，135M和7B模型在验证的每token成员资格变化下推理输出位精确。相比公平静态核心分区，弹性成员资格帕累托占优：在25%、50%、75%推理负载下，通用吞吐量分别是静态8核分割的1.75倍、1.52倍、1.28倍，推理吞吐量相等或更优；推理空闲时恢复所有8个核心；饱和时收敛到分割状态。归还借出核心成本0.22微秒（p50），获取繁忙租户核心成本约16毫秒。解码吞吐量在gang宽度8时饱和。

Conclusion: 弹性gang调度成功解决了设备端LLM推理与系统其他进程的资源竞争问题，在保证正确性（位精确输出）的前提下实现了资源的高效利用。该机制自动在线调整gang规模，为设备端LLM部署提供了安全、高效的内核级解决方案。

Abstract: On-device LLM decoding is a hard-barriered CPU-SIMD computation that wants every core for milliseconds per token, while the rest of the OS wants those same cores continuously. A barriered gang cannot simply be dropped into a preemptive scheduler: an unannounced departure deadlocks a barrier, and an unannounced arrival silently corrupts logits. I present the elastic gang of Anima OS, a bare-metal x86-64 Rust kernel in which the inference gang is a first-class schedulable entity whose core membership may change between any two tokens. The core mechanism is an ACK-latched epoch protocol that never waits on a named core: a seqlock-style generation-tagged latch composed with RCU/epoch-style membership consent, so each token's participant set is the intersection of the cores the gang requested and the cores that acked the current epoch. An un-acked core is outside this token and joins at most one token later. Displaced general processes migrate and keep running; cores return to them the moment a generation ends.
  On a real AMD Zen 5 machine (8C/16T), inference output is bit-exact under verified per-token membership change on both a 135M and a 7B model, the property that makes elasticity safe in a kernel whose safety gate reads logits. Against fair static core partitions, elastic membership Pareto-dominates: at intermediate inference duty cycles it delivers 1.75x (25%), 1.52x (50%), and 1.28x (75%) the general throughput of a static 8-core split at equal or better inference throughput, recovers all eight stranded cores when inference is idle, and converges to the split at saturation. Returning a lent core costs 0.22 us (p50); acquiring a busy, tenant-occupied core costs one scheduling quantum (~16 ms): a running tenant is never preempted mid-slice. Decode throughput saturates at gang width 8, so ceding cores past the knee is nearly free: elasticity auto-sizes the gang online.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [389] [A Binary and System Integrated Analysis Approach for Securing the QUIC Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03149)
*Maitha Alshaali,Wanqing Tu,Gaofei Huang,Mthandazo Ndhlovu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BSISA方法，结合二进制级分析和系统级网络流量分析来评估QUIC协议实现的安全性，在4个生产环境QUIC服务器和6种攻击场景下，组合分类器达到45.8%准确率，优于单独使用二进制(37.5%)或系统级(25.0%)分析，并发现了仅通过网络捕获无法识别的"声明但静默"防御问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QUIC协议安全性分析主要关注捕获的网络流量，而二进制级别的QUIC实现分析仍未被探索。这导致一个关键问题悬而未决：QUIC标准规定的防御机制是否真正存在于编译后的二进制文件中，并在服务器遭受攻击时处于激活状态。随着QUIC协议在物联网设备和应用中的广泛使用，这一安全评估空白亟需填补。

Method: 本文评估了二进制与系统集成安全分析（BSISA）方法，该方法将QUIC服务器的编译后二进制级别分析与捕获网络流量的系统级分析相结合。研究在六种攻击场景下对四个生产环境QUIC服务器实现进行了评估，涵盖24个测试单元。通过组合分类器配置，同时分析二进制代码中的防御函数和网络流量中的攻击行为。

Result: 组合分类器配置是唯一在每种攻击场景下都能正确分类至少一个测试单元的配置，整体准确率达45.8%，而二进制级配置为37.5%，系统级配置仅25.0%。BSISA成功识别了编译二进制中吸收每次攻击的具体防御函数，并发现了"声明但静默"防御问题（在四个堆栈中有三个存在Retry-token验证，quiche中存在反放大机制，但在攻击期间从未执行），这类发现是仅通过网络捕获无法产生的。效率方面，picoquic在slowloris和连接ID耗尽攻击下合法客户端可用性失败率分别为72.4%和73.3%，而其他三个实现将失败率保持在0.5%或以下。

Conclusion: BSISA方法证明了结合二进制分析和网络流量分析的价值，能够识别传统网络流量分析无法发现的防御机制缺失问题。研究发现的"声明但静默"防御问题揭示了QUIC实现中潜在的安全隐患。这些发现为物联网固件部署中的QUIC安全评估提供了重要见解，有助于提升QUIC协议在物联网环境中的安全性。

Abstract: The Quick UDP Internet Connections (QUIC) protocol is increasingly used to provide secure transport for Internet of Things (IoT) firmware and applications. Existing security analyses of QUIC focus on the captured network traffic, while binary-level analyses of QUIC implementations remain unexplored, leaving open the question of whether a defence specified by the QUIC standard is both present in the compiled binary and active when the server is under attack. This paper evaluates the Binary and System Integrated Security Analysis (BSISA) approach, in which a binary-level analysis of the compiled QUIC server is combined with a system-level analysis of the captured network traffic, on four production QUIC server implementations under six attack scenarios. Across 24 cells, the combined classifier configuration is the only configuration that correctly classifies at least one cell on every attack scenario, achieving 45.8% overall accuracy compared with 37.5% for the binary-level configuration and 25.0% for the system-level configuration. BSISA also identifies the specific defence function in the compiled binary that absorbed each attack, and flags declared-but-silent defences, routines that are present in the compiled binary (Retry-token validation in three of four stacks, anti-amplification in quiche) but never execute during attack, a class of finding that network capture alone cannot produce. In terms of efficiency, picoquic loses legitimate-client availability under slowloris and connection- ID exhaustion with failure rates of 72.4% and 73.3% respectively, while the other three implementations hold the failure rate at or below 0.5%. We hope these insights will be informative for QUIC security evaluations in IoT firmware deployments.

</details>


### [390] [Agentic-SecPBFT: Agentic AI-Driven Proactive Security Framework for Wireless PBFT Consensus in Mobile Ad-Hoc Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03269)
*Haoxiang Luo,Yinqiu Liu,Ruichen Zhang,Guangyuan Liu,Gang Sun,Hongfang Yu,Zhu Han,Dong In Kim*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agentic-SecPBFT框架，通过引入智能体AI范式和层次化多智能体深度Q网络(MADQN)算法，为移动自组织网络中的PBFT协议提供自适应安全防护，在33%恶意节点环境下实现了95.0%的攻击检测率和显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PBFT协议在移动自组织网络等动态环境中存在严重安全漏洞，容易遭受女巫攻击、拜占庭合谋和消息篡改等复杂威胁，而现有的静态防御机制无法有效应对这些智能且协调的攻击行为。

Method: 基于智能体AI范式构建分布式多智能体系统，为每个共识节点配备智能代理。采用层次化多智能体深度Q网络(MADQN)算法，通过观察本地网络行为、消息一致性和动态声誉评分，协作识别可疑行为并推荐防御动作，所有操作均在标准PBFT仲裁和成员规则下进行。

Result: Agentic-SecPBFT实现了95.0%的攻击检测率和1.8%的误报率。在33%恶意节点环境下，相比主流PBFT变体，平均吞吐量提升3.1倍，延迟降低56%。

Conclusion: Agentic-SecPBFT为去中心化无线系统提供了一个鲁棒且自适应的安全解决方案，有效弥补了传统PBFT在动态网络环境中的安全缺陷，证明了智能体AI在区块链共识安全领域的应用价值。

Abstract: The standard Practical Byzantine Fault Tolerance (PBFT) protocol, designed for stable, wired environments, exhibits critical vulnerabilities when deployed in settings like mobile ad-hoc networks, thus making it susceptible to sophisticated threats such as Sybil attacks, Byzantine collusion, and message manipulation. Existing static defense mechanisms are ill-equipped to handle the intelligent and coordinated nature of these attacks. To address this challenge, this paper leverages the Agentic AI paradigm to build a distributed multi-agent system in which each consensus node is equipped with an intelligent agent. These agents employ a hierarchical Multi-Agent Deep Q-Network (MADQN) algorithm to learn and execute proactive security policies in real-time. By observing local network behavior, message consistency, and dynamically maintained reputation scores, the agents collaboratively identify suspicious behavior and recommend defensive actions under standard PBFT quorum and membership rules, thereby improving the integrity of the consensus process. We refer to the resulting framework as Agentic-SecPBFT. Then, we formally model key attack vectors and conduct extensive simulations. The results demonstrate that Agentic-SecPBFT reaches a 95.0% attack detection rate with a 1.8% false positive rate. Compared with mainstream PBFT variants, it achieves 3.1* higher throughput with 56% lower latency on average under 33% malicious nodes, offering a robust and adaptive security solution for decentralized wireless systems.

</details>


### [391] [Beam Hopping Low Earth Orbit Satellite Resource Allocation for Differentiated Services and Robustness Analysis under Model Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03859)
*Shuang Zheng,Xing Zhang,Quan Z. Sheng,Haixu Wang,Wenbo Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出BRIDGE框架，利用数字孪生技术和强化学习解决波束跳跃LEO卫星的动态资源分配问题，在保证服务质量的同时提升了系统的能效、吞吐量和公平性，并证明了在对抗攻击下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波束跳跃(BH)使能的LEO卫星在下一代通信网络中具有重要作用，但星上资源受限和潜在对抗扰动对AI驱动的动态资源分配和管理带来了效率和鲁棒性挑战。现有方法难以准确捕获用户-卫星可见性的时空动态特性，也无法有效应对对抗性攻击。

Method: 提出BRIDGE框架：1)利用数字孪生技术精确捕获用户-卫星可见性的时空动态特性，为决策提供精确状态信息；2)将BH调度和功率分配的联合优化建模为马尔可夫决策过程；3)集成基于QoS驱动的子信道调度机制实现高效差异化资源分配；4)创新性地结合狄利克雷分布和Gumbel-TopK探索策略。在三种经典对抗攻击下系统性评估模型鲁棒性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法在能效、服务吞吐量和公平性方面均达到优越性能；鲁棒性分析显示，在所考虑的有界对抗扰动下，模型性能保持稳定，验证了其在实际部署中的可靠性。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了BH使能LEO卫星网络中资源分配的效率和鲁棒性问题，证明了数字孪生技术与强化学习相结合的有效性。BRIDGE框架不仅提升了系统性能，还增强了对抗攻击的防御能力，为下一代卫星通信网络的智能化资源管理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Beam hopping (BH)-enabled Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellites play a pivotal role in next-generation communication networks by providing global coverage, improving spectrum efficiency, and supporting flexible adaptation to heterogeneous service demands. To fully exploit these capabilities, artificial intelligence (AI) techniques are increasingly employed for dynamic resource allocation and power management. However, limited onboard resources and potential adversarial perturbations pose challenges to both efficiency and robustness. To address these issues, we leverage digital twin technology to accurately capture the spatio-temporal dynamics of user-satellite visibility, thereby providing precise state information for decision-making. Building on this, we formulate a joint optimization framework for BH scheduling and power allocation as a Markov Decision Process and propose BRIDGE, i.e., BH with Reinforcement learning incorporating Integrated Dirichlet and Gumbel-TopK Exploration, which integrates a quality of service (QoS)-driven subchannel scheduling mechanism to ensure efficient and differentiated resource allocation. The robustness of the model is systematically evaluated under three classical adversarial attacks. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves superior energy efficiency, service throughput, and fairness, while the robustness analysis shows stable performance under the considered bounded adversarial perturbations.

</details>


### [392] [Evaluating 5G-connected IoT for Power Line Temperature Prediction: Real-World Latency and Cost Trade-offs Between MEC and Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03993)
*Aakash Sharma,Sigmund Akselsen,Anders Andersen,Lars Ailo Bongo,Arne Munch-Ellingsen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究在真实5G网络环境下实验验证了移动边缘计算(MEC)相比云端的延迟优势，实现44.62ms延迟，但仍无法满足智能电网8ms的严苛要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模物联网部署目前依赖云端服务，但需要更低延迟。5G网络提供更高带宽和更低延迟，将MEC与5G结合有望同时获得云计算的便利性和边缘计算的低延迟特性，这对需要实时响应的户外物联网应用至关重要。

Method: 在5G蜂窝网络上构建实验性MEC设置，开发概念验证系统用于实时电力传输线路分析，将边缘计算服务器方案与最先进的多区域云端部署进行对比评估。

Result: 真实环境评估显示MEC实现44.62ms的低延迟，显著优于云端区域部署，但两者差距正在缩小。虽然该延迟水平对实际部署有益，但仍无法满足智能电网操作的严苛要求(约8ms)。

Conclusion: 5G-MEC组合相比云端具有延迟优势，能够支持实时数据分析应用，但对于超低延迟要求的关键基础设施应用(如智能电网)仍存在挑战，需要进一步优化以满足更严格的延迟需求。

Abstract: One of the key promises of Mobile Edge Computing (MEC) is its low latency. Current large-scale IoT deployments rely on cloud for their reliability, low cost, and ease of use. For outdoor IoT deployments, 5G cellular networks offer significantly enhanced bandwidth and dramatically reduced latency compared to previous generations, enabling real-time data processing and control. Therefore, leveraging 5G connectivity is crucial for outdoor IoT applications requiring responsiveness and complex data handling. Combining MEC with 5G has the potential to provide the ease of cloud computing alongside low latency. We investigate the latency performance on a 5G cellular network with an experimental MEC setup. In our proof-of-concept, we demonstrate the benefits of using an edge-based compute server for real-time power transmission line analytics. We compare our solution with state-of-the-art multi-region cloud deployments and discuss the advantages of mobile edge computing (MEC). Our real-world evaluation demonstrates a low latency of 44.62 ms for MEC compared to cloud regions; however, the gap is narrowing. While such low latencies can benefit real-world deployments, they remain insufficient to meet the stringent requirements of smart power grid operations (~8 ms).

</details>


### [393] [Agentic-V2X: Small Language Model Agents for Deadline-Aware V2X Scheduling in 5G/6G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04290)
*Gerasimos Papanikolaou-Ntais,Alexandros Kaloxylos,Athanasios Kanavos*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic-V2X架构，使用小型本地部署的语言模型作为周期性非实时策略生成器，配合轻量级控制器执行验证后的策略，用于5G NR V2X调度。实验表明该方法能生成有效可执行策略，在某些场景下优于比例公平调度，但整体性能仍低于最优静态策略，证明其作为安全的小型LLM策略生成架构的可行性，而非通用最优调度器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为下一代网络控制接口被提出，但其延迟、幻觉问题和缺乏控制保证使其不适合近实时分组调度器，特别是在动态V2X环境中。需要一种架构能够在利用LLM能力的同时解决其实时性和可靠性问题。

Method: 提出Agentic-V2X架构，包含两个核心组件：(1)小型本地部署的语言模型作为周期性非实时rApp启发式策略创建器，根据场景摘要、服务目标和遥测数据生成包含服务优先级、权重边界和安全约束的结构化策略；(2)轻量级xApp类控制器以适合调度的时间间隔执行验证后的策略。验证器检查并修复策略后，控制器通过ns-3/ns3-ai中的调度器权重自适应来实施策略，针对具有异构服务（远程驾驶、协同感知、高精地图共享和传感器共享）的截止时间感知5G NR V2X调度。

Result: 在126次完整运行的评估中，对比了比例公平调度、静态专家策略、启发式xApp、静态LLM策略和自适应LLM-rApp策略。评估指标包括截止时间约束的分组接收率、尾延迟、截止时间违规、吞吐量、公平性、策略有效性和安全干预。结果显示自适应LLM-rApp/xApp设计能够生成有效可执行的策略，在多个操作点保持竞争力，包括在最高密度下优于PF的平均关键可靠性。但配对统计分析表明自适应方法并非最佳聚合方法，整体仍低于最强静态策略。

Conclusion: Agentic-V2X是一个安全、可执行的小型LLM策略生成架构，能够有效生成V2X调度策略并在某些场景下展现竞争力，但并非通用最优调度器。该架构为LLM在网络控制领域的应用提供了一种可行的安全框架，通过策略验证和控制器分离解决了LLM的实时性和可靠性问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are proposed as control interfaces for next-generation networks, but their latency, hallucinations, and lack of control guarantees make them unsuitable for near-real-time packet schedulers, especially in dynamic V2X environments. This paper introduces Agentic-V2X, an architecture where a small, locally deployed language model acts as a periodic non-real-time rApp-inspired policy creator, while a lightweight xApp-like controller executes validated policies at intervals suitable for scheduling. The framework targets deadline-aware 5G NR V2X scheduling with heterogeneous services (teleoperated driving, cooperative awareness, HD map sharing, and sensor sharing). Given a scenario summary, service objective, and telemetry, the LLM generates a structured policy containing service priorities, weight bounds, and safety constraints. A validator checks and repairs the policy before the controller enforces it via scheduler-weight adaptation in ns-3/ns3-ai. The evaluation compares proportional fair scheduling, static expert policies, a heuristic xApp, static LLM policies, and adaptive LLM-rApp policies over 126 completed runs. Metrics include deadline-constrained packet reception ratio, tail latency, deadline violations, throughput, fairness, policy validity, and safety interventions. Results show that the adaptive LLM-rApp/xApp design generates valid and executable policies and remains competitive at several operating points, including improved mean critical reliability over PF at the highest density. However, paired statistical analysis shows that the adaptive method is not the best aggregate method and remains below the strongest static policies overall. These results support Agentic-V2X as a safe, executable small-LLM policy-generation architecture rather than a universally dominant scheduler.

</details>


### [394] [On the Physical Plausibility and Distribution Alignment for Sim-to-Real RF Positioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04400)
*Ararat Saribekyan,Armen Manukyan,Hrant Khachatrian,Theofanis P. Raptis*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 在RF定位的仿真到真实迁移中，RSSI分布对齐比物理真实性和数据集规模更重要；城市规模的无约束数据预训练在已知街道表现最佳，但在未知街道上需结合分布归一化才能获得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实路测数据成本高昂且覆盖有限，难以支持在训练时未见过的街道上的可靠RF定位。此前研究表明射线追踪仿真可为深度定位模型提供预训练数据，但仿真侧的关键因素（基站校准、物理真实性、数据规模、RSSI分布对齐）如何影响迁移效果尚不清楚。

Method: 使用Sionna重建罗马部署场景，对每个基站进行位置、高度、方位角和发射功率的校准。对比物理合理的校准与允许非真实基站放置的无约束校准；对比部署特定数据与更大规模的城市级数据集；研究RSSI分布归一化的影响。

Result: 无约束校准能更好匹配测量RSSI但不总能提升定位精度；所有合成预训练方法均提升已知街道性能，城市规模无约束数据效果最佳；仅增大数据规模无法改善未知街道性能；对仿真RSSI进行归一化以匹配真实分布后，在保留街道上取得最佳结果。

Conclusion: 对于仿真到真实的RF定位任务，分布对齐的重要性超过物理真实性和数据集规模。这一发现为合成数据预训练策略提供了指导，强调了在迁移学习中优先考虑数据分布匹配的必要性。

Abstract: Reliable radio frequency (RF) positioning from cellular measurements is limited by the high cost and limited coverage of real drive-test data, especially when models must work on streets not seen during training. Previous work showed that ray tracing simulations can provide useful synthetic data for pretraining deep positioning models. In this paper, we focus on the simulation side and study how base-station calibration, physical realism, synthetic-data scale, and RSSI distribution alignment affect transfer to real data. Using a Sionna reconstruction of a Rome deployment, we calibrate each base station by adjusting its location, height, azimuth, and transmit power. We compare physically plausible calibrations with unconstrained ones that allow unrealistic base-station placements. We also compare deployment-specific synthetic data with much larger city-scale datasets. Although unconstrained calibration matches measured RSSI better, it does not always improve positioning accuracy. All synthetic pretraining approaches improve performance on known streets, with the best result obtained using city-scale unconstrained data. However, larger synthetic datasets alone do not improve performance on unseen streets. The best results on held-out streets are achieved only after normalizing simulated RSSI values to better match the real distribution. Overall, the results suggest that distribution alignment is more important than physical realism or dataset size for sim-to-real RF positioning.

</details>


### [395] [Wireless Gas Leak Detection and Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04524)
*Fabien Chraim,Yusuf Bugra Erol,Kris Pister*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无线气体泄漏检测与定位解决方案，在200m²区域内部署20个无线设备进行实验，实现了91%的检测率、5米的定位精度，平均检测延迟为108秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每年发生数千起工业气体泄漏事故，导致人员伤亡、设备损坏和严重的环境影响。尽管已有多种解决方案尝试解决此问题，但效果有限，因此迫切需要更有效的气体泄漏检测与定位方法。

Method: 提出一种无线气体泄漏检测与定位系统。在200m²的监测区域内部署由20个无线设备组成的监测网络，进行60次丙烷释放实验。针对采集的浓度数据应用所提出的检测和定位算法，并对方法进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，该系统实现了91%的检测率，在三天内仅记录了7次误报警，平均检测延迟为108秒。定位精度达到5米。

Conclusion: 本文验证了所提无线气体泄漏检测与定位解决方案的有效性，并针对未来爆炸性气体传感器的设计提出了建议，以进一步提升检测性能。

Abstract: Thousands of industrial gas leaks occur every year, with many leading to injuries, deaths, equipment damage, and a disastrous environmental effect. There have been many attempts at solving this problem, but with limited success. This paper proposes a wireless gas leak detection and localization solution. With a monitoring network of 20 wireless devices covering 200m^2, 60 propane releases are performed. The detection and localization algorithms proposed here are applied to the collected concentration data, and the methodology is evaluated. A detection rate of 91% is achieved, with seven false alarms recorded over three days, and an average detection delay of 108 seconds. The localization results show an accuracy of 5 meters. Recommendations for future explosive gas sensor design are then presented.

</details>


### [396] [Version-Aware Communication in Multi-Hop IoT Networks with Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04996)
*Erfan Delfani,Nikolaos Pappas*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多跳物联网中基于确认反馈的版本信息年龄(VAoI)，通过双层优化框架联合优化源节点更新和中继节点转发策略，推导出了最优阈值策略和闭式表达式，证明了VAoI和反馈机制能有效减少冗余传输并保持数据新鲜度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息时效性研究主要关注单跳网络中VAoI的一阶矩分析，缺乏对多跳网络中VAoI分布特性及反馈机制影响的研究。传统AoI指标仅量化信息新鲜度，未考虑内容演化，而VAoI能跟踪接收端的版本滞后，提供了实用的内容感知指标。

Method: 建立了考虑传输约束和确认反馈的多跳网络VAoI分析模型；提出双层优化框架，联合优化速率受限源节点的更新策略和中间节点感知反馈的转发策略；推导最优阈值更新策略及其闭式解；针对最优阈值策略和随机基线策略，推导稳态分布、平均VAoI及更新速率的闭式表达式。

Result: 证明了最优源节点策略遵循基于阈值的更新策略，并给出了最优阈值的闭式表达式；获得了稳态分布和平均VAoI的闭式表达式；推导了反馈感知转发下网络节点更新速率的闭式表达式；数值实验验证了理论分析的正确性。

Conclusion: 利用VAoI指标和反馈机制可以在保持数据新鲜度和信息性的同时有效减少冗余传输，为多跳物联网系统的信息时效性优化提供了新的理论框架和实用策略。

Abstract: Timely communication of information in Internet of Things (IoT) networks is critical to enhancing system performance and energy efficiency by minimizing the transmission of outdated or redundant data. Although timeliness metrics such as the Age of Information (AoI) effectively quantify information freshness, they do not account for content evolution. The Version Age of Information (VAoI) addresses this gap by tracking version lag at the receiver, thereby providing a practical content-aware metric. However, prior research has primarily focused on first-moment analyses in single-hop settings, leaving the distributional properties of VAoI in multi-hop networks, as well as the impact of feedback mechanisms, unexplored. In this study, we provide a comprehensive characterization of VAoI in multi-hop networks with transmission constraints and acknowledgment-based feedback. A bi-level optimization framework is formulated to jointly optimize the update policy of a rate-constrained source and the feedback-aware forwarding policies of the intermediate nodes, aiming to minimize communication overhead while maintaining VAoI performance at the destination. We show that the optimal source policy follows a threshold-based update strategy and derive the optimal threshold in closed form. For both the optimal threshold policy and a randomized baseline, we obtain closed-form expressions for the stationary distribution and average VAoI, along with the corresponding update rates across network nodes under feedback-aware forwarding. Numerical results corroborate the analytical findings and illustrate the advantages of utilizing VAoI and feedback to reduce redundant transmissions while preserving data freshness and informativeness in multi-hop systems.

</details>


### [397] [RANPilot: Making AI Functionalities Robust to Dynamic O-RAN Reconfigurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05038)
*Shimin Yu,Leming Shen,Jianing Zhang,Xin Li,Xianjin Xia,Yuanqing Zheng,Yaxiong Xie*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了RANPilot框架，通过构建轻量级虚拟O-RAN主动适应AI模型，解决了网络重配置导致的数据漂移问题，将AI服务中断时间减少了85%-94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN架构的灵活性和AI驱动控制能力带来了前所未有的优势，但也暴露了关键脆弱性：AI模型在一种网络配置下训练后，在升级后由于显著的数据漂移导致性能严重下降。现有的反应式重训练解决方案速度过慢，会导致网络在数十分钟内处于次优状态，破坏了O-RAN动态性的核心优势。

Method: 提出RANPilot框架，通过构建轻量级"虚拟O-RAN"（一个基于轨迹驱动的仿真器），在物理变更发生前合成高保真的训练数据来表示重配置后的状态，使AI模型能够提前进行适应。这种主动式方法将AI演进范式从被动重开发转变为主动准备。

Result: 在真实5G测试平台上进行了大量实验，结果表明RANPilot在重配置时实现了近乎无中断的AI服务，与反应式基线方法相比，AI停机时间减少了85%到94%。

Conclusion: 通过数字引导方法实现了可重配置O-RAN部署中的鲁棒AI能力，RANPilot成功探索了从被动响应到主动准备的范式转变，为未来动态网络环境中的AI模型部署提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: The Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) promises unprecedented flexibility through its reconfigurable architecture and AI-driven control. However, this agility exposes a critical fragility: AI models trained on one network configuration suffer significant performance degradation after an upgrade due to dramatic data drift. The standard solution, reactive retraining, is unacceptably slow, leaving the network in a suboptimal state for tens of minutes and undermining the core benefits of O-RAN's dynamism. This paper introduces RANPilot, the first framework to address this challenge through proactive AI adaptation. RANPilot constructs a lightweight "virtual O-RAN" (a trace-driven emulator) to synthesize high-fidelity training data representing the post-reconfiguration state before the physical change occurs, allowing AI models to be adapted in advance. Extensive experiments on a real-world 5G testbed demonstrate that RANPilot achieves near interruption-free AI services upon reconfiguration, reducing AI downtime by 85% to 94% against reactive baselines. By shifting the AI evolution paradigm from reactive redevelopment to proactive preparation, RANPilot explores a digital-leadoff approach to enable robust AI in reconfigurable O-RAN deployments.

</details>


### [398] [A Modular O-RAN Testbed Based on SRS Open Source O-CU/O-DU and Massive Beams Modular O-RU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05146)
*Fabian Göttsch,Oriol Font-Bach,Andreas Benzin,Dennis Osterland,Andre Puschmann,Felix-Christopher Lutz,Wilhelm Keusgen,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化开放无线接入网(O-RAN)系统，由SRS的OCUDU（集成O-CU和O-DU）和Massive Beams的MODRAD-SC O-RU组成，支持split 7.2a和split 8两种配置，可实现5G gNodeB及多种应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了满足学术界和工业界对灵活、模块化、易于使用的实验平台需求，支持从5G演示到6G前实验等多种应用场景。

Method: 基于O-RAN split 7.2a架构，OCUDU负责高层及高物理层功能，O-RU负责低物理层和射频功能，构成符合标准的5G gNodeB；同时支持split 8配置实现软件无线电方式的非实时实验；还可连接模拟波束成形器实现混合数模波束成形。

Result: 成功实现了符合O-RAN标准的模块化系统，提供对O-CU、O-DU和O-RU的完全控制，支持多种配置和功能扩展。

Conclusion: 该系统具有良好的灵活性和模块化特性，适合作为实验和实际部署的易用平台，能够满足多样化的应用需求。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a modular open radio access network (O-RAN) consisting of the 5G Core, a central (O-CU) and distributed unit (O-DU) by Software Radio Systems (SRS) and an O-RAN radio unit (O-RU), MODRAD-SC, by Massive Beams (MB). OCUDU provides an open source 5G-compliant O-CU and O-DU solution developed by SRS, while MB's radio unit is a fully O-RAN compliant category A O-RU. According to O-RAN split 7.2a, OCUDU performs higher layer functions up to the high physical (PHY) layer, while the O-RU handles low PHY and RF functions. This results in an O-RAN-compliant 5G gNodeB. In an alternative configuration, OCUDU and MODRAD-SC operate in a software-defined radio fashion corresponding to split 8, facilitating non-real-time experiments among others. In both cases, the system provides full control over O-CU, O-DU, and O-RU. In addition, we will discuss the possibility to attach an analog beamformer to the O-RU, enabling hybrid digital-analog beamforming. The flexibility and modularity offered by OCUDU and MODRAD-SC enable the practical realization of a multitude of applications, ranging from 5G demonstrators to pre-6G experiments. The system addresses the requirements of academia and industry and is well-suited as an easy-to-use platform for experimental and practical deployments.

</details>
